f_clamp(_val, _min, _max) => math.max(_min, math.min(_max, _val))
// 27.08 ALTYAPI YAMASI (3. madde, GERCEKLIK_ALARM_3 kaynagindan alindi):
// BIST resmi fiyat adimi tablosuna dayali. bist_tick_limit_referansi.py
// ile AYNI mantik, Pine'a cevrildi. Karar mantigina DOKUNMUYOR (dogrulandi,
// kurul analizi 2026-08-27).
f_bistTick(_price) =>
    _price < 20.0 ? 0.01 : _price < 50.0 ? 0.02 : _price < 100.0 ? 0.05 :
     _price < 250.0 ? 0.10 : _price < 500.0 ? 0.25 : _price < 1000.0 ? 0.50 :
     _price < 2500.0 ? 1.00 : 2.50
f_roundDownToTick(_price, _tick) => _tick > 0 ? math.floor(_price / _tick) * _tick : _price
f_roundUpToTick(_price, _tick) => _tick > 0 ? math.ceil(_price / _tick) * _tick : _price
// 17.09 V162.2: BIST tick yuvarlama - f_bistTick/f_roundDownToTick ZATEN
// VARDI (yalniz limit-fiyat hesaplarinda kullaniliyordu), stop/TP
// ciktilarina hic UYGULANMIYORDU (orn. webhook'a 254,37 gibi BIST'te
// gercekte islem gormeyen bir fiyat gidebiliyordu - 250 uzeri tick 0,25).
// Stop icin ASAGI (daha fazla marj, erken tetiklenmeyi onler), TP icin de
// ASAGI (ulasilabilir/muhafazakar) yuvarlanir - ayni f_bistTick tablosu.
f_bistRound(_price) => f_roundDownToTick(_price, f_bistTick(_price))
// 17.09 TICK YUVARLAMA YONU DUZELTMESI (dış kurul incelemesi, bulgu #12 -
// dogrulandi): f_bistRound (ASAGI) TP icin dogru (muhafazakar/ulasilabilir)
// ama STOP icin YANLIS - long pozisyonda stopu ASAGI yuvarlamak stopu
// UZAKLASTIRIR (fiyattan daha uzaga), bu da hesaplanandan DAHA FAZLA risk
// almak demektir. Stop icin YUKARI yuvarlanmali (daha siki, hesaplanan
// riskten FAZLASINA izin vermez).
f_bistRoundStop(_price) => f_roundUpToTick(_price, f_bistTick(_price))
// PATCH 3 (Loop Engineering P1 - curve-fit seffafligi): bu iki esik daha once
// f_v107MarketQuality icinde SABIT (1.8 / 1.035) olarak gomuluydu - hangi veriyle
// optimize edildigi belirsizdi, kullanici test/tune edemiyordu. Varsayilan degerler
// AYNI KALDI (davranis DEGISMEDI), sadece artik gorunur ve ayarlanabilir.
lateAtrMult = input.float(1.8, "Gec Giris: ATR Carpani (EMA uzerinde)", minval=0.5, maxval=5.0, step=0.1, group="Gec Giris / FOMO Filtresi")
lateEmaExtPct = input.float(1.035, "Gec Giris: EMA Uzerinde Max Yuzde (1.035=%3.5)", minval=1.0, maxval=1.20, step=0.005, group="Gec Giris / FOMO Filtresi")
// === GORSEL DUZELTME: Uzun sektor isimleri (orn. "BANKACILIK", "KIMYA/TARIM") DSS
// hucresinde syminfo.ticker ile yan yana yazilinca tablo geregi genisliyordu (Pine

f_secShort(_sec) =>
    _sec == "BANKACILIK" ? "BANK" : _sec == "HOLDING" ? "HOLD" : _sec == "PERAKENDE" ? "RTL" :
     _sec == "TEKNOLOJI" ? "TECH" : _sec == "SAVUNMA" ? "DEF" : _sec == "HAVACILIK" ? "AIR" :
     _sec == "ILETISIM" ? "TEL" : _sec == "SIGORTA" ? "SIG" : _sec == "SINAI" ? "IND" :
     _sec == "ENERJI" ? "ENR" : _sec == "INSAAT" ? "INS" : _sec == "GYO" ? "GYO" :
     _sec == "OTOMOTIV" ? "OTO" : _sec == "KIMYA/TARIM" ? "KIM" : "GEN"
f_autoSector(_ticker) =>
    string _result = "GENEL"
    if str.contains(_ticker,"GARAN") or str.contains(_ticker,"AKBNK") or str.contains(_ticker,"YKBNK") or str.contains(_ticker,"HALKB") or str.contains(_ticker,"VAKBN") or str.contains(_ticker,"ISATR") or str.contains(_ticker,"ISCTR") or str.contains(_ticker,"QNBFB") or str.contains(_ticker,"ALBRK") or str.contains(_ticker,"SKBNK") or str.contains(_ticker,"KLNMA")
        _result := "BANKACILIK"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"KCHOL") or str.contains(_ticker,"SAHOL") or str.contains(_ticker,"DOHOL") or str.contains(_ticker,"MPARK") or str.contains(_ticker,"KTLEV")
        _result := "HOLDING"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"BIMAS") or str.contains(_ticker,"MGROS") or str.contains(_ticker,"SOKM") or str.contains(_ticker,"CRFSA") or str.contains(_ticker,"BIZIM")
        _result := "PERAKENDE"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"LOGO") or str.contains(_ticker,"INDES") or str.contains(_ticker,"NETAS") or str.contains(_ticker,"ARENA") or str.contains(_ticker,"FONET") or str.contains(_ticker,"ESCOM")
        _result := "TEKNOLOJI"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"ASELS") or str.contains(_ticker,"ASELSAN") or str.contains(_ticker,"ROKET") or str.contains(_ticker,"HAVAS") or str.contains(_ticker,"OSES") or str.contains(_ticker,"FNVSY") or str.contains(_ticker,"SDTTR") or str.contains(_ticker,"PAPIL")
        _result := "SAVUNMA"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"THYAO") or str.contains(_ticker,"PGSUS") or str.contains(_ticker,"CLEBI") or str.contains(_ticker,"TAVHL")
        _result := "HAVACILIK"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"TCELL") or str.contains(_ticker,"TTKOM")
        _result := "ILETISIM"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"ANHYT") or str.contains(_ticker,"RAYSG") or str.contains(_ticker,"AGESA") or str.contains(_ticker,"AKGRT") or str.contains(_ticker,"ANSGR")
        _result := "SIGORTA"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"EREGL") or str.contains(_ticker,"KRDMD") or str.contains(_ticker,"CEMTS") or str.contains(_ticker,"SISE") or str.contains(_ticker,"TRALT") or str.contains(_ticker,"KOZAL") or str.contains(_ticker,"CIMSA") or str.contains(_ticker,"OYAKC")
        _result := "SINAI"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"TUPRS") or str.contains(_ticker,"AYGAZ") or str.contains(_ticker,"PETKM") or str.contains(_ticker,"AKSEN") or str.contains(_ticker,"ZOREN") or str.contains(_ticker,"ASTOR") or str.contains(_ticker,"ENJSA") or str.contains(_ticker,"GWIND") or str.contains(_ticker,"AKFYE") or str.contains(_ticker,"NATEN")
        _result := "ENERJI"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"ENKAI") or str.contains(_ticker,"TKFEN") or str.contains(_ticker,"EDIP")
        _result := "INSAAT"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"EKGYO") or str.contains(_ticker,"ISGYO") or str.contains(_ticker,"TRGYO") or str.contains(_ticker,"VKGYO") or str.contains(_ticker,"ALGYO")
        _result := "GYO"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"FROTO") or str.contains(_ticker,"TOASO") or str.contains(_ticker,"DOAS") or str.contains(_ticker,"OTKAR") or str.contains(_ticker,"YEOTK") or str.contains(_ticker,"TTRAK")
        _result := "OTOMOTIV"
    else if str.contains(_ticker,"HEKTS") or str.contains(_ticker,"GUBRF") or str.contains(_ticker,"SASA") or str.contains(_ticker,"BAGFS") or str.contains(_ticker,"ALKIM")
        _result := "KIMYA/TARIM"
    _result
f_sectorIndex(_sector) =>
    string _result = "BIST:XU100"
    if _sector == "BANKACILIK"
        _result := "BIST:XBANK"
    else if _sector == "HOLDING"
        _result := "BIST:XHOLD"
    else if _sector == "PERAKENDE"
        _result := "BIST:XGIDA"
    else if _sector == "TEKNOLOJI"
        _result := "BIST:XUTEK"
    else if _sector == "SAVUNMA"
        _result := "BIST:XUSIN"
    else if _sector == "HAVACILIK"
        _result := "BIST:XULAS"
    else if _sector == "ILETISIM"
        _result := "BIST:XILTM"
    else if _sector == "SIGORTA"
        _result := "BIST:XSGRT"
    else if _sector == "SINAI"
        _result := "BIST:XMANA"
    else if _sector == "ENERJI"
        _result := "BIST:XELKT"
    else if _sector == "INSAAT"
        _result := "BIST:XINSA"
    else if _sector == "GYO"
        _result := "BIST:XGMYO"
    else if _sector == "OTOMOTIV"
        _result := "BIST:XMESY"
    else if _sector == "KIMYA/TARIM"
        _result := "BIST:XKMYA"
    _result
_t = syminfo.ticker
autoSector = f_autoSector(_t)
autoSectorIndex = f_sectorIndex(autoSector)
_isBankGYO       = autoSector == "BANKACILIK" or autoSector == "GYO" or autoSector == "SIGORTA"
_isMomSec        = autoSector == "TEKNOLOJI" or autoSector == "HAVACILIK" or autoSector == "KIMYA/TARIM"
_isHeavySec      = autoSector == "SINAI" or autoSector == "ENERJI" or autoSector == "INSAAT" or autoSector == "HAVACILIK"
_isDefSec        = autoSector == "SAVUNMA" or autoSector == "PERAKENDE" or autoSector == "ILETISIM"
_isHolding       = autoSector == "HOLDING"
_isHoldingBank   = str.contains(_t,"SAHOL")
_isHoldingEnerji = str.contains(_t,"KCHOL")
_isEnerji        = autoSector == "ENERJI"
_isGoldStock     = str.contains(_t,"TRALT") or str.contains(_t,"KOZAL")
_isBrentSensitive= str.contains(_t,"TUPRS") or str.contains(_t,"PETKM") or str.contains(_t,"AYGAZ") or str.contains(_t,"TRCAS")
_isUtility       = str.contains(_t,"ENJSA") or str.contains(_t,"GWIND") or str.contains(_t,"AKFYE") or str.contains(_t,"NATEN")
_isEnerjiAlt     = _isEnerji and not _isBrentSensitive and not _isUtility
_isAkbnk         = str.contains(_t,"AKBNK")
_isTtkom         = str.contains(_t,"TTKOM") or str.contains(_t,"TCELL")
_isMomStock      = _isGoldStock or str.contains(_t,"ASTOR") or str.contains(_t,"ROKET") or str.contains(_t,"ASELS") or str.contains(_t,"ASELSAN")
_isBankImmune = autoSector == "ENERJI" or autoSector == "SAVUNMA" or autoSector == "SINAI" or autoSector == "OTOMOTIV" or autoSector == "KIMYA/TARIM" or autoSector == "HAVACILIK" or autoSector == "TEKNOLOJI" or autoSector == "PERAKENDE" or autoSector == "ILETISIM"
f_rsiUpper(_sector) =>
    int _result = 68
    if _sector == "SAVUNMA"
        _result := 82
    else if _sector == "TEKNOLOJI"
        _result := 80
    else if _sector == "HAVACILIK"
        _result := 78
    else if _sector == "KIMYA/TARIM"
        _result := 75
    else if _sector == "BANKACILIK"
        _result := 72
    else if _sector == "OTOMOTIV" or _sector == "SINAI" or _sector == "ENERJI" or _sector == "HOLDING"
        _result := 70
    else if _sector == "PERAKENDE" or _sector == "ILETISIM"
        _result := 65
    _result
f_tpMult(_sector, _ticker) =>
    float _result = 3.0
    if _sector == "SAVUNMA" or _sector == "TEKNOLOJI"
        _result := 4.0
    else if _sector == "HAVACILIK" or _sector == "KIMYA/TARIM"
        _result := 3.5
    else if _sector == "SINAI"
        _result := str.contains(_ticker, "TRALT") ? 3.0 : 2.5
    else if _sector == "ENERJI"
        _result := str.contains(_ticker, "ASTOR") ? 3.0 : 2.5
    else if _sector == "HOLDING" or _sector == "GYO"
        _result := 2.5
    else if _sector == "ILETISIM" or _sector == "PERAKENDE"
        _result := 2.0
    _result
f_scoreMult(_sector, _brentSensitive) =>
    float _result = 0.90
    if _sector == "SAVUNMA" or _sector == "TEKNOLOJI"
        _result := 1.00
    else if _sector == "HAVACILIK" or _sector == "KIMYA/TARIM" or _sector == "BANKACILIK"
        _result := 0.95
    else if _sector == "OTOMOTIV"
        _result := 0.92
    else if _sector == "ENERJI"
        _result := not _brentSensitive ? 0.97 : 0.90
    else if _sector == "INSAAT" or _sector == "GYO" or _sector == "PERAKENDE"
        _result := 0.88
    else if _sector == "ILETISIM"
        _result := 0.85
    _result
f_sectorFireThreshold(_sector) =>
    float _result = 0.0
    if _sector == "BANKACILIK" or _sector == "HAVACILIK"
        _result := 1.5
    else if _sector == "HOLDING" or _sector == "PERAKENDE"
        _result := 1.0
    else if _sector == "SINAI" or _sector == "SAVUNMA"
        _result := 1.2
    _result
f_sectorEarlyFire(_sector, _sectorRet, _indexRet, _sectorUp) =>
    _threshold = f_sectorFireThreshold(_sector)
    _threshold > 0 and _sectorRet >= _threshold and _sectorRet > _indexRet + 0.6 and _sectorUp
f_trendDominance(_close, _emaFast, _emaMid, _emaSlow, _adx, _weakening, _panic) =>
    _close > _emaFast and _emaFast > _emaMid and _emaMid > _emaSlow and
         _adx > 22 and not _weakening and not _panic
f_kgsV103Adjust(_sector, _fake, _panic, _cvdUp, _smcStrength, _health, _htfAlign, _rsSector, _cvdBull, _hiddenDist) =>
    _retail = _sector == "PERAKENDE" and not _fake and not _panic and (_cvdUp or _smcStrength >= 2) ? 4 : 0
    _aviation = _sector == "HAVACILIK" and _health == "STRONG" and _htfAlign < 1 and _rsSector > 0 ? 4 : 0
    _quality = (_health == "STRONG" or _health == "NORMAL") and _smcStrength >= 1 and (_cvdUp or _cvdBull) and not _hiddenDist ? 4 : 0
    _retail + _aviation + _quality
f_leaderCandidate(_posOpen, _kgs, _health, _smcBull, _smcStrength, _cvdUp, _cvdBull, _fake, _panic, _distribution, _hiddenDist) =>
    not _posOpen and _kgs > 60 and (_health == "STRONG" or _health == "NORMAL") and
         (_smcBull or _smcStrength >= 1) and (_cvdUp or _cvdBull) and
         not _fake and not _panic and not _distribution and not _hiddenDist
f_turnaroundCandidate(_posOpen, _kgs, _earlyTurn, _dipHunter, _cvdUp, _cvdBull, _rsiUp, _panic, _distribution) =>
    not _posOpen and _kgs >= 15 and _kgs < 46 and (_earlyTurn or _dipHunter) and
         (_cvdUp or _cvdBull) and _rsiUp and not _panic and not _distribution
f_xbankEarlyLeader(_isBank, _xbankRet3h, _relVol, _close, _emaFast, _emaSlopeUp, _sessionOk, _fake, _panic, _limitZone) =>
    _isBank and _xbankRet3h > 1.0 and _relVol > 1.3 and _close > _emaFast and
         _emaSlopeUp and _sessionOk and not _fake and not _panic and not _limitZone
f_confidence(_emaStrong, _emaWeak, _emaCrit, _htf, _rs, _state, _holdDisplay,
     _trendFresh, _trendOld, _trendAging, _panic, _marketRisk, _bankGyo,
     _xbankFire, _xbankBear, _hasExitReason, _exitRiskFull, _fakeRisk,
     _isMomentum, _isDefensive, _leaderStock, _rsLeader, _stockSectorLeader, _rsPrev3) =>
    _base = _emaStrong and _htf == 2 and _rs > 1.0 ? 54 : _emaStrong and _htf >= 1 ? 48 : _emaStrong ? 44 : not _emaWeak and not _emaCrit ? 42 : _emaWeak and _htf >= 1 ? 40 : _emaWeak ? 36 : _emaCrit and not _panic ? 32 : 26
    _stateMult = _state == "BUY" and _holdDisplay == "HOLD STRONG" ? 1.12 : _state == "BUY" ? 1.08 : _state == "HOLD" and _holdDisplay == "HOLD STRONG" ? 1.07 : _state == "HOLD" and _holdDisplay == "HOLD WEAK" ? 0.92 : _state == "HOLD" ? 1.00 : _state == "PREP" ? 0.95 : _state == "WATCH" ? 0.85 : _state == "SELL" ? 0.58 : _state == "NEUTRAL" ? 0.72 : 0.78
    _rsBonus = _rs > 3.0 ? 5 : _rs > 1.5 ? 3 : _rs > 0.0 ? 1 : _rs > -1.5 ? -2 : -6
    _htfBonus = _htf == 2 ? 3 : _htf == 1 ? 1 : -3
    _trendBonus = _trendFresh ? 3 : _trendOld ? -5 : _trendAging ? -2 : 0
    _riskPenalty = _panic ? -14 : _marketRisk and _bankGyo ? -8 : _marketRisk ? -5 : 0
    _bankBonus = _bankGyo and _xbankFire ? 4 : _bankGyo and _xbankBear ? -5 : 0
    _exitPenalty = _hasExitReason ? -7 : _exitRiskFull ? -5 : 0
    _fakePenalty = _fakeRisk and _state == "HOLD" ? -4 : 0
    _dnaBonus = _isMomentum and _emaStrong ? 3 : _isDefensive ? -1 : 0
    _leaderBonus = _leaderStock and _rsLeader and _rs > 1.5 ? 18 : _leaderStock and _rs > 1.5 ? 15 : _leaderStock ? 8 : _stockSectorLeader and _rs > 0 ? 5 : 0
    _accelBonus = _rs > _rsPrev3 + 1.0 ? 5 : _rs > _rsPrev3 + 0.5 ? 2 : 0
    f_clamp(math.round(_base * _stateMult) + _rsBonus + _htfBonus + _trendBonus +
         _riskPenalty + _bankBonus + _exitPenalty + _fakePenalty + _dnaBonus +
         _leaderBonus + _accelBonus, 6, 95)
f_kgsTrend(_emaStrong, _htf, _emaWeak, _emaCrit) =>
    _emaStrong and _htf == 2 ? 18 : _emaStrong ? 15 : not _emaWeak and not _emaCrit ? 13 : _emaWeak ? 6 : 2
f_kgsEma(_aboveMain, _aboveSlow, _aboveMid, _htf) =>
    f_clamp((_aboveMain ? 7 : -4) + (_aboveSlow ? 4 : -2) + (_aboveMid ? 2 : -1) + (_htf >= 1 ? 3 : -2), -6, 14)
f_kgsRs(_rsRank, _sectorRank, _momentum, _volumeSpike) =>
    _base = _rsRank >= 80 and _sectorRank >= 70 ? 20 : _rsRank >= 70 and _sectorRank >= 55 ? 16 : _rsRank >= 55 ? 12 : _rsRank >= 40 ? 8 : _rsRank >= 25 ? 5 : _rsRank >= 10 ? 2 : 0
    _base + (_momentum and _volumeSpike ? 5 : _momentum ? 3 : 0)
f_kgsVol(_volumeHuge, _volumeSpike, _fake, _green, _relVol, _volumeDry) =>
    _volumeHuge and not _fake and _green ? 12 : _volumeSpike and not _fake ? 9 : _relVol > 1.2 and not _volumeDry ? 7 : _relVol > 0.9 and not _volumeDry ? 5 : _volumeDry ? 1 : 3
f_kgsSector(_strongEff, _stockLeader, _alpha, _strong, _rsSector, _leadScore) =>
    _base = _strongEff and _stockLeader and _alpha > 2.0 ? 12 : _strongEff and _stockLeader ? 10 : _strongEff ? 8 : _strong ? 5 : _rsSector > 0.0 ? 3 : _rsSector < -2.0 ? 1 : 2
    _base + math.max(0, _leadScore)
f_kgsCvd(_bullDiv, _cvdUp, _obvTrend, _hiddenDist) =>
    _bullDiv and _obvTrend ? 10 : _bullDiv ? 8 : _cvdUp and _obvTrend and not _hiddenDist ? 7 : _cvdUp and not _hiddenDist ? 4 : _hiddenDist ? 0 : 3
f_kgsKurum(_accum, _distribution, _mfiBull, _pressure, _obvTrend) =>
    _accum and not _distribution and _mfiBull ? 12 : _accum and not _distribution ? 10 : _pressure and not _distribution ? 8 : _obvTrend and _mfiBull ? 6 : _obvTrend ? 4 : _distribution ? 1 : 2
f_kgsRisk(_panic, _marketRisk, _bankGyo, _aboveMain, _exitRisk, _fake) =>
    _panic ? 15 : _marketRisk and _bankGyo and _aboveMain ? 2 : _marketRisk and _bankGyo ? 5 : _marketRisk ? 3 : _exitRisk ? 4 : _fake ? 1 : 0
f_kgsBankAdj(_bankGyo, _xbankBear, _aboveMain, _xbankFire) =>
    _bankGyo and _xbankBear and _aboveMain ? -4 : _bankGyo and _xbankBear ? -8 : _bankGyo and _xbankFire ? 4 : 0
f_kgsState(_state, _holdDisplay) =>
    _state == "BUY" and _holdDisplay == "HOLD STRONG" ? 7 : _state == "BUY" ? 5 : _state == "HOLD" and _holdDisplay == "HOLD STRONG" ? 4 : _state == "HOLD" and _holdDisplay == "HOLD WEAK" ? -2 : _state == "HOLD" ? 2 : _state == "WATCH" ? -1 : _state == "SELL" ? -7 : _state == "NEUTRAL" ? -2 : 0
f_leaderAcceleration(_rsDelta, _relVol, _cvdDelta, _cvdNorm, _emaSlope, _atr) =>
    _rsPart = f_clamp(math.round((_rsDelta + 0.25) * 12.5), 0, 25)
    _volPart = f_clamp(math.round((_relVol - 0.8) * 20.0), 0, 25)
    _cvdPart = f_clamp(math.round((_cvdDelta / math.max(_cvdNorm, 1.0) + 0.2) * 31.25), 0, 25)
    _emaPart = f_clamp(math.round((_emaSlope / math.max(_atr, syminfo.mintick)) * 125.0), 0, 25)
    _rsPart + _volPart + _cvdPart + _emaPart
f_reloadSignal(_barsSinceFake, _fakeClean, _relVol, _cvdUp, _rsUp, _emaUp, _aboveEma, _panic, _distribution) =>
    _barsSinceFake >= 1 and _barsSinceFake <= 5 and _fakeClean and _relVol > 1.2 and
         _cvdUp and _rsUp and _emaUp and _aboveEma and not _panic and not _distribution
f_v105Setup(_rs, _relVol, _cvd, _emaFast, _atr, _lastFakeBar, _fakeClean, _cvdUp, _panic, _distribution) =>
    _rsDelta = _rs - _rs[3]
    _cvdDelta = _cvd - _cvd[3]
    _cvdNorm = ta.sma(math.abs(_cvd - _cvd[1]), 20) * 3.0
    _emaSlope = _emaFast - _emaFast[3]
    _accel = f_leaderAcceleration(_rsDelta, _relVol, _cvdDelta, _cvdNorm, _emaSlope, _atr)
    _barsSinceFake = na(_lastFakeBar) ? 1000 : bar_index - _lastFakeBar
    _reload = f_reloadSignal(_barsSinceFake, _fakeClean, _relVol, _cvdUp,
         _rs > _rs[1], _emaFast > _emaFast[1], close > _emaFast, _panic, _distribution)
    _accelReady = _relVol > 1.3 and _emaFast > _emaFast[1] and _rs > 0 and
         _cvdDelta >= 0 and _barsSinceFake > 3
    [_accel, _reload, _accelReady]
f_v105Signals(_posOpen, _accel, _accelReady, _health, _rsSector, _close, _emaFast, _emaMid, _reloadSetup,
     _xbankEarly, _fake, _panic, _distribution, _kgs, _hiddenDist) =>
    _accelSignal = not _posOpen and _accel >= 75 and _accelReady and
         (_health == "STRONG" or _health == "NORMAL") and _rsSector > 0 and
         _close > _emaFast and not _reloadSetup and not _xbankEarly and
         not _fake and not _panic and not _distribution
    _reloadSignal = not _posOpen and _reloadSetup and _kgs >= 60 and
         _close > _emaMid and _health != "CRITICAL" and not _hiddenDist
    [_accelSignal, _reloadSignal]
f_volumeQuality(_green, _breakout, _fake, _relVol) =>
    _greenPts = _green ? 35 : 0
    _breakPts = _breakout ? 40 : 0
    _relPts = _relVol >= 2.0 ? 25 : _relVol >= 1.5 ? 20 : _relVol > 1.2 ? 15 : _relVol > 0.9 ? 8 : 0
    _fakePenalty = _fake ? 45 : 0
    _rawScore = _greenPts + _breakPts + _relPts - _fakePenalty
    _score = _fake ? f_clamp(_rawScore, 0, 100) : f_clamp(math.max(20, _rawScore), 0, 100)
    _txt = _fake ? "FAKE HACIM" : _breakout and _green ? "KIRILIM + YESIL" :
         _breakout ? "KIRILIM HACMI" : _green and _relVol > 1.2 ? "YESIL HACIM" :
         _relVol > 1.2 ? "HACIM VAR" : "HACIM ZAYIF"
    [_score, _txt]
// [OKUNABILIRLIK] Hacim/kirilim kalitesini olcer + gec-giris (FOMO) tespiti yapar.
f_v107MarketQuality(_close, _open, _high, _low, _relVol, _breakout, _srBreak,
     _fake, _isLimitUp, _emaFast, _atr) =>
    _green = _close > _open and _close >= _low + (_high - _low) * 0.60 and _relVol > 1.2
    _break = (_breakout or _srBreak) and _relVol > 1.2
    _fakeVol = _fake and _relVol > 1.2
    [_volQuality, _volTxt] = f_volumeQuality(_green, _break, _fakeVol, _relVol)
    _strong = _volQuality >= 70
    _extensionAtr = (_close - _emaFast) / math.max(_atr, syminfo.mintick)
    _late = _isLimitUp or _close > _emaFast + _atr * lateAtrMult or _close > _emaFast * lateEmaExtPct
    [_volQuality, _volTxt, _strong, _late, _extensionAtr]
f_buyWindow(_kgs, _health, _sectorLeader, _flowPositive, _volumeQuality,
     _extensionAtr, _lateEntry, _trendFresh, _fake, _panic) =>
    _clean = not _fake and not _panic
    _open = _clean and not _lateEntry and _kgs >= 70 and _sectorLeader and _flowPositive and
         (_health == "STRONG" or _health == "NORMAL") and _volumeQuality >= 45
    _narrowing = _clean and _kgs >= 70 and _kgs < 82 and _sectorLeader and _flowPositive and
         _health == "STRONG" and _trendFresh and _extensionAtr <= 2.4
    _open ? "ACIK" : _narrowing ? "DARALIYOR" : "KAPALI"
f_finalAlertEvent(_finalDecision) =>
    _raw = _finalDecision == "AL" or _finalDecision == "TEYITLI AL" or _finalDecision == "ERKEN AL"
    _raw and (not _raw[1] or _finalDecision != _finalDecision[1])
f_lcCooldown(_candidate, _cooldownBars) =>
    var int _lastBar = na
    _fire = _candidate and (na(_lastBar) or bar_index - _lastBar > _cooldownBars)
    if _fire
        _lastBar := bar_index
    _fire
// [OKUNABILIRLIK] Giris zamanlamasi penceresini (DARALIYOR/ACIK/KAPALI) hesaplar.
f_v111TimingTrigger(_buyWindow, _kgs, _health, _sectorLeader, _flowPositive,
     _emaSlopeUp, _priceUp, _rsiUp, _volumeQuality, _extensionAtr, _fake, _panic) =>
    _volumeConfirm = _volumeQuality >= 45 or _volumeQuality > _volumeQuality[1] + 10
    _setup = _buyWindow == "DARALIYOR" and _kgs >= 70 and _health == "STRONG" and
         _sectorLeader and _flowPositive and _emaSlopeUp and _priceUp and _rsiUp and
         _volumeConfirm and _extensionAtr <= 2.1 and not _fake and not _panic
    _setup and not _setup[1]
f_wakingUp(_kgs, _close, _rsi, _fake, _panic) =>
    _momentum3 = not na(_close[3]) and _close[3] > 0 ? (_close / _close[3] - 1.0) * 100.0 : 0.0
    _kgs < 25 and _momentum3 > 5.0 and _close > _close[1] and _rsi > _rsi[1] and
         not _fake and not _panic
f_entryScore(_kgs, _health, _secRank, _flowStrong, _flowPositive, _flowNegative,
     _cvdStrong, _cvdNegative, _volumeQuality, _accel, _buyWindow, _timingTrigger,
     _lateEntry, _fake, _highVolWick, _panic, _distribution, _htfAlign, _trendAge,
     _rr, _atrExpansion, _momentumSetup, _earlyAlpha, _leaderFloorBoost,
     _kgsBridgeBoost, _holdingBankBoost) =>
    _base = _kgs * 0.38 + _volumeQuality * 0.12 + _accel * 0.10
    _trendPts = _health == "STRONG" ? 10.0 : _health == "NORMAL" ? 5.0 : _health == "WEAK" ? -3.0 : -12.0
    _sectorPts = _secRank >= 80 ? 10.0 : _secRank >= 65 ? 6.0 : _secRank >= 50 ? 3.0 : 0.0
    _flowPts = _flowStrong ? 12.0 : _flowPositive ? 8.0 : _flowNegative ? -8.0 : 0.0
    _cvdPts = _cvdStrong ? 5.0 : _cvdNegative ? -5.0 : 0.0
    _windowPts = _buyWindow == "ACIK" ? 7.0 : _buyWindow == "DARALIYOR" ? 3.0 : -5.0
    _momentumPts = _momentumSetup ? 10.0 : _timingTrigger ? 5.0 : 0.0
    _htfPts = _htfAlign == 2 ? 6.0 : _htfAlign == 1 ? 0.0 : -12.0
    _rrPts = _rr > 2.0 ? 6.0 : _rr >= 1.5 ? 1.0 : -12.0
    _volatilityPts = _atrExpansion and _health == "STRONG" ? 4.0 : _atrExpansion and _health == "WEAK" ? -5.0 : 0.0
    _agePenalty = _trendAge > 40 ? 24.0 : _trendAge > 20 ? 18.0 : _trendAge > 12 ? 8.0 : 0.0
    _latePenalty = _lateEntry ? (_health == "STRONG" ? 7.0 : 14.0) : 0.0
    _riskPts = _latePenalty + (_fake ? 16.0 : 0.0) + (_highVolWick ? 10.0 : 0.0) +
         (_panic ? 25.0 : 0.0) + (_distribution ? 12.0 : 0.0)
    _raw = _base + _trendPts + _sectorPts + _flowPts + _cvdPts + _windowPts +
         _momentumPts + _htfPts + _rrPts + _volatilityPts + (_earlyAlpha ? 6.0 : 0.0) +
         _kgsBridgeBoost + _holdingBankBoost -
         _agePenalty - _riskPts
    _leaderFloor = _leaderFloorBoost
    int(math.round(f_clamp(math.max(_raw, _leaderFloor), 0, 100)))
// [OKUNABILIRLIK] ANA KARAR FONKSIYONU - tum skorlardan nihai kararı (AL/IZLE/PAS GEC) uretir.
f_v114Decision(_entryScore, _kgs, _health, _sectorLeader, _flowPositive, _fake, _panic,
     _daytradeMode, _stateBuy, _freshBuy, _hasPortfolioPosition, _leaderCandidate,
     _accelSignal, _accel, _reloadSignal, _relVol, _lateEntry, _strongVolume,
     _volumeQuality, _trendFresh, _exitRisk, _buyWindow, _timingTrigger,
     _htfAlign, _trendAge, _rr) =>
    _quality = ""
    _valid = not _lateEntry and _sectorLeader and ((_stateBuy and _kgs >= 70) or
         (_leaderCandidate and _kgs >= 70) or (_accelSignal and _accel >= 75) or
         (_reloadSignal and _relVol > 1.2))
    _daytrade = _valid and (_health == "STRONG" or _health == "NORMAL") and _strongVolume
    _decision = not _daytradeMode ? "SWING FILTRE" : _daytrade ? "DAYTRADE UYGUN" :
         _kgs >= 58 and _health != "CRITICAL" ? "SWING UYGUN / DAYTRADE DEGIL" : "SADECE IZLE"
    _earlyLeaderEntry = _leaderCandidate and _kgs >= 70 and _sectorLeader and _flowPositive and
         _trendFresh and (_health == "STRONG" or _health == "NORMAL") and _volumeQuality >= 45
    // PATCH 2 (Loop Engineering P1): ayni acik _windowCandidate'te de vardi - bu
    // degisken P2 ADAY alarmini besliyor (satir ~3130), gec giriste tetiklenmemesi
    // gerekir.
    _windowCandidate = _buyWindow == "DARALIYOR" and _kgs >= 70 and _sectorLeader and
         _flowPositive and not _lateEntry
    _blocked = _fake or _panic or _health == "CRITICAL"
    _entryBlocked = _htfAlign < 1 or _trendAge > 20 or _rr < 1.5
    _alGate = _htfAlign == 2 and _trendAge <= 20 and _rr > 2.0 and not _lateEntry and
         _sectorLeader and _flowPositive
    // 17.09 B1: "and _kgs >= 75" kaldirildi (bkz. header, V162.1 EK)
    _scoreAlGate = _entryScore >= 85 and _sectorLeader and _health == "STRONG" and
         _htfAlign >= 1 and _trendAge <= 20 and _rr >= 1.5 and not _lateEntry
    // 17.09 B1: "and _kgs >= 70" kaldirildi (bkz. header, V162.1 EK)
    _scoreEarlyGate = _entryScore >= 75 and _sectorLeader and _health != "CRITICAL" and
         _htfAlign >= 1 and _trendAge <= 20 and not _lateEntry
    // PATCH 1 (Loop Engineering P1): _earlyGate eskiden not _lateEntry KONTROLU
    // ICERMIYORDU - _alGate/_scoreAlGate/_scoreEarlyGate'te bu kontrol VARDI ama
    // burada unutulmustu. Sonuc: fiyat zaten asiri uzamisken (limit-up veya EMA'nin
    // %3.5+ ustunde) bile "ERKEN AL" sinyali sizabiliyordu. Mantik YIKILMADI, sadece
    // zaten var olan tutarlilik kurali tamamlandi.
    // 17.09 B1: "and _kgs > 75" kaldirildi (bkz. header, V162.1 EK)
    _earlyGate = _entryScore >= 65 and _entryScore < 80 and _sectorLeader and
         _htfAlign >= 1 and _trendAge <= 20 and not _fake and not _panic and not _lateEntry
    _final = _hasPortfolioPosition and _exitRisk ? "CIK" : _hasPortfolioPosition ? "HOLD" :
         _blocked ? "PAS GEC" :
         _entryScore >= 88 and _freshBuy and _alGate and _buyWindow != "KAPALI" ? "TEYITLI AL" :
         _scoreAlGate ? "AL" :
         _entryScore >= 80 and _alGate and _buyWindow != "KAPALI" ? "AL" :
         _scoreEarlyGate ? "ERKEN AL" :
         _earlyGate ? "ERKEN AL" :
         _entryBlocked and _entryScore >= 65 ? "IZLE" :
         _entryScore >= 68 ? "ADAY AL" : _entryScore >= 55 ? "IZLE" : "PAS GEC"
    [_quality, _valid, _decision, _final, _daytrade, _earlyLeaderEntry, _windowCandidate]
// [OKUNABILIRLIK] Reload/ekleme (piramitleme) adayligini degerlendirir.
f_v118Meta(_reloadSignal, _kgs, _close, _emaMid, _relVol, _flowStrong, _flowPositive,
     _health, _emaFast, _lateEntry, _finalDecision, _entryScore, _leadScore, _fake,
     _panic, _windowCandidate, _leaderCandidate, _wakingUp, _smState, _setupRef) =>
    _reloadQualityScore = f_clamp((_reloadSignal ? 20 : 0) + (_kgs >= 82 ? 25 : _kgs >= 70 ? 20 : _kgs >= 60 ? 12 : 0) +
         (_close > _emaMid ? 15 : 0) + (_relVol > 1.5 ? 20 : _relVol > 1.2 ? 12 : 0) +
         (_flowStrong ? 15 : _flowPositive ? 10 : 0) + (_health == "STRONG" ? 15 : _health == "NORMAL" ? 8 : 0), 0, 100)
    _reloadTxt = not _reloadSignal ? "RELOAD YOK" :
         _reloadQualityScore >= 85 ? "A+ RELOAD" :
         _reloadQualityScore >= 70 ? "A RELOAD" :
         _reloadQualityScore >= 55 ? "B RELOAD" : "C RELOAD"
    _reloadClr = not _reloadSignal ? color.rgb(85, 92, 108) :
         _reloadQualityScore >= 85 ? color.rgb(0, 220, 80) :
         _reloadQualityScore >= 70 ? color.rgb(30, 150, 80) :
         _reloadQualityScore >= 55 ? color.rgb(220, 180, 0) : color.rgb(200, 60, 60)
    _ema9DistPct = _emaFast > 0 ? (_close / _emaFast - 1.0) * 100.0 : 0.0
    _setupDistPct = not na(_setupRef) and _setupRef > 0 ? (_close / _setupRef - 1.0) * 100.0 : 0.0
    _fomoRefPct = math.max(_ema9DistPct, _setupDistPct)
    _fomoTxt = _fomoRefPct >= 8 ? "FOMO KIRMIZI | KOVALAMA" :
         _fomoRefPct >= 5 ? "FOMO TURUNCU | RISK" :
         _fomoRefPct >= 3 ? "FOMO SARI | DIKKAT" :
         _lateEntry ? "GEC GIRIS" :
         _reloadSignal ? "EARLY KACTI | RELOAD" : "FOMO YOK"
    _fomoClr = _fomoRefPct >= 8 ? color.rgb(200, 60, 60) :
         _fomoRefPct >= 5 ? color.rgb(220, 120, 0) :
         _fomoRefPct >= 3 ? color.rgb(220, 180, 0) :
         _lateEntry ? color.rgb(220, 120, 0) : color.rgb(85, 92, 108)
    // 17.09 B1: "and _kgs >= 70" kaldirildi (bkz. header, V162.1 EK) -
    // _leadScore >= 70 DOKUNULMADI (test kapsami disinda)
    _alarmP1 = (_finalDecision == "AL" or _finalDecision == "TEYITLI AL" or _finalDecision == "ERKEN AL") and
         _entryScore >= 65 and _leadScore >= 70 and not _lateEntry
    // 17.09 B1: "and _kgs >= 58" kaldirildi (bkz. header, V162.1 EK) -
    // _leadScore >= 60 DOKUNULMADI (test kapsami disinda)
    _alarmP2 = (_finalDecision == "ADAY AL" or _windowCandidate or _reloadSignal) and
         _entryScore >= 55 and _leadScore >= 60 and not _fake and not _panic
    _alarmP3 = (_leaderCandidate or _wakingUp or _smState == "WATCH") and _health != "CRITICAL"
    _alarmTxt = _alarmP1 ? "P1 KALITELI AL" : _alarmP2 ? "P2 ADAY" : _alarmP3 ? "P3 RADAR" : "PAS"
    _alarmClr = _alarmP1 ? color.rgb(0, 220, 80) : _alarmP2 ? color.rgb(220, 180, 0) :
         _alarmP3 ? color.rgb(220, 120, 0) : color.rgb(200, 60, 60)
    [_reloadTxt, _reloadClr, _fomoRefPct, _fomoTxt, _fomoClr, _alarmTxt, _alarmClr]
// [OKUNABILIRLIK] Sektor lideri siralama puanini hesaplar.
f_v118Lead(_secLeadRank, _rsRank, _momRank) =>
    _leadRaw = f_clamp(math.round(_secLeadRank * 0.40 + _rsRank * 0.40 + _momRank * 0.20), 0, 100)
    f_clamp(math.round(ta.ema(_leadRaw * 1.0, 3)), 0, 100)
f_passReason(_score, _health, _late, _volumeQuality, _fake, _panic, _distribution,
     _htfAlign, _trendAge, _rr) =>
    _fake or _panic or _distribution or _htfAlign < 1 or _rr < 1.5 ? "PAS (RISK)" :
     _late or _trendAge > 20 ? "PAS (GEC)" :
     _volumeQuality < 40 ? "PAS (HACIM)" :
     _score < 55 or _health == "WEAK" or _health == "CRITICAL" ? "PAS (ZAYIF)" : "PAS"
f_noBuyReason(_final, _htfAlign, _rr, _late, _trendAge, _volumeQuality, _leadScore,
     _flowPositive, _buyWindow, _fake, _panic) =>
    _isBuy = _final == "AL" or _final == "TEYITLI AL" or _final == "ERKEN AL"
    _rrLow = _rr < 1.5
    _rrTxt = "RR<1.5(" + str.tostring(_rr,"#.#") + ")"
    _r1 = _fake or _panic ? "RISK" :
         _htfAlign < 1 ? "HTF" :
         _rrLow ? _rrTxt :
         _late or _trendAge > 20 ? "GEC" :
         _volumeQuality < 40 ? "HACIM" :
         _leadScore < 60 ? "LIDER" :
         not _flowPositive ? "AKIS" :
         _buyWindow == "KAPALI" ? "PENCERE" : "TEYIT"
    _r1IsRr = _rrLow and _r1 == _rrTxt
    _r2 = not _r1IsRr and _rrLow ? " + " + _rrTxt :
         _r1 != "HACIM" and _volumeQuality < 40 ? " + HACIM" :
         _r1 != "AKIS" and not _flowPositive ? " + AKIS" :
         _r1 != "GEC" and (_late or _trendAge > 20) ? " + GEC" : ""
    _isBuy ? "TETIK OK" : "ALMADI: " + _r1 + _r2
f_radarLite(_relVol, _range, _atr, _close, _low, _vwap, _flowStrong, _flowPositive,
     _hiddenDist, _highVolWick, _leaderAccel, _emaFast, _rsIndex, _trendWeakening,
     _upperWick, _body, _trendAge, _rsi, _atrAvg, _leadScore, _sector, _xbankFire,
     _holdRot, _marketRiskOn, _sectorStrong, _marketRisk, _health, _volQ) =>
    _loc = _range > syminfo.mintick ? (_close - _low) / _range * 100.0 : 50.0
    _sm = f_clamp(math.round((_relVol > 1.5 ? 25 : _relVol > 1.2 ? 18 : _relVol > 0.9 ? 10 : 0) + (_range > _atr * 1.2 ? 20 : _range > _atr * 0.8 ? 12 : 0) + (_loc > 70 ? 20 : _loc > 55 ? 12 : 0) + (_close > _vwap ? 15 : 0) + (_flowStrong ? 20 : _flowPositive ? 10 : 0) - (_hiddenDist ? 15 : 0) - (_highVolWick ? 10 : 0)), 0, 100)
    _mom = f_clamp(math.round(_leaderAccel * 0.45 + (_emaFast > _emaFast[1] ? 15 : 0) + (_rsIndex > 0 ? 10 : 0) + (_relVol > 1.2 ? 10 : 0) + (not _trendWeakening ? 20 : 0)), 0, 100)
    _exh = f_clamp(math.round((_upperWick > _body * 1.2 ? 25 : 0) + (_relVol < 0.8 and _trendAge > 8 ? 15 : 0) + (_trendWeakening ? 25 : 0) + (_rsi > 75 ? 15 : 0) + (_atr < _atrAvg ? 10 : 0) + (_hiddenDist ? 20 : 0)), 0, 100)
    _rank = _sector + " #" + (_leadScore >= 85 ? "1" : _leadScore >= 75 ? "2" : _leadScore >= 65 ? "3" : _leadScore >= 55 ? "4" : "5+")
    _reg = _xbankFire ? "BANK LEAD" : _holdRot ? "HOLD LEAD" : _marketRiskOn and _sectorStrong ? "BROAD ON" : _sectorStrong ? "ROTATION" : _marketRisk ? "RISK OFF" : "NOTR"
    _hold = f_clamp(math.round((_health == "STRONG" ? 30 : _health == "NORMAL" ? 18 : 5) + _mom * 0.25 + _sm * 0.20 + _leadScore * 0.15 + _volQ * 0.10), 0, 100)
    [_sm, _mom, _exh, _rank, _reg, _hold]
f_alarmClass(_final, _entry, _kgs, _lead, _health, _flow, _fake, _panic,
     _lc, _wake, _state, _window, _reload, _early) =>
    _p1 = _final == "AL" or _final == "TEYITLI AL" or _final == "ERKEN AL"
    _sectorWatch = _entry >= 55 and _lead >= 55 and _kgs >= 70 and _health == "STRONG" and _flow and not _fake and not _panic
    _p2 = not _p1 and (_final == "ADAY AL" or _window or _reload or _sectorWatch)
    _p3 = not _p1 and not _p2 and (_lc or _wake or _state == "WATCH" or (_entry > 40 and _kgs > 50)) and _health != "CRITICAL" // 08.08 DUZELTME: esik 30->40 (sinyal_dogrulama.py: T+1 ort getiri negatif, dogrulanan %42.9 - gevsek esik supheli)
    _p1a = _p1 and _entry >= 80 and _kgs >= 75 and _lead >= 65
    _p2b = _p2 and (_reload or _lc or _early or _sectorWatch)
    [_p1, _p2, _p3, _p1a, _p1 and not _p1a, _p2 and not _p2b, _p2b]
f_blockers(_health, _rsRank, _volQ, _flow, _lead, _rr, _late, _exh, _htfA, _age, _secLead) =>
    (_health == "WEAK" or _health == "CRITICAL" ? "TR " : "") +
     (_rsRank < 40 ? "RS " : "") +
     (_volQ < 40 ? "VOL " : "") +
     (not _flow ? "FLOW " : "") +
     (_lead < 55 ? "LEAD " : "") +
     (_rr < 1.5 ? "RR " : "") +
     (_late ? "LATE " : "") +
     (_exh >= 70 ? "FAT " : "") +
     (_htfA < 2 ? "HTF " : "") +
     (_age > 20 ? "AGE " : "") +
     (not _secLead ? "SECLEAD " : "")
f_adaptiveVolumeQuality(_volQuality, _sector, _relVol, _health, _waking) =>
    _soften = _sector == "BANKACILIK" ? 8 :
         _sector == "HOLDING" ? 10 :
         _sector == "PERAKENDE" ? 16 :
         _sector == "ENERJI" ? 8 :
         _sector == "HAVACILIK" ? 6 : 0
    _recovery = _waking and (_sector == "PERAKENDE" or _sector == "ENERJI" or _sector == "HOLDING") ? 8 : 0
    _trendKeep = (_health == "STRONG" and _relVol > 0.9) or (_health == "NORMAL" and (_sector == "PERAKENDE" or _sector == "HOLDING") and _relVol > 0.75) ? 4 : 0
    f_clamp(_volQuality + _soften + _recovery + _trendKeep, 0, 100)
f_leaderFloor(_sector, _kgs, _secRank, _health, _htfAlign, _fake, _panic) =>
    _valid = _kgs >= 75 and _secRank >= 65 and _htfAlign >= 1 and not _fake and not _panic
    _base = _sector == "HOLDING" ? 60.0 :
         _sector == "BANKACILIK" ? 56.0 :
         _sector == "HAVACILIK" ? 55.0 :
         _sector == "PERAKENDE" ? 45.0 :
         _sector == "ENERJI" ? 38.0 : 48.0
    _valid and (_health == "STRONG" or _health == "NORMAL") ? _base : 0.0
// [OKUNABILIRLIK] CVD/SMC (akilli para) baglamini (kurumsal akis, birikim) degerlendirir.
f_v113Context(_cvdRising, _cvdBull, _accum, _institutional, _mfiBull, _distributionFlow,
     _obvTrend, _relVol, _hiddenDist, _mfi, _cvd, _cvdSma, _cvdBear, _cvdFalling,
     _high, _high3, _emaFast, _emaMid, _indexChg5, _stockRoc, _rsIndex,
     _upperWick, _body, _close, _range) =>
    _flowPos = _cvdRising or _cvdBull or _accum or _institutional
    _flowStrongLocal = (_accum and _mfiBull and not _distributionFlow) or
         (_institutional and _obvTrend and _relVol > 1.5)
    _flowNeg = _distributionFlow or _hiddenDist or (not _obvTrend and _mfi < 40)
    _cvdStrongLocal = _cvdBull or (_cvdRising and _cvd > _cvdSma and _cvd > _cvd[5])
    _cvdNeg = _cvdBear or (_cvdFalling and _cvd < _cvdSma)
    _momentum = _high > _high3 and _emaFast > _emaMid and
         (_emaFast - _emaMid) > (_emaFast[1] - _emaMid[1]) and _relVol > 1.3
    _alpha = math.abs(_indexChg5) < 0.75 and _stockRoc > 0 and _rsIndex > 2.0
    _wickRisk = _relVol > 1.5 and _upperWick > _body * 1.2 and
         _close < _high - _range * 0.35
    [_flowPos, _flowStrongLocal, _flowNeg, _cvdStrongLocal, _cvdNeg, _momentum, _alpha, _wickRisk]
// === MODUL 2 (kurumsal): GIRIS ANI FAKTOR-KATKI - 6 ana adlandirilmis alt-skordan en
// yuksek katkiliyi bulur. Saf fonksiyon (parametre disi hicbir global degiskene
// dokunmuyor), DONGU YOK, mevcut fonksiyon imzalarina DOKUNULMADI. ===
f_topFactor(sTrend, sRs, sSector, sFlow, sObv, sInst) =>
    mx = math.max(sTrend, math.max(sRs, math.max(sSector, math.max(sFlow, math.max(sObv, sInst)))))
    mx == sTrend ? "TREND" : mx == sRs ? "RS" : mx == sSector ? "SECTOR" : mx == sFlow ? "FLOW" : mx == sObv ? "OBV" : "INST"
// [OKUNABILIRLIK] GERCEK islem yasam dongusu (giris/cikis/PnL) - panelde gosterilen
// BT/EXPECT/OOS/REJIM-BT gibi TUM istatistiklerin veri kaynagi budur.
f_v112Validation(_entryEvent, _exitEvent, _price) =>
    var bool _open = false
    var float _entry = na
    var int _total = 0
    var int _wins = 0
    var float _sumW = 0.0
    var float _sumL = 0.0
    var float _last = 0.0
    var float _eq = 0.0
    var float _peak = 0.0
    var float _maxDd = 0.0
    _entered = false
    if _entryEvent and not _open
        _open := true
        _entry := _price
        _entered := true
    else if _exitEvent and _open and not na(_entry)
        _last := (_price - _entry) / _entry * 100.0
        _total += 1
        if _last > 0
            _wins += 1
            _sumW += _last
        else
            _sumL += math.abs(_last)
        _eq += _last
        _peak := math.max(_peak, _eq)
        _maxDd := math.min(_maxDd, _eq - _peak)
        _open := false
        _entry := na
    _losses = _total - _wins
    _wr = _total > 0 ? _wins / _total * 100.0 : 0.0
    _avgW = _wins > 0 ? _sumW / _wins : 0.0
    _avgL = _losses > 0 ? _sumL / _losses : 0.0
    // 08.08 DUZELTME: kayip YOK (sumL~0) ama kazanc VARSA PF artik
    // 999 ('mukemmel') gosterilir, 0.0 DEGIL - eskiden 'hic kaybetmedi'
    // ile 'hic kazanmadi' AYNI (0.0) gorunuyordu, TERSI durumlardi.
    _pf = _sumL > 0.001 ? _sumW / _sumL : (_sumW > 0 ? 999.0 : 0.0)
    _exp = _wr / 100.0 * _avgW - (1.0 - _wr / 100.0) * _avgL
    [_entered, _open, _entry, _total, _wr, _pf, _exp, _avgW, _avgL, _maxDd]
f_perfBucket(_closed, _pnl, _active) =>
    var int _n = 0
    var int _w = 0
    var float _sw = 0.0
    var float _sl = 0.0
    if _closed and _active
        _n += 1
        if _pnl > 0
            _w += 1
            _sw += _pnl
        else
            _sl += math.abs(_pnl)
    _wr = _n > 0 ? _w / _n * 100.0 : 0.0
    // 08.08 DUZELTME: ayni PF=0 belirsizligi (bkz. f_v112Validation)
    _pf = _sl > 0.001 ? _sw / _sl : (_sw > 0 ? 999.0 : 0.0)
    [_n, _wr, _pf]
f_buyZone(_emaFast, _emaMid, _srBreak, _srRes, _close) =>
    _emaLo = math.min(_emaFast, _emaMid)
    _emaHi = math.max(_emaFast, _emaMid)
    _retLo = _srBreak and not na(_srRes) ? _srRes * 0.997 : na
    _retHi = _srBreak and not na(_srRes) ? _srRes * 1.006 : na
    _loRaw = not na(_retLo) ? math.max(_retLo, _emaLo) : _emaLo
    _hiRaw = not na(_retHi) ? math.min(math.max(_retHi, _loRaw), _close) : _emaHi
    [math.min(_loRaw, _hiRaw), math.max(_loRaw, _hiRaw)]
f_alarmQuality(_alarm, _price, _tp1, _tp2, _high, _low) =>
    var int _n1 = 0
    var int _w1 = 0
    var int _n3 = 0
    var int _w3 = 0
    var int _n5 = 0
    var int _w5 = 0
    var int _tp1N = 0
    var int _tp2N = 0
    var int _falseN = 0
    var float _worstDd = 0.0
    _p1 = _alarm[1] and not na(_price[1]) ? (_price / _price[1] - 1.0) * 100.0 : na
    _p3 = _alarm[3] and not na(_price[3]) ? (_price / _price[3] - 1.0) * 100.0 : na
    _p5 = _alarm[5] and not na(_price[5]) ? (_price / _price[5] - 1.0) * 100.0 : na
    _hi5 = ta.highest(_high, 5)
    _lo5 = ta.lowest(_low, 5)
    if not na(_p1)
        _n1 += 1
        _w1 += (_p1 > 0 ? 1 : 0)
    if not na(_p3)
        _n3 += 1
        _w3 += (_p3 > 0 ? 1 : 0)
    if not na(_p5)
        _n5 += 1
        _w5 += (_p5 > 0 ? 1 : 0)
        _dd = (_lo5 / _price[5] - 1.0) * 100.0
        _tp1Hit = not na(_tp1[5]) and _hi5 >= _tp1[5]
        _tp2Hit = not na(_tp2[5]) and _hi5 >= _tp2[5]
        _worstDd := math.min(_worstDd, _dd)
        _tp1N += (_tp1Hit ? 1 : 0)
        _tp2N += (_tp2Hit ? 1 : 0)
        _falseN += (_p5 < 0 and not _tp1Hit ? 1 : 0)
    _wr1 = _n1 > 0 ? _w1 / _n1 * 100.0 : 0.0
    _wr3 = _n3 > 0 ? _w3 / _n3 * 100.0 : 0.0
    _wr5 = _n5 > 0 ? _w5 / _n5 * 100.0 : 0.0
    _tp1Rate = _n5 > 0 ? _tp1N / _n5 * 100.0 : 0.0
    _tp2Rate = _n5 > 0 ? _tp2N / _n5 * 100.0 : 0.0
    _falseRate = _n5 > 0 ? _falseN / _n5 * 100.0 : 0.0
    [_n5, _wr1, _wr3, _wr5, _tp1Rate, _tp2Rate, _falseRate, _worstDd]

// === SAAT-AYARLI HACIM TABANI (U-egrisi duzeltmesi) ===============

volAvgSma  = ta.sma(volume, volLen)
_todTfMin  = math.max(timeframe.in_seconds() / 60, 1)

// yalniz TAMAMLANMIS onceki gun - repaint yok. Deger yuzde (2.8 = gunluk %2.8).
_dVolPct = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "D", math.sqrt(ta.ema(math.pow(close / close[1] - 1.0, 2), 32)[1]) * 100.0, lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_on)
// Hedef 2.5 = BIST100 gunluk EWMA oynaklik medyani (arsivden aylik guncellenir).
_lotCarpan = math.max(0.4, math.min(1.6, 2.5 / _dVolPct))
xu100Ema50 = ta.ema(nz(xu100Close, close), 50)
idxClose = request.security(indexSymbol,        timeframe.period, close, lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)
_secRaw  = request.security(activeSectorSymbol, timeframe.period, close, lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)
secClose = na(_secRaw) ? xu100Close : _secRaw

_xbankRet3h = not na(_xbank60Close) and not na(_xbank60Close3) and _xbank60Close3 != 0 ?
     (_xbank60Close - _xbank60Close3) / _xbank60Close3 * 100 : 0.0

_xbankBear  = not na(_xbankClose) and not na(_xbankEma50) and _xbankClose < _xbankEma50
_xbankEarlyFire = false

     (_xbankClose - _xbankClose[3]) / _xbankClose[3] * 100 : 0.0
_xbankStrong  = _xbankReturn3 > 1.5
_xbankFire    = _xbankReturn3 > 2.5
_brentRawBase = request.security("NYMEX:CL1!", timeframe.period, close, ignore_invalid_symbol=true, lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)

_brentClose    = na(_brentRaw) ? na : _brentRaw

energyRsBonus = _isBrentSensitive ? (_brentStrength > 2.0 ? 8 : _brentStrength > 0.5 ? 5 : _brentStrength >= 0.0 ? 2 : _brentStrength > -1.0 ? 0 : -2) : 0
_goldRaw_base   = request.security("TVC:GOLD",      timeframe.period, close, ignore_invalid_symbol=true, lookahead=barmerge.lookahead_off)

idxReturn1      = (idxClose - idxClose[1]) / math.max(nz(idxClose[1], xu100Close), 0.001)
stkReturn1      = (close - close[1]) / math.max(close[1], 0.001)
rsStrengthBonus = idxReturn1 < -0.003 and stkReturn1 > idxReturn1
bbBasis    = ta.sma(close, 20)
bbStdev    = ta.stdev(close, 20)
bbUpper    = bbBasis + 2 * bbStdev
bbWidth    = (bbBasis + 2 * bbStdev) - (bbBasis - 2 * bbStdev)
bbWidthAvg = ta.sma(bbWidth, 50)
idxRoc   = ta.roc(nz(idxClose, xu100Close), 20)
secRoc   = ta.roc(nz(secClose, close), 20)
stkRoc   = ta.roc(close, 20)
rsIndex  = stkRoc - idxRoc
rsSector = stkRoc - secRoc
rsStrong = rsIndex > 2.0 and rsSector > 1.0
_rsEma20 = ta.ema(rsIndex, 20)
_rsEma50 = ta.ema(rsIndex, 50)
_rsLeader = _rsEma20 > _rsEma50 and rsIndex > 1.0
rsRank      = math.round(ta.percentrank(rsIndex, 100))
rsSectorRnk = math.round(ta.percentrank(rsSector, 100))
rsAccel     = rsRank - nz(rsRank[5], rsRank)
_rsPercentile = math.round(rsRank * 0.6 + rsSectorRnk * 0.4)
_rsTopQuartile = _rsPercentile >= 75
_rsMomentum    = rsRank > rsRank[5] + 5
isSectorStrong       = secRoc > idxRoc + 0.5
isStockLeadingSector = rsSector > 1.0 and rsIndex > 0
sectorStrong         = secRoc > idxRoc
_isRSLeader = (_rsTopQuartile and _rsLeader) or (rsRank >= 80 and isStockLeadingSector)
_secLeadRank   = math.round(ta.percentrank(rsSector, 63))  // ~3 aylk pencere
_secLeadStreak = rsSector > 0 and rsSector[3] > 0 and rsSector[5] > 0  // 5 bar sektr st
_secLeadScore =
     _secLeadRank >= 80 and _secLeadStreak ? 3 :
     _secLeadRank >= 65 ? 2 :
     _secLeadRank >= 45 ? 1 :
     _secLeadRank >= 25 ? 0 : -1
_leadScore = f_v118Lead(_secLeadRank, rsRank, math.round(ta.percentrank(ta.roc(close, 5), 63)))
_leadScore := (autoSector == "BANKACILIK" or _isHolding) and secRoc > idxRoc and rsSector > 0 ? math.max(_leadScore, 58) : _leadScore
_leadScore := (autoSector == "BANKACILIK" or _isHolding) and rsSector > 2 and secRoc > idxRoc + 0.5 ? math.min(100, _leadScore + 10) : _leadScore
_leadScore := autoSector == "BANKACILIK" and secRoc > idxRoc + 0.75 and rsIndex > 1.0 ? math.max(_leadScore, 65) : _leadScore
_rsSmooth            = ta.ema(rsIndex, 10)
isSectorStrongEff = isSectorStrong or (_isMomStock and rsIndex > 2.0)
marketRiskOn = xu100Close > xu100Ema50
_xu100DayRet = (xu100Close - xu100Close[1]) / math.max(xu100Close[1], 0.001) * 100
_secDayRet   = (secClose - secClose[1]) / math.max(secClose[1], 0.001) * 100
_sectorEarlyFire = f_sectorEarlyFire(autoSector, _secDayRet, _xu100DayRet, secClose > secClose[1])
breadthWeak  = _xu100DayRet < -0.5 and _secDayRet < -0.3
marketRisk   = xu100Close < xu100Ema50 and _xbankBear and breadthWeak
marketRiskPenalty =
     marketRisk ? (
          autoSector == "SAVUNMA"     ? -1 :
          autoSector == "ENERJI"      ? -2 :
          autoSector == "SIGORTA"     ? -2 :
          autoSector == "KIMYA/TARIM" ? -2 :
          autoSector == "PERAKENDE"   ? -3 :
          autoSector == "BANKACILIK"  ? -3 :  // V89
          autoSector == "HOLDING"     ? (_isHoldingBank ? -6 : _isHoldingEnerji ? -4 : -5) :
          autoSector == "GYO"         ? -5 :
          autoSector == "HAVACILIK"   ? -2 : -4)
     : (not marketRiskOn and _xbankBear and not _isBankImmune) ? -2 : 0
_brentMitigation = (_isBrentSensitive and not na(_brentStrength) and _brentStrength > 0.5) ? 0.5 : 1.0
marketRiskScore  = math.round(marketRiskPenalty * _brentMitigation)
_mreThreshAdj    = marketRisk ? -1 : 0
sectorHeat =
     (_isBankGYO and _xbankFire) ? math.max(3, (rsSector > 1.0 ? 3 : rsSector > 0.0 ? 2 : 1)) :
     rsSector > 3.0 ? 3 : rsSector > 1.0 ? 2 : rsSector > 0.0 ? 1 : rsSector > -1.0 ? 0 : rsSector > -3.0 ? -2 : -4
isMomentum  = _atrPct > 1.8 and _rsSmooth > 2.0
isHeavy     = not isMomentum and dailyVolTL > 800000000 and _atrPct < 1.5
isDefensive = not isMomentum and not isHeavy and _atrPct < 1.0 and _rsSmooth < 1.5
isKurumsal  = not isMomentum and not isHeavy and not isDefensive and _atrPct > 0.8 and dailyVolTL > 300000000
_autoDNA    = isMomentum ? "MOMENTUM" : isHeavy ? "HEAVY" : isDefensive ? "DEFENSIVE" : "KURUMSAL"
_blendedDNA = dnaMode != "AUTO" ? dnaMode : (_autoDNA == autoSectorDNA) ? _autoDNA : autoSectorDNA
var string stableDNA    = "KURUMSAL"
var int    dnaSameCount = 0
if barstate.isconfirmed
    dnaSameCount := _blendedDNA == stableDNA ? dnaSameCount + 1 : 1
stableDNA := (dnaSameCount >= 3 or dnaMode != "AUTO") ? _blendedDNA : stableDNA
isMomentum  := stableDNA == "MOMENTUM"
isKurumsal  := stableDNA == "KURUMSAL"
isDefensive := stableDNA == "DEFENSIVE"
isHeavy     := stableDNA == "HEAVY"
adaptiveCooldown  = isMomentum ? math.max(cooldownBars - 2, 3) : isDefensive or isKurumsal ? cooldownBars + 2 : cooldownBars
adaptiveCooldown := _isBankGYO ? adaptiveCooldown + 2 : adaptiveCooldown
adaptiveCooldown := _isAkbnk   ? adaptiveCooldown + 3 : adaptiveCooldown
_srPivHigh = srEnabled ? ta.pivothigh(high, srLookback, srLookback) : na
_srPivLow  = srEnabled ? ta.pivotlow(low,  srLookback, srLookback) : na
var float _srRes1 = na
var float _srRes2 = na
var float _srSup1 = na
var float _srSup2 = na
var float _srResStrg1 = 0.0
var float _srSupStrg1 = 0.0
if not na(_srPivHigh)
    if na(_srRes1) or math.abs(_srPivHigh - _srRes1) / math.max(_srRes1,1) * 100 > srTolPct
        _srRes2 := _srRes1
        _srRes1 := _srPivHigh
        _srResStrg1 := relVol
    else
        _srResStrg1 := _srResStrg1 + relVol
if not na(_srPivLow)
    if na(_srSup1) or math.abs(_srPivLow - _srSup1) / math.max(_srSup1,1) * 100 > srTolPct
        _srSup2 := _srSup1
        _srSup1 := _srPivLow
        _srSupStrg1 := relVol
    else
        _srSupStrg1 := _srSupStrg1 + relVol
_srNearestRes     = (not na(_srRes1) and _srRes1 > close) ? _srRes1 :
     (not na(_srRes2) and _srRes2 > close) ? _srRes2 : float(na)
_srNearestSup     = (not na(_srSup1) and _srSup1 < close) ? _srSup1 :
     (not na(_srSup2) and _srSup2 < close) ? _srSup2 : float(na)
_srNearestResStrg = _srResStrg1
_srNearestSupStrg = _srSupStrg1
_srTolAbs = close * srTolPct / 100
_srAtResistance   = srEnabled and not na(_srNearestRes) and math.abs(close - _srNearestRes) <= _srTolAbs
_srAtSupport      = srEnabled and not na(_srNearestSup) and math.abs(close - _srNearestSup) <= _srTolAbs
_srBrokeResistance= srEnabled and not na(_srNearestRes) and close > _srNearestRes and close[1] <= _srNearestRes and volume > volAvg * srVolMult
_srBrokeSupportDn = srEnabled and not na(_srNearestSup) and close < _srNearestSup and close[1] >= _srNearestSup and volume > volAvg * srVolMult
_srResIsStrong    = _srNearestResStrg >= srMinTouch * 1.2
_srSupIsStrong    = _srNearestSupStrg >= srMinTouch * 1.2
volumeSpike = volume > volAvg * (isDefensive ? 1.15 : 1.3)
volumeHuge  = volume > volAvg * 2.5
volumeDry   = volume < volAvg * 0.60
upperWick   = high - math.max(open, close)
lowerWick   = math.min(open, close) - low
candleBody  = math.abs(close - open)
candleRange = high - low
_emaChopThresh = _isBankGYO ? 0.25 : 0.20
emaChopFilter  = math.abs(emaFastA - emaMidA) > atr * _emaChopThresh
_ema9_21gap    = math.abs(emaFast - emaMid) / math.max(close, 0.001)

_emaChopGap    = autoSector == "PERAKENDE" ? 0.002 : _isHolding ? 0.003 : 0.004

_atrVolThresh  = _isBankGYO ? 0.25 : 0.35

rangeCompression = _isBankGYO ?
     (math.abs(emaFastA - emaMidA) < atr * 0.25 and atr < atrAvg * 0.9 and rsIndex < 1.0) :
     (math.abs(emaFastA - emaMidA) < atr * 0.20 and atr < atrAvg * 0.85 and rsIndex < 0.5)
liqSweepUp =
     upperWick > candleBody * 1.8 and close < high - candleRange * 0.35 and
     high > ta.highest(high[1], 10) and volumeSpike
liqSweepDown =
     lowerWick > candleBody * 1.8 and close > low + candleRange * 0.35 and
     low < ta.lowest(low[1], 10)
lookback   = 15
prevHigh15 = ta.highest(high[1], lookback)
_fakeWick     = upperWick
_fakeBody     = math.max(candleBody, syminfo.mintick)

     autoSector == "ENERJI" and not _isBrentSensitive ? 1.8 :
     isKurumsal ? 2.2 :
     isHeavy    ? 1.7 : 1.3
fakeUpBar1    = (high > prevHigh15) and (close < prevHigh15) and (close < open) and (_fakeWick > _fakeBody * fakeWickRatio)
fakeUpBar2    = high[1] > prevHigh15[1] and close[1] < prevHigh15[1] and close[1] < open[1]
fakeWickTol   = isMomentum ? 1.05 : isHeavy ? 1.035 : 1.0
fakeVolumeFail= volume < volAvg * 0.72
_fakeVolThresh =
     autoSector == "ENERJI"      ? volAvg * 1.60 :
     autoSector == "SAVUNMA"     ? volAvg * 1.70 :
     autoSector == "BANKACILIK"  ? volAvg * 1.40 :
     autoSector == "PERAKENDE"   ? volAvg * 1.50 :
     autoSector == "GYO"         ? volAvg * 1.25 :
     autoSector == "SIGORTA"     ? volAvg * 1.25 :
     autoSector == "KIMYA/TARIM" ? volAvg * 1.10 : volAvg * 1.15

fakeBreakStrict = strictFake ?
     (isMomentum ?
          fakeUpBar1 and _fakeVolSpike and (high > prevHigh15 * fakeWickTol) :
          (fakeUpBar1 or fakeUpBar2) and (_fakeVolSpike or fakeVolumeFail))
     : (fakeUpBar1 and _fakeVolSpike)
pumpDump      = high > prevHigh15 * 1.05 and close < open * 0.98 and volumeHuge
_fakeEarlyVol = _isBrentSensitive ? volAvg * 1.9 : volAvg * 1.5
fakeEarlyTrap = high > ta.highest(high[1], 5) and close < emaFast and close < open and volume > _fakeEarlyVol and not volumeHuge
_astorFailure = _isEnerjiAlt and high > prevHigh15 and close < open and close < emaFastA and volume > volAvg * 1.1
_trendUpProxy = close > emaMidA and emaMidA > emaMidA[3]
fakeRisk  = (fakeBreakStrict and (close < emaMidA or not _trendUpProxy)) or
     pumpDump or liqSweepUp or
     (fakeEarlyTrap and close < emaMidA) or _astorFailure
fakeClean = not fakeRisk
var int lastFakeBar = na
if fakeRisk
    lastFakeBar := bar_index
_fakeCoolLen  = isStockLeadingSector ? 2 : 3
fakeCooldownOk = na(lastFakeBar) or (bar_index - lastFakeBar > _fakeCoolLen)
_buyVolFR  = close >= open ? volume : volume * math.max(close - low, 0) / math.max(high - low, syminfo.mintick)
_sellVolFR = close < open  ? volume : volume * math.max(high - close, 0) / math.max(high - low, syminfo.mintick)
_bankFakeRelax =
     autoSector == "BANKACILIK" and close > emaFastA and emaFastA > emaFastA[1] and
     _buyVolFR > _sellVolFR and relVol > 1.0 and rsSector > 0

var int _lastFakeMgros = na

    _lastFakeMgros := bar_index

_bqVol   = relVol > 1.3

_bqCount = (_bqVol?1:0) + (_bqBody?1:0) + (_bqClose?1:0) + (_bqRsi?1:0) + (_bqEma?1:0)
breakoutQualityOk = _bqCount >= 3
_minVolTLeff = autoSector == "PERAKENDE" ? minVolTL * 0.5 : minVolTL
liquidOk    = filterLowLiq ? (dailyVolTL >= _minVolTLeff) : true

isLimitDown  = filterLimit and (close <= close[1] * limitDivider)

// [CL002] ===================================================================== -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
atrExpansion = atr > atrAvg * 1.10

trendUp    = close > emaFastA and emaFastA > emaMidA and emaMidA > emaSlow and emaSlow > emaMain
trendDown  = close < emaFastA and emaFastA < emaMidA and emaMidA < emaSlow
// === MODUL 1 (kurumsal): REJIM ETIKETI - mevcut trendUp/trendDown'dan turetilir, yeni

regimeTag = trendUp ? "TREND" : trendDown ? "DUSUS" : "YATAY"

var string regimeTagConfirmed = regimeTag

aboveMain  = close > emaMain
rangeMode  = not trendUp and not trendDown
recovering = close > emaSlow and emaSlow > emaMain and not trendUp
mtfScore   = f_clamp(math.round((trendUp ? 25 : recovering ? 15 : 5) +
     (htfBull ? 25 : 0) + (htf2Bull ? 25 : 0) +
     (htfSlopeBull ? 15 : htfSlopeOk ? 8 : 0) + (rsIndex > 0 ? 10 : 0)), 0, 100)
var int trendStartBar = na

    trendStartBar := bar_index

    trendStartBar := na

     (trendAging or trendOld ? 10 : 0)), 0, 100)
panicSell =
     close < close[1] * 0.985 and volumeHuge and close < emaSlow and rsi < 35

     rsi > rsiLowerBand and not distribution and not panicSell and obvTrend
baseBuild     =

recoveryState = (recovering or (isKurumsal and low < emaMain and close > emaMain and close > low + (high-low)*0.65 and relVol > 1.35 and close > close[1]))
     and not panicSell
trendState    = trendUp and not distribution and not panicSell
_priorSwingLow = ta.lowest(low[3], 10)
recoveryMode   = aboveMain and close > emaFast and low > _priorSwingLow and not trendUp and not panicSell
_emaBreakRaw     = close < emaFastA
_defenseEMABreak = autoSector == "SAVUNMA" ? (_emaBreakRaw and _emaBreakRaw[1]) : _emaBreakRaw
var int _weakCount = 0

    _weakCount := (_defenseEMABreak and rsi < rsi[3]) ? _weakCount + 1 : math.max(_weakCount - 1, 0)

    _weakCount := 0
_weakThreshold = (autoSector == "SAVUNMA") ? 3 : 3
trendWeakening = trendState and _weakCount >= _weakThreshold and (obvFalling or volumeDry)
majorTrend    = close > emaMain and emaMain > emaMain[10]
severeBearish = close < emaMain and emaMid < emaSlow and emaSlow < emaMain
earlyAccumState =
     close > emaFastA and rsi > rsi[2] and relVol > 0.9 and obvTrend and
     not trendUp and not panicSell and aboveMain
marketState =
     panicSell      ? "PANIC SELL"   :
     distribution   ? "DISTRIBUTION" :
     trendWeakening ? "TREND ZYF "  :
     trendState     ? "TREND"        :
     pullbackState  ? "PULLBACK"     :
     recoveryState  ? "RECOVERY"     :
     earlyAccumState? "EARLY ACCUM"  :
     isSqueeze      ? "SQUEEZE"      :
     baseBuild      ? "BASE BUILD"   : "NEUTRAL"
_telStateBonus = _isTtkom and aboveMain and htfAlign >= 1 ? 0.08 : 0.0
stateScoreMult =
     panicSell       ? 0.55 :
     distribution    ? 0.70 :
     trendWeakening  ? 0.85 :
     trendState      ? 1.00 :
     pullbackState   ? 0.88 :
     recoveryState   ? (0.80 + _telStateBonus) :
     earlyAccumState ? 0.82 :
     baseBuild       ? 0.75 :
     (0.82 + _telStateBonus * 0.5)
noTradeBase = rangeMode and volumeDry and atr < atrAvg * (isDefensive ? 0.55 : 0.70)
_noTradePerakendeFix = autoSector == "PERAKENDE" and aboveMain and rsIndex > 0 and obvTrend
_noBankAccum =
     autoSector == "BANKACILIK" and close > emaFastA and rsi > rsi[1] and relVol > 0.9 and
     rsSector > -1.0 and not panicSell
_noBankStrong =
     autoSector == "BANKACILIK" and close > emaFastA and close > emaMidA and
     emaFastA > emaFastA[2] and emaFastA > emaFastA[1] and
     htfSlopeOk and rsi > 42 and not panicSell
_noTradeHoldingFix =
     _isHolding and close > emaFastA and emaFastA > emaFastA[1] and
     close > low + (high - low) * 0.55 and relVol > 0.9 and
     (aboveMain or close > emaMain * 0.97) and not panicSell
_noTradeTtkomFix =
     _isTtkom and close > emaFastA and
     (emaFastA > emaFastA[1] and emaFastA[1] > emaFastA[2]) and
     htfSlopeOk and not panicSell
_noTradeSavunmaFix =
     autoSector == "SAVUNMA" and close > emaFastA and rsi > rsi[2] and
     relVol > 0.5 and not panicSell
_noTradeSinaiFix =
     autoSector == "SINAI" and not panicSell and
     ((aboveMain and close > emaFastA and rsi > rsi[2] and relVol > 0.85) or
      (volumeHuge and rsi > rsi[1] and close > low + (high - low) * 0.60))
_noTradeEnerjifix =
     _isEnerjiAlt and (trendFresh or breakoutBar or (relVol > 1.5 and atrExpansion)) and
     close > emaFastA and not panicSell
_sectorNoTradeFix =
     _noTradePerakendeFix or _noBankAccum or _noBankStrong or _noTradeHoldingFix or
     _noTradeTtkomFix or _noTradeSavunmaFix or _noTradeSinaiFix or _noTradeEnerjifix
_emaChopReject = emaChopTight and relVol < 0.8 and math.abs(close - vwapVal) / math.max(close, syminfo.mintick) < 0.003
_holdingAboveMain = _isHolding and (aboveMain or close > emaMain * 0.95)
_kchol_chopBearBlock = false
_emaSpreadTight = _emaSqueeze and volumeDry and not breakoutBar and not squeezeBreakout
_noTradeCount = (_emaSpreadTight ? 1 : 0) + (noTradeBase ? 1 : 0) +
     (rangeCompression ? 1 : 0) + (not emaChopFilter ? 1 : 0) +
     (not atrVolFilter ? 1 : 0) +
     (_isHolding and _emaChopReject and not _holdingAboveMain ? 1 : 0) +
     (_kchol_chopBearBlock ? 1 : 0)
noTrade = (_noTradeCount >= 3 and not breakoutBar) and not _sectorNoTradeFix
_recoveryAbove =
     aboveMain or (close > emaSlow and emaSlow > emaSlow[2] and rsIndex > 0 and
     relVol > 1.1 and not rangeCompression)
var int _lastRCVBar = na
_barsSinceRCV = na(_lastRCVBar) ? 999 : bar_index - _lastRCVBar
allowRCV  = _barsSinceRCV > 6
rcvValid  = recoveryMode and close > emaFast and emaFast > emaMid and volume > volAvg * 1.1
rcvSignal = rcvValid and allowRCV
if rcvSignal
    _lastRCVBar := bar_index
_ema9Slope    = emaFast > emaFast[1] and emaFast[1] > emaFast[2]
_recentLow20  = ta.lowest(low, 20)
_barsSinceLow = ta.barssince(low <= _recentLow20 * 1.002)
_higherLow    = ta.lowest(low, 3) > ta.lowest(low[4], 4)
bankRecovery  = _isBankGYO and not na(_barsSinceLow) and _barsSinceLow <= 6 and
     _ema9Slope and _higherLow and relVol >= 1.0 and close > vwapVal and
     (close > emaFastA or close > emaMidA) and (not _isAkbnk or adxStrong)
if autoSector == "BANKACILIK" and bankRecovery and close > emaFastA and relVol > 0.9
    fakeRisk := false
stateFloor =
     isKurumsal and pullbackState and not panicSell ? (marketRiskOn ? 22 : 15) :
     isHeavy    and pullbackState and not panicSell ? (marketRiskOn ? 18 : 10) :
     isKurumsal and recoveryState                   ? (marketRiskOn ? 18 : 10) :
     isKurumsal and not panicSell                   ? (marketRiskOn ? 12 : 10) :
     isHeavy    and not panicSell                   ? (marketRiskOn ? 10 : 8)  :
     isDefensive and not panicSell                  ? (marketRiskOn ? 8 : 4)   :
     panicSell                                      ? 0 : marketRiskOn ? 8 : 0
stateFloor := _isBankGYO and not panicSell ? math.max(stateFloor,
     aboveMain ? (marketRiskOn ? 28 : 22) :
     (bankRecovery or recoveryMode) ? (marketRisk ? 30 : 18) : stateFloor) : stateFloor
stateFloor := _isEnerjiAlt and close > emaSlow and not panicSell ? math.max(stateFloor, 14) : stateFloor
stateFloor := autoSector == "PERAKENDE" ? (not aboveMain ? math.min(stateFloor,16) : not panicSell ? math.max(stateFloor,14) : stateFloor) : stateFloor
stateFloor := _isTtkom and not panicSell ? math.max(stateFloor, aboveMain and htfAlign >= 1 ? 20 : close > emaFastA and close > emaMidA ? 18 : stateFloor) : stateFloor
stateFloor := autoSector == "HAVACILIK" and not panicSell ? math.max(stateFloor, aboveMain ? 22 : close > emaSlow ? 18 : stateFloor) : stateFloor
stateFloor := _isHolding and not panicSell ? math.max(stateFloor, close > emaFastA and close > emaMidA and close > emaSlow ? 24 : close > emaFastA and close > emaMidA ? 22 : close > emaSlow ? 20 : aboveMain ? 16 : 14) : stateFloor
_leaderRsThreshold = isMomentum ? 1.5 : isHeavy ? 0.8 :
     (autoSector == "SAVUNMA" or autoSector == "SIGORTA") ? 0.6 : 0.7
_marketWeak      = not marketRiskOn or (xu100Close < xu100Close[3])
leaderMode       =
     _marketWeak and
     (rsSector > _leaderRsThreshold and isSectorStrongEff) and
     (rsIndex > _leaderRsThreshold and rsStrengthBonus) and
     (close > emaFastA and close > emaMidA and not trendWeakening and not distribution) and
     not panicSell and liquidOk and adxStrong
// [CL004] 17.09 GLOBAL SCOPE KUCULTME ("main body of script is too long" hatasi -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
f_scoreCore() =>
    scoreLeaderMode = leaderMode ? (autoSector == "SAVUNMA" ? 10 : 8) : 0
    scoreSectorLead = rsIndex>5 and rsSector>3 and trendUp and htfAlign==2?20:rsIndex>4 and rsSector>2 and trendUp?15:rsIndex>3 and rsSector>1.5 and trendUp?10:rsIndex>2 and rsSector>1?5:0
    scoreTrend     = trendUp ? 25 : (trendDown and isDefensive) ? -7 : trendDown ? -14 : recovering ? 11 : -5
    scoreAboveMain =
         aboveMain                                          ? 10 :
         (close > emaSlow and not aboveMain)                ? -4 :
         recoveryMode                                       ? -6 :
         (close < emaSlow and rsIndex > 0)                  ? -8 :
         (isDefensive and close > emaSlow * 0.97)           ? -6 :
         -14
    scoreHTF = autoSector == "BANKACILIK" ?
         (htfAlign == 2 ? 20 : htfAlign == 1 ? 13 : bankRecovery ? 5 : -2) :
         isDefensive ?
         (htfAlign == 2 ? 18 : htfAlign == 1 ? 10 : -3) :
         autoSector == "HAVACILIK" ?
         (htfAlign == 2 ? 20 : htfAlign == 1 ? 12 : htfAlign == 0 ? -2 : -5) :
         (htfAlign == 2 ? 20 : htfAlign == 1 ? 10 : htfAlign == 0 ? -3 : -6)
    scoreVwap      = close > vwapVal ? 10 : -10
    _rsXusinBoost  = (autoSector == "SAVUNMA") ? math.max(0, rsIndex) : 0
    _rsM = autoSector == "PERAKENDE" ? 0.80 : 1.0
    _rsRawVal = rsIndex > 5 ? 20 : rsIndex > 3 ? 15 : rsIndex > 2 ? 12 :
         rsIndex > 1 ? 8 : rsIndex > 0 ? 4 : rsIndex > -1 ? -4 : rsIndex > -2 ? -8 : -12
    scoreRsIndex = math.round(_rsRawVal * _rsM)
    scoreRsSector  = rsSector > 1 ? 10 : rsSector > 0 ? 5 : -5
    scoreSectorStr = sectorStrong ? 8 : -3
    scoreLeader    = isStockLeadingSector ? 10 : 0
    scoreSectorRot = isSectorStrongEff ? 5 : -2
    scoreDefenseBoost = (autoSector == "SAVUNMA" and rsIndex > 0) ?
         math.round(_rsXusinBoost * 2) + (volumeSpike ? 1 : 0) +
         (close > emaSlow ? 4 : 0) +
         (close > emaMid  ? 2 : 0)
         : (autoSector == "SAVUNMA" and close > emaSlow and rsi > rsi[2] ? 2 : 0)
    _xholdRoc      = ta.roc(nz(_xholdClose, close), 20)
    _holdingAlpha  = stkRoc - _xholdRoc
    scoreHoldingRS = _isHolding ? (_holdingAlpha > 3 ? 8 : _holdingAlpha > 1 ? 4 : _holdingAlpha > 0 ? 1 : -2) : 0
    scoreHoldingAbove =
         (_isHolding and close > emaMain and obvTrend)               ? 10 :
         (_isHolding and close > emaMain)                            ?  7 :
         (_isHolding and close > emaSlow and close > emaMain * 0.97) ?  4 :
         (_isHolding and close > emaSlow)                            ?  2 :
         (_isHolding and not aboveMain)                              ? -3 : 0
    _xbankRs     = not na(_xbankClose) ? (close/close[20]) / (_xbankClose/_xbankClose[20]) - 1 : 0
    scoreXbankRS = _isBankGYO ?
         (_xbankFire and _xbankRs > 0 ? 6 :
          _xbankStrong and _xbankRs > 0 ? 4 :
          _xbankRs > 0.02 ? 3 :
          _xbankRs > 0 ? 1 : -3) : 0
    scoreRecovery   = (rcvSignal and _isHolding) ? 5 : (rcvSignal and autoSector == "SINAI" and not trendUp) ? 1 : rcvSignal ? 2 : 0
    scoreSevereBear =
         severeBearish                              ? -8 :
         (not aboveMain and trendDown)              ? -5 :
         (not aboveMain and not recovering)         ? -3 : 0
    scoreMajorTrend = majorTrend ? (autoSector == "SAVUNMA" ? 4 : 2) : 0
    _isGoldSector = str.contains(_t,"TRALT") or str.contains(_t,"KOZAL") or autoSector == "KIMYA/TARIM"
    sectorWeight =
         autoSector == "SAVUNMA"     ? 1.25 :
         autoSector == "ENERJI"      ? 1.15 :
         _isGoldSector               ? 1.20 :
         autoSector == "PERAKENDE"   ? 1.10 :
         autoSector == "HAVACILIK"   ? 1.10 :
         autoSector == "SINAI"       ? 1.10 :
         autoSector == "BANKACILIK"  ? 1.08 :
         autoSector == "HOLDING"     ? 1.10 : 1.05
    _recentLow5 = ta.lowest(low, 5)
    _pullbackRecovery = _isEnerji and not _isBrentSensitive and close > emaFast and
         _ema9Slope and close > _recentLow5 * 1.001 and emaMid < emaSlow * 1.005 and
         not trendUp and aboveMain
    _brentPullback = _isBrentSensitive and close > emaFast and _ema9Slope and
         atr < atrAvg * 1.8 and not trendUp and aboveMain and not panicSell
    scorePullbackRec  = _pullbackRecovery ? 4 : _brentPullback ? 3 : 0
    scorePullbackRec := _isHoldingEnerji ? scorePullbackRec + 2 : scorePullbackRec
    scoreEnergyMomentum =
         (_isEnerji and not _isBrentSensitive and rsIndex > 2.0) ? 3 :
         (_isEnerji and not _isBrentSensitive and rsIndex > 0.0) ? 2 :
         (_isEnerji and rsSector > 1.0)                          ? 2 :
         (_isEnerji and rsSector > 0.0)                          ? 1 : 0
    _emaFullStack = trendUp and emaFastA>emaMidA and emaMidA>emaSlow and emaSlow>emaMain
    _emaStackBonus = _emaFullStack?(trendFresh and breakoutBar?20:trendFresh and relVol>1.3?15:trendFresh?10:not trendOld?6:0):0
    coreScore = scoreTrend + scoreAboveMain + scoreHTF + scoreVwap + scoreRsIndex +
         scoreRsSector + scoreSectorStr + scoreLeader + scoreSectorRot +
         energyRsBonus + marketRiskScore + sectorHeat + scoreLeaderMode +
         scoreEnergyMomentum + scoreDefenseBoost + scoreHoldingRS +
         scoreHoldingAbove + scorePullbackRec + scoreRecovery +
         scoreSevereBear + scoreMajorTrend + scoreXbankRS + brokerBonus +
         scoreSectorLead + _emaStackBonus + _tr05yBonus
    flowPositive = obv > ta.sma(obv, 20)  // V88: ta.cum ikinci hesab kaldrld
    instFlow  = mfiBull and obvTrend and flowPositive
    scoreFlow = flowPositive and mfiBull ? 14 : flowPositive ? 8 : mfiBull ? 5 : -5
    scoreInstF   = (instFlow and relVol > 1.3) ? 14 : instFlow ? 10 : 0
    _obvNewHigh    = obv >= ta.highest(obv, 20) and close < ta.highest(close, 20) * 0.99
    _obvDivergence = _obvNewHigh and not trendUp and not panicSell
    scoreObv = obvTrend and not hiddenDist and _obvDivergence ? 18 :
         obvTrend and not hiddenDist and relVol > 1.2 ? 12 :
         obvTrend and not hiddenDist ? 9 :
         hiddenDist ? -10 : -5
    scoreBreak = breakoutBar and relVol > 1.5 ? 18 : breakoutBar ? 14 : 0
    scoreSqz     = squeezeBreakout ? 15 : 0
    scoreVolDry  = volumeDry and rangeMode ? -10 : 0
    scoreRsBonus = rsStrengthBonus ? 12 : 0
    flowScore    = scoreFlow + scoreInstF + scoreObv + scoreBreak + scoreSqz + scoreVolDry + scoreRsBonus + goldBonus
    scoreFake = fakeClean ? 8 :
         (rsIndex > 4.0 and aboveMain) ? -3 :
         _isEnerjiAlt ? -6 : -8
    scoreRsiH    = healthyRsi ? 8 : (isKurumsal and deepWeak) ? 3 : 0
    scoreOverH   = overheated ? -20 : overExtended ? -15 : 0
    scoreExhaust = exhaustion or exhaustion2 ? -18 : 0
    scoreLiq     = liquidOk ? 0 : -50
    scorePump    = pumpDump ? -40 : 0
    scoreMktReg  = isDefensive ? (marketRiskOn?4:3) : isHeavy ? (marketRiskOn?6:-6) : (marketRiskOn?8:-12)
    scoreDnaB    = (isMomentum and trendUp and relVol>1.2) ? 6 :
         (isKurumsal and obvTrend and htfAlign==2) ? 6 :
         (isDefensive and rsi<55 and close>emaMidA) ? 4 :
         (isHeavy and breakoutBar) ? 8 : 0
    scoreTrendAge= trendOld ? -18 : trendAging ? -8 : trendMature ? -3 : 0
    scoreBankRev = (isKurumsal and low < emaMain and close > emaMain and
         close > low+(high-low)*0.65 and relVol > 1.35) ? 10 : (_isHolding and recoveryMode ? 4 : 0)
    scoreHeavyR  = (isHeavy and close > emaFastA and low[1] < emaSlow) ? 7 : 0
    scoreStatB = trendState?10 : pullbackState?5 : recoveryState?7 :
         baseBuild?5 : distribution?-18 : panicSell?-35 : -2
    scoreLiqSwp  = liqSweepUp ? -12 : 0
    scoreAdxPow  = adxPowerful ? 6 : 0
    _trendDominance = f_trendDominance(close, emaFastA, emaMidA, emaSlow, adxVal, trendWeakening, panicSell)
    scoreTrendDominance = _trendDominance ? 5 : 0
    scoreSectorEarlyFire = _sectorEarlyFire ? 8 : 0
    scoreBankRecFinal = (bankRecovery and not panicSell) ?
         (_isAkbnk ? 3 : 6) : 0
    riskScore = scoreFake + scoreRsiH + scoreOverH + scoreExhaust + scoreLiq +
         scorePump + scoreMktReg + scoreDnaB + scoreTrendAge + scoreBankRev +
         scoreHeavyR + scoreStatB + scoreLiqSwp + scoreAdxPow + scoreBankRecFinal
    _isGoldStock2 = str.contains(_t,"TRALT") or str.contains(_t,"KOZAL") or str.contains(_t,"GLDTR")
    coreW = (_isGoldStock2 or isMomentum) ? 0.42 : isKurumsal ? 0.45 : isDefensive ? 0.42 : 0.43
    _perakFlowBonus = autoSector == "PERAKENDE" ? 0.08 : 0.0
    _goldFlowBonus  = _isGoldStock2 ? 0.05 : 0.0
    flowW = (isMomentum ? 0.40 : isKurumsal ? 0.30 : isDefensive ? 0.30 : 0.35) + _perakFlowBonus + _goldFlowBonus
    riskW = 1.0 - coreW - flowW
    healthyPullback = close > emaMidA and low <= emaFastA and close > open and relVol < 1.2 and rsi > 50
    scoreMultiplier = dnaMode != "AUTO" ? (isMomentum?1.0:isKurumsal?0.95:isHeavy?0.90:1.0) : autoScoreMult
    scoreCalcRaw = math.round(
         (coreScore*coreW + flowScore*flowW + riskScore*riskW) *
         scoreMultiplier * stateScoreMult * sectorWeight) + (healthyPullback ? 6:0) +
         scoreTrendDominance + scoreSectorEarlyFire
    [scoreCalcRaw, scoreTrend, scoreRsIndex, scoreSectorLead, scoreFlow, scoreObv, scoreInstF, _trendDominance, healthyPullback]
[scoreCalcRaw, scoreTrend, scoreRsIndex, scoreSectorLead, scoreFlow, scoreObv, scoreInstF, _trendDominance, healthyPullback] = f_scoreCore()
scoreCeiling = isMomentum ? 96 : isHeavy ? 92 : 90
_gapPct = close[1] > 0 ? (open - close[1]) / close[1] * 100 : 0.0
_gapMomentum = _gapPct > 1.2 and relVol > 1.8 and atrExpansion and close > open
_momentumBoost = volumeHuge or _gapMomentum or (relVol > 2.0 and atrExpansion and rsIndex > 3.0)
scoreCeiling  := _momentumBoost ? math.min(scoreCeiling + 6, 99) : scoreCeiling
score        = f_clamp(scoreCalcRaw, stateFloor, scoreCeiling)
if recoveryState and rsIndex > 0 and close > emaFastA and not panicSell
    score := math.max(score, 42)
if recoveryState and _isMomStock and rsIndex > 1.0 and close > emaFastA
    score := math.max(score, 48)
if autoSector == "SAVUNMA" and recoveryState and close > emaSlow and rsIndex > 0 and not panicSell
    score := math.max(score, 44)
if autoSector == "PERAKENDE" and recoveryState and aboveMain and close > emaFastA and rsIndex > 0
    score := math.max(score, 42)
if autoSector == "SAVUNMA" and aboveMain and not panicSell
    score := math.max(score, 25)
if autoSector == "SAVUNMA" and aboveMain and close > emaSlow and not panicSell
    score := math.max(score, 32)
if autoSector == "PERAKENDE" and aboveMain and close > emaFastA and obvTrend and rsIndex > -1.0 and not panicSell
    score := math.max(score, 30)
if autoSector == "BANKACILIK" and close > emaSlow and not panicSell and (_xbankStrong or _xbankFire)
    score := math.max(score, 38)
_prepScore = 0
_bankPrep    = autoSector == "BANKACILIK" and close > emaFastA and
     emaFastA > emaFastA[1] and (obv > obv[3]) and
     relVol > 0.8 and rsi > rsi[1] and rsSector > -1.0 and not panicSell
_defPrep     = autoSector == "SAVUNMA" and close > emaFastA and
     rsi > rsi[2] and relVol > 0.9 and rsIndex > -1.5 and not panicSell
_holdPrep    = _isHolding and close > emaFastA and emaFastA > emaFastA[1] and
     emaMidA > emaMidA[3] and close > vwapVal and rsSector > 0 and not panicSell
_sinaiPrep   = autoSector == "SINAI" and close > emaFastA and
     rsi > rsi[1] and relVol > 0.9 and rsIndex > -1.0 and not panicSell
_enerjiPrep  = _isEnerji and close > emaMidA and close > vwapVal and
     (obv > obv[3]) and not panicSell
_retailPrep  = autoSector == "PERAKENDE" and aboveMain and close > emaFastA and
     relVol > 1.0 and obvTrend and rsSector > 0 and not panicSell
_telPrep     = _isTtkom and close > emaMidA and emaFastA > emaMidA and
     emaFastA > emaFastA[1] and rsi > 50 and rsSector > 0 and not panicSell
_dipPrep     = close > low + (high-low)*0.60 and relVol > 1.0 and
     rsi > rsi[1] and close > close[1] and rsIndex > -3.0 and
     not panicSell and aboveMain
_prepScore := _prepScore +
     (_bankPrep  ? 4 : 0) +
     (_bankPrep and bankRecovery ? 2 : 0) +
     (_defPrep   ? (close > emaSlow ? 3 : 2) : 0) +
     (_holdPrep  ? (close > emaSlow ? 4 : 3) : 0) +
     (_sinaiPrep ? 2 : 0) +
     (_enerjiPrep ? 3 : 0) +
     (_retailPrep ? 3 : 0) +
     (_telPrep   ? 3 : 0) +
     (_dipPrep   ? (autoSector == "SAVUNMA" or _isEnerjiAlt ? 5 : 3) : 0)
_prepScoreCap = isMomentum ? 10 : isDefensive ? 6 :
     (_isBankGYO and _xbankFire) ? 8 :
     _isBankGYO ? 6 :
     _isHolding ? 6 : 8
_prepScore := math.min(_prepScore, _prepScoreCap)
var int   _prep5Score      = 0
var bool  _prep5Trend      = false
var bool  _prep5CVD        = false
var bool  _prep5SMC        = false
var bool  _prep5Volume     = false
var bool  _prep5EMA        = false
var string _prep5Txt       = "BEKLE"
var string _prep5MissingTxt = ""
_prep5Clr = color.rgb(120, 60, 60)
if earlyAccumState and _prepScore >= 4
    marketState := "PREP HAZIR"
else if earlyAccumState and _prepScore >= 2
    marketState := "IZLE/HAZIRLAN"
_isLeaderStock = _isMomStock or
     (rsIndex > 3.0 and rsSector > 1.5 and aboveMain) or
     (leaderMode and aboveMain)
_penaltyMult = _isLeaderStock ? 0.70 : 1.0
_ema9Lost = close < emaFast and emaFast < emaFast[1] and emaFast[1] < emaFast[2]
if _ema9Lost and not panicSell
    score := math.max(0, score - math.round(5 * _penaltyMult))
_bearShock = close < open and (open - close) > atr * 1.2 and relVol > 1.0
if _bearShock and not recoveryState
    score := math.max(0, score - math.round(4 * _penaltyMult))
_falseRecovery = recovering and (close < emaFast or close < emaMid)
if _falseRecovery and not panicSell
    score := math.max(0, score - math.round(3 * _penaltyMult))
_astorPenalty = 0
if _isEnerjiAlt and close < emaMidA
    _astorPenalty := _astorPenalty + 6
if _isEnerjiAlt and close < emaSlow
    _astorPenalty := _astorPenalty + 4
if _isEnerjiAlt and trendDown
    _astorPenalty := _astorPenalty + 3
_failedBreakout = _isEnerjiAlt and high > prevHigh15 and close < prevHigh15 and
     close < open * 0.995 and volume > volAvg * 1.2
if _failedBreakout
    _astorPenalty := _astorPenalty + 5
_astorExhaustion = _isEnerjiAlt and rsi > 78 and rsi < rsi[1] and relVol < 1.0 and obvFalling
if _astorExhaustion
    _astorPenalty := _astorPenalty + 4
if _isEnerjiAlt and liqSweepUp
    _astorPenalty := _astorPenalty + 4
_astorPenalty := math.min(_astorPenalty, 6)
if _isEnerjiAlt and _astorPenalty > 0
    score := math.max(0, score - _astorPenalty)
if autoSector == "PERAKENDE" and aboveMain and close > emaFastA and (emaFastA > emaFastA[2]) and
     rsi > 42 and rsi > rsi[2] and close > close[1] and rsIndex > -1.0 and
     rsSector > 0 and score >= 30
    score := math.min(score + 3, scoreCeiling)
score := f_clamp(score, stateFloor, scoreCeiling)
var float _scoreRamped = na
_rampStep = isMomentum ? 6.0 : isDefensive ? 1.0 : isHeavy ? 3.0 : 2.0
_scoreRamped := na(_scoreRamped) or _momentumBoost ? float(score) : score > _scoreRamped ?
     math.min(float(score), _scoreRamped + _rampStep) : float(score)
score := math.round(_scoreRamped)
score := f_clamp(score, stateFloor, scoreCeiling)
_xbankChg5 = not na(_xbankClose[5]) and _xbankClose[5] != 0 ? (_xbankClose - _xbankClose[5]) / _xbankClose[5] * 100 : 0.0
_xbankChg1 = not na(_xbankClose[1]) and _xbankClose[1] != 0 ? (_xbankClose - _xbankClose[1]) / _xbankClose[1] * 100 : 0.0
_xu100Chg5 = not na(xu100Close[5]) and xu100Close[5] != 0 ? (xu100Close - xu100Close[5]) / xu100Close[5] * 100 : 0.0
_xu100Chg1 = not na(xu100Close[1]) and xu100Close[1] != 0 ? (xu100Close - xu100Close[1]) / xu100Close[1] * 100 : 0.0
_brentChg5 = not na(_brentClose) and not na(_brentClose[5]) and _brentClose[5] != 0 ? (_brentClose - _brentClose[5]) / _brentClose[5] * 100 : 0.0
_xholdChg5 = not na(_xholdClose) and not na(_xholdClose[5]) and _xholdClose[5] != 0 ? (_xholdClose - _xholdClose[5]) / _xholdClose[5] * 100 : 0.0
_xbankEarlyFire := _xbankChg1 > 3.0 and _xbankChg1 > _xu100Chg1 + 2.0 and volume > volAvg * 1.3
_isRiskOn   = marketRiskOn and xu100Close > xu100Close[3]
_isBankRot  = _xbankChg5  > (_xu100Chg5 + 2.0) or _xbankEarlyFire

     (htfAlign == 2       ? 2 : htfAlign == 1 ? 1 : 0) +

     tradeGrade == "A+" ? 1.0 :
