     _retailDip  ? "PERAKENDE DIP" :
     _telDip     ? "ILETISIM DIP"  :
     _genericDip ? "EMA200 DIP"    : ""
earlyTurnSignal =
     (accumEngine_signal or dipHunter_any) and _dipRsiDonumu and fakeClean and
     fakeCooldownOk and sessionOk and liquidOk and not panicSell and
     not isLimitZone and canBuy and scoreSmoothed >= 30 and
     (autoSector != "SAVUNMA" or relVol > 1.0)
if _strongAccum
    score := math.min(score + 8, scoreCeiling)
else if _accumTolerance
    score := math.min(score + 4, scoreCeiling)
if dipHunter_any
    score := math.min(score + 6, scoreCeiling)
if dipHunter_any
    score := math.max(score, 35)
if _strongAccum
    score := math.max(score, 30)
score := f_clamp(score, stateFloor, scoreCeiling)
scoreSmoothed := ta.ema(score, scoreSmLen)
var float _mfe        = 0.0
var float _mae        = 0.0
var int   _bt_total   = 0
var int   _bt_wins    = 0
var float _bt_sumW    = 0.0
var float _bt_sumL    = 0.0
var float _bt_lastPnL = 0.0
var float _bt_peak    = 0.0
var float _bt_maxDD   = 0.0
var float _bt_cumPnL  = 0.0
var bool  _posOpen         = false
var float _entryPrice      = na
// 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi, "stop kronolojisi" P0): giris barinin
// index'i - _stopHit'in AYNI bar icinde geriye-donuk tetiklenmesini
// onlemek icin (asagida kullanilacak).
var int   _entryBar        = na
// 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi, "3 kucuk duzeltme" #2): stop kirilma
// aninda latch edilecek degerler - _stopPrice temizlendikten SONRA bile
// webhook'un o barda DOGRU seviyeyi gosterebilmesi icin.
var float _lastStopTrigger = na
var float _lastStopFill    = na
var float _stopPrice       = na
var float _tradeEntryForTP = na
// 17.09 TP DONDURMA DUZELTMESI (dış kurul incelemesi, kritik bulgu #2):
// _tradeEntryForTP (giris fiyati) donuyordu ama _tp1Level/_tp2Level
// formulunde kullanilan atr HALA CANLI/guncel bardi - TP1/TP2 hedefleri
// ATR degistikce kayiyordu (ATR yukselince uzaklasiyor, dusunce
// yaklasiyor), "dondurulmus" gorunen ama aslinda YARIM dondurulmus bir
// hedefti. _entryAtr eklendi - giriste ATR de donuyor.
var float _entryAtr        = na
var bool _fBankEng    = false
var bool _fRetailEng  = false
var bool _fEnergyEng  = false
var bool _fSinaiEng   = false
var bool _fDefansEng  = false
var bool _fHoldingEng = false
var bool _fTtkomEng   = false
var bool _fElite      = false
var bool _fReal       = false
var bool _fEarlyMom   = false
var bool _fLiderAvci  = false
var bool _fDipIzleme  = false
var bool _fDipHunter  = false
var bool _fEarlyTurn  = false
var bool _fAccum      = false
var bool _fSMC        = false
baseCondBuy = fakeClean and liquidOk and not exhaustion and not exhaustion2 and not pumpDump and
     sessionOk and not noTrade and not hiddenDist and fakeCooldownOk and
     not panicSell and not distribution and not isLimitZone and
     (isSectorStrongEff or isStockLeadingSector or rsIndex > 1.5) and
     marketState != "TREND ZYF " and not liqSweepUp and
     not (marketRisk and not _isBankImmune and not leaderMode)
_riskOffPenalty = (not marketRiskOn) ? 5 : 0
_earlyLeaderDiscount = _sectorEarlyFire and _trendDominance ? 4 : _sectorEarlyFire or _trendDominance ? 2 : 0
_earlyBuyThresh = ((scoreSectorLead > 0 or smcBullSignal or leaderMode) ? 62 : 66) +
     _riskOffPenalty - _earlyLeaderDiscount
earlyBuy = baseCondBuy and canBuy and not _posOpen and not (mfi > 80) and
     scoreSmoothed >= _earlyBuyThresh and scoreSmoothed < 73 and
     close > emaFastA and close > vwapVal and
     relVol > 1.1 and rsi > 48 and htfSlopeOk and
     marketState != "DISTRIBUTION" and marketState != "PANIC SELL"
_momTrendOk       = trendUp or recoveryState or _recoveryAbove
_earlyMomBoost    = _isMomStock and _recoveryAbove and rsIndex > 2.0 and
     _momTrendOk and not panicSell and atr > atrAvg * 0.8
earlyBuyMom = _earlyMomBoost and canBuy and not _posOpen and not (mfi > 80) and
     scoreSmoothed >= 52 and scoreSmoothed < 73 and
     close > emaFastA and close > vwapVal and
     relVol > 1.1 and rsi > 50 and htfSlopeOk and
     fakeClean and fakeCooldownOk and sessionOk and liquidOk and
     not noTrade and not isLimitZone and not panicSell
realBuy = baseCondBuy and canBuy and not _posOpen and not (mfi > 80) and
     scoreSmoothed >= 73 and scoreSmoothed < 88 and
     close > emaFastA and close > vwapVal and
     volumeSpike and htfAlign >= 1 and not htfBear and not htf2Bear and
     emaSlow > emaMain and htfSlopeOk and
     (marketState == "TREND" or marketState == "PULLBACK" or marketState == "RECOVERY")
eliteThreshold = isMomentum ? 80 : isKurumsal ? 78 : isHeavy ? 76 : 76
eliteBuy = baseCondBuy and canBuy and not _posOpen and
     scoreSmoothed >= eliteThreshold and
     close > emaFastA and close > vwapVal and
     htfAlign >= 1 and rsIndex > 1.2 and obvTrend and
     (scoreFlow > 15 or (scoreFlow > 8 and relVol > 1.25)) and
     not htfBear and emaSlow > emaMain and htfSlopeBull and
     (marketState == "TREND" or marketState == "RECOVERY")
lastEliteOrReal = ta.barssince(eliteBuy or realBuy)
contCooldownOk  = na(lastEliteOrReal) or lastEliteOrReal > adaptiveCooldown * 2
barsSinceTrendUp = ta.barssince(trendUp and not trendUp[1])
trendContBarsOk  = na(barsSinceTrendUp) ? false : barsSinceTrendUp <= 15
trendContinuation =
     trendUp and (trendFresh or contCooldownOk or trendContBarsOk) and
     close > emaFastA and emaFastA > emaMidA and close > emaMain and
     relVol > 1.05 and rsi > 52 and obvTrend and scoreSmoothed > 60 and
     fakeClean and fakeCooldownOk and sessionOk and liquidOk and
     not _posOpen and not isLimitZone and marketState == "TREND" and
     htfAlign >= 1 and htfSlopeOk and breakoutQualityOk
emaFastCross = close > emaFastA and close[1] < emaFastA[1]
reEntry =
     not _posOpen and scoreSmoothed > 65 and emaFastCross and relVol > 1.1 and
     fakeClean and fakeCooldownOk and sessionOk and close > emaSlow and
     emaSlow > emaMain and not exhaustion and not exhaustion2 and liquidOk and
     not panicSell and not distribution and not isLimitZone and
     not htfBear and not htf2Bear and not noTrade
_viopConflict := _viopEnabled and not na(_viopClose) and _viopShort and (eliteBuy or realBuy or bankEngine_any or holdingEngine_any or energyEngine_any)
_viopConfirm  := _viopEnabled and not na(_viopClose) and _viopLong  and (eliteBuy or realBuy or bankEngine_any or holdingEngine_any)
viopBonus      = not _viopEnabled ? 0 : _viopConfirm ? 3 : _viopConflict ? -3 : _viopBullish and _viopBasis > 1.0 ? 2 : _viopBullish ? 1 : _viopBearish and _viopBasis < -1.0 ? -2 : _viopBearish ? -1 : 0
scoreSmoothed := ta.ema(score + viopBonus, scoreSmLen)
scoreSmoothed := f_clamp(scoreSmoothed, float(stateFloor), float(scoreCeiling))
if (eliteBuy or realBuy or trendContinuation or reEntry or earlyBuy or earlyBuyMom or
     (liderAvci and not _posOpen) or bankEngine_any or retailEngine_signal or
     energyEngine_any or sinaiEngine_any or defansEngine_any or holdingEngine_any or
     ttkomEngine_signal or earlyTurnSignal or dipHunter_any) and not _posOpen
    _posOpen         := true
    _entryPrice      := close
    _entryBar        := bar_index
    _stopPrice       := gbmEnabled and not na(_gbmStopFinal) ?
         _gbmStopFinal : close - atr * slMult1
    _tradeEntryForTP := close
    _entryAtr        := atr
    _mfe := 0.0
    _mae := 0.0
_felRef = _posOpen ? _entryPrice : close   // [V163-3] felaket siniri referansi (-%5 / -%8)
_pnlPct = not na(_entryPrice) and _entryPrice > 0 ?
     (close - _entryPrice) / _entryPrice * 100 : 0.0
if _posOpen and not na(_entryPrice)
    _mfe := math.max(_mfe, _pnlPct)
    _mae := math.min(_mae, _pnlPct)
_isLeaderHold = rsIndex > 3.0 and rsSector > 1.5 and not trendWeakening
_trailBase    = _isLeaderHold ? trailMult * 1.2 : trailMult
trailMultActive =
     (_pnlPct > 15 and (trendWeakening or obvFalling)) ? _trailBase * 0.5 :
     (_pnlPct > 10 and not trendUp)                    ? _trailBase * 0.65 :
     (_pnlPct > 5)                                      ? _trailBase * 0.8  :
     (trendOld and close < close[3] and rsi < rsi[3] and obvFalling) ? _trailBase * 0.7 :
     _trailBase
if _posOpen
    _stopPrice := math.max(_stopPrice, close - atr * trailMultActive)
_tp1Level = _posOpen and not na(_tradeEntryForTP) and not na(_entryAtr) ?
     _tradeEntryForTP + _entryAtr * (tpMult1 * 0.5) : na
_tp2Level = _posOpen and not na(_tradeEntryForTP) and not na(_entryAtr) ?
     _tradeEntryForTP + _entryAtr * tpMult1 : na
partialTPZone  = _posOpen and not na(_tp1Level) and close >= _tp1Level and close[1] < _tp1Level
partialTP2Zone = _posOpen and not na(_tp2Level) and close >= _tp2Level and close[1] < _tp2Level
if _posOpen and partialTPZone and not na(_entryPrice)
    _stopPrice := math.max(_stopPrice, _entryPrice)
if _posOpen and partialTP2Zone and not na(_tp1Level)
    _stopPrice := math.max(_stopPrice, _tp1Level)
if _posOpen and _pnlPct > 3.0 and not na(emaMidA)
    _stopPrice := math.max(_stopPrice, emaMidA * 0.998)
if _posOpen and _pnlPct > 5.0 and not na(emaFastA)
    _stopPrice := math.max(_stopPrice, emaFastA * 0.997)
if _posOpen and _pnlPct > 8.0 and not na(emaFastA)
    _stopPrice := math.max(_stopPrice, emaFastA * 0.995)
_intradayReversal = _posOpen and close < open and (open - close) > atr * 1.5 and
     relVol > 1.2 and not recoveryState
if _intradayReversal
    _stopPrice := math.max(_stopPrice, close - atr * _trailBase * 0.5)
_fBankEng    := bankEngine_any
_fRetailEng  := retailEngine_any
_fEnergyEng  := energyEngine_any
_fSinaiEng   := sinaiEngine_any
_fDefansEng  := defansEngine_any
_fHoldingEng := holdingEngine_any
_fTtkomEng   := ttkomEngine_signal
_fElite      := eliteBuy
_fReal       := realBuy
_fEarlyMom   := earlyBuyMom
_fLiderAvci  := liderAvci
_fDipIzleme  := _dipIzleme
_fDipHunter  := dipHunter_any
_fEarlyTurn  := earlyTurnSignal
_fAccum      := accumEngine_signal
_fSMC        := smcBullSignal
velocityLossThresh = isKurumsal ? 1.3 : isHeavy ? 1.35 : 1.5
velocityLoss = close < close[1] and relVol > velocityLossThresh and rsi < rsi[1] and close < emaFastA
panicExit        = panicSell and _posOpen
distributionExit = distribution and close < emaMidA and _posOpen
_softCount =
     (scoreSmoothed < 30 and close < emaMidA and marketState != "PULLBACK" ? 1 : 0) +
     (fakeRisk and close < emaSlow ? 1 : 0) +
     (exhaustion and close < emaFastA ? 1 : 0) +
     (velocityLoss and marketState != "PULLBACK" ? 1 : 0) +
     (trendWeakening and close < emaFastA ? 1 : 0) +
     (panicSell ? 2 : 0) +
     (distribution and close < emaMidA ? 1 : 0)
earlyExit =
     _posOpen and trendWeakening and obvFalling and close < emaFastA and
     not pullbackState and not liqSweepDown
_hardExitNew =
     _posOpen and close < emaMidA and rsi < rsi[1] and rsi < 50 and
     not pullbackState and not liqSweepDown
_softCountFinal = _softCount + (earlyExit ? 1 : 0) + (_hardExitNew ? 1 : 0)
_holdingPullbackTol = (_isHolding and pullbackState) ? 1 : 0
_sectorTolerance    = (_isHolding or _isHeavySec or autoSector == "HAVACILIK") ? 1 : 0
_leaderHoldTol = (rsIndex > 3.0 and rsSector > 1.5 and trendUp and not trendWeakening) ? 2 : 0
_savunmaTol         = (autoSector == "SAVUNMA" and pullbackState) ? 2 : 0
_energyTol          = _isBrentSensitive ? 2 : 0
_retailHold         = (_isDefSec and trendUp and aboveMain) ? 1 : 0
_airlineTol         = (autoSector == "HAVACILIK") ? 1 : 0
_bankExtraTol       = (_isBankGYO or _isHolding) and not marketRisk ? 1 : 0
softExitThresh = math.max(1,
     (_isDefSec ? 0 : _isBankGYO ? 2 : 2) + _mreThreshAdj +
     _sectorTolerance + _holdingPullbackTol + _savunmaTol +
     _energyTol + _retailHold + _airlineTol + _bankExtraTol +
     _leaderHoldTol)
softExitRaw = canExit and _softCountFinal >= softExitThresh
softExit    = panicExit or distributionExit or softExitRaw or (earlyExit and canExit)
var int lastExitSignalBar = na
exitCooldownOk = na(lastExitSignalBar) or (bar_index - lastExitSignalBar > adaptiveCooldown + 2)
exitRisk = panicExit or distributionExit or (softExit and exitCooldownOk)
if exitRisk
    lastExitSignalBar := bar_index
hardExitRaw =
     _posOpen and (
          (close < emaMidA and close < emaFastA and rsi < 45 and
           marketState != "PULLBACK" and not liqSweepDown) or
          (trendDown and rsIndex < -4) or pumpDump or panicSell)
climaxExit   = _posOpen and volumeHuge and close < open and rsi > rsiUpperBand and
     upperWick > candleBody * 0.8 and not pullbackState
vwapLoseExit = _posOpen and close < vwapVal and close[1] >= vwapVal[1] and
     relVol > 1.1 and rsi < 50 and not pullbackState and not recoveryState
emaCrossDown = _posOpen and emaFastA < emaMidA and emaFastA[1] >= emaMidA[1] and not pullbackState
trailAccel   = _posOpen and trendOld and close < close[3] and rsi < rsi[3] and obvFalling
newExitAny   = (climaxExit or vwapLoseExit or emaCrossDown) and exitCooldownOk
exitRiskFull = exitRisk or (newExitAny and _posOpen)
// [CL006] 17.09 GERCEK STOP DUZELTMESI (dış kurul incelemesi, kritik bulgu #1 - -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
_prevStopPrice = _stopPrice[1]
_stopHit = _posOpen and bar_index > _entryBar and not na(_prevStopPrice) and low <= _prevStopPrice
// GERCEKLESME FIYATI: gap-asagi durumunda open'dan, degilse stop
// seviyesinden cikildigi varsayilir (gercek bir stop emrinin calisma
// mantigi) - kapanis fiyatindan HESAPLANMAZ (eskiden oyleydi, WR/PF/
// Kelly'yi olduğundan iyi gosterebiliyordu).
_stopFill = open < _prevStopPrice ? open : _prevStopPrice
_stopPnlPct = not na(_entryPrice) and _entryPrice > 0 and not na(_stopFill) ?
     (_stopFill - _entryPrice) / _entryPrice * 100 : 0.0
// 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi, madde 5): _stopHit ic pozisyonu kapatiyordu
// ama AYRI bir alarma baglanmamisti (CORE_CIKIS ayri bir kosula - exitRiskFull/
// hardExitRaw - bagli, GERCEK STOP KIRILDI ise manuel gercekStopSeviye
// girdisine bagli - IKISI DE _stopHit'ten BAGIMSIZ). Kenar-tetikli olay
// eklendi, asagida ayri bir alertcondition ile kullaniliyor - MEVCUT
// CORE/GERCEK STOP alarmlarina DOKUNULMADI (davranis degisikligi riski
// almadan, yalniz EKSIK olan gorunurluk eklendi).
_stopHitEvent = _stopHit and not _stopHit[1]
exitTypeStr =
     _stopHit         ? "STOP KIRILDI "  :
     hardExitRaw      ? "ACL IKI "  :
     panicExit        ? "PANK IKI " :
     distributionExit ? "DAITIM "      :
     climaxExit       ? "CLMAX EXIT "  :
     vwapLoseExit     ? "VWAP KAYBI "   :
     emaCrossDown     ? "EMA CROSS "    :
     trailAccel       ? "TRAIL HIZLI "  :
     exitRisk         ? "RSK/IKI "   : ""
if (hardExitRaw or softExit or newExitAny or _stopHit) and _posOpen
    // 17.09: stop-kirilmali cikislarda kapanis fiyati DEGIL, _stopPnlPct
    // (gap-farkinda gercekleme fiyati) kullaniliyor - diger cikis
    // turlerinde (hardExitRaw/softExit/newExitAny, bunlar zaten kapanis-
    // teyitli sinyaller) eskisi gibi _pnlPct kaliyor.
    _exitPnlPct = _stopHit ? _stopPnlPct : _pnlPct
    // 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi, "3 kucuk duzeltme" #2 - dogrulandi):
    // _stopPrice bu blokta na'ya cekiliyor, ama webhook'un _wStop atamasi
    // (satir ~3895) SONRA calisiyor - stop kirilma barinin TAM KENDISINDE
    // webhook yanlislikla ADAY stop'u (_planStop) gosterebiliyordu, gercek
    // kirilan seviyeyi degil. _lastStopTrigger/_lastStopFill, temizlenmeden
    // ONCE burada latch ediliyor.
    if _stopHit
        _lastStopTrigger := _stopPrice
        _lastStopFill    := _stopFill
    _posOpen         := false
    _entryPrice      := na
    _entryBar        := na
    _stopPrice       := na
    _tradeEntryForTP := na
    _entryAtr        := na
    _bt_lastPnL := _exitPnlPct
    _bt_total   := _bt_total + 1
    if _exitPnlPct > 0
        _bt_wins := _bt_wins + 1
        _bt_sumW := _bt_sumW + _exitPnlPct
    else
        _bt_sumL := _bt_sumL + math.abs(_exitPnlPct)
_bt_cumPnL  := _bt_sumW - _bt_sumL
_bt_peak    := math.max(_bt_peak, _bt_cumPnL)
_bt_maxDD   := _bt_peak > 0 ? math.min(_bt_maxDD, _bt_cumPnL - _bt_peak) : _bt_maxDD
// 08.08 DUZELTME: ayni PF=0 belirsizligi (bkz. f_v112Validation)
var string _smState     = "NEUTRAL"
var int    _smStateBars = 0
var int    _smWatchBars = 0
var int    _smPrepBars  = 0
var float  _smEntryPrice = na
var int    _smRegress   = 0
_smMinWatch = isMomentum ? 2 : _isTtkom ? 2 : _isBankGYO ? 2 : isDefensive ? 4 : _isHolding ? 3 : 3
_smMinPrep  = isMomentum ? 2 : isDefensive ? 3 : _isHolding ? 3 : 2
_smSavunmaSignal =
     autoSector == "SAVUNMA" and close > emaSlow and
     (obvTrend or rsi > rsi[2]) and not panicSell
_smHoldingSignal =
     _isHolding and close > emaSlow and
     (cvdRising or obvTrend or _prepScore > 0) and
     not panicSell and not distribution
_watchQualify = obvTrend or rsIndex > 0 or htfAlign >= 1 or bankRecovery
_smHasPosSignal =
     _prepScore > 0 or earlyAccumState or cvdBullDiv or
     (cvdRising and aboveMain) or bankRecovery or dipHunter_any or
     (_isBankGYO and _xbankStrong and not panicSell) or
     (_isEnerjiAlt and rsIndex > 2.0 and cvdRising and trendUp and not panicSell) or
     _smSavunmaSignal or _smHoldingSignal or
     (close > emaFastA and close > emaMidA and rsi > rsi[2] and relVol > 0.8 and
      aboveMain and not panicSell and not trendWeakening)
_kgsWatchProxy = not (
     (panicSell or (close < emaMain and trendDown and not recovering) or severeBearish)
     and rsIndex < -2.0 and not aboveMain)
_smToWatch  = _smState == "NEUTRAL" and not panicSell and
     liquidOk and sessionOk and scoreSmoothed >= 26 and scoreSmoothed < 62 and
     _prepScore >= 2 and _watchQualify and _smHasPosSignal and _kgsWatchProxy and
     not (severeBearish and not aboveMain)
_smBankPrepBypass = _smState == "WATCH" and autoSector == "BANKACILIK" and (_xbankFire or _xbankEarlyFire) and _prepScore >= 3 and scoreSmoothed >= 36 and not panicSell and _smWatchBars >= 2
_smMomPrepBypass = _smState == "WATCH" and _isEnerjiAlt and rsIndex > 3.0 and cvdRising and trendUp and relVol > 1.5 and _prepScore >= 3 and scoreSmoothed >= 38 and not panicSell and _smWatchBars >= 2 and not liqSweepUp
_smSavunmaPrepBypass = _smState == "WATCH" and autoSector == "SAVUNMA" and close > emaSlow and obvTrend and _prepScore >= 2 and scoreSmoothed >= 35 and not panicSell and _smWatchBars >= 2
_smHoldingPrepBypass = _smState == "WATCH" and _isHolding and close > emaSlow and (cvdRising or obvTrend) and _prepScore >= 2 and scoreSmoothed >= 35 and not panicSell and _smWatchBars >= 2
_smPrep5Bypass = _smState == "WATCH" and _smWatchBars >= 2 and not panicSell and ((_prep5Score >= 5 and scoreSmoothed >= 32 and _prepScore >= 2) or (_prep5Score >= 4 and scoreSmoothed >= 36 and _prepScore >= 2))
_smToPrep = (_smState == "WATCH" and _smWatchBars >= _smMinWatch and _prepScore >= 4 and scoreSmoothed >= 40 and not panicSell) or _smBankPrepBypass or _smMomPrepBypass or _smSavunmaPrepBypass or _smHoldingPrepBypass or _smPrep5Bypass
_smBankBuyBypass = _smState == "PREP" and autoSector == "BANKACILIK" and _xbankFire and scoreSmoothed >= 44 and _prepScore >= 3 and not panicSell
_smMomBuyBypass = _isEnerjiAlt and rsIndex > 3.0 and cvdRising and trendUp and relVol > 1.5 and _prepScore >= 3 and scoreSmoothed >= 44 and not panicSell and not liqSweepUp and (_smState == "PREP" or (_smState == "WATCH" and rsIndex > 4.0 and scoreSmoothed >= 46))
_smSavunmaBuyBypass = not _isEnerjiAlt and _smState == "PREP" and autoSector == "SAVUNMA" and close > emaSlow and trendUp and _prepScore >= 3 and scoreSmoothed >= 46 and not panicSell
_smTtkomBuyBypass = _smState == "PREP" and _isTtkom and close > emaSlow and trendUp and _prepScore >= 4 and scoreSmoothed >= 42 and htfAlign >= 1 and not panicSell
_smHoldingBuyBypass = _smState == "PREP" and _isHolding and close > emaSlow and (cvdRising or obvTrend) and _prepScore >= 3 and scoreSmoothed >= 46 and not panicSell
_smSectorBuyBypass = (_smState == "WATCH" or _smState == "PREP") and
     _sectorEarlyFire and _trendDominance and scoreSmoothed >= 48 and
     _prepScore >= 2 and htfAlign >= 1 and rsSector > 0 and
     close > vwapVal and not distribution and not panicSell and not isLimitZone
_smToBuy    = (_smState == "PREP" and _smPrepBars >= _smMinPrep and scoreSmoothed >= 50) or
     (_smState == "WATCH" and scoreSmoothed >= 62 and _prepScore >= 8) or
     _smBankBuyBypass or _smMomBuyBypass or
     _smSavunmaBuyBypass or _smHoldingBuyBypass or _smTtkomBuyBypass or
     _smSectorBuyBypass
_smToHold   = _smState == "BUY" and _smStateBars >= 1 and scoreSmoothed >= 46
_pnlPctHold = not na(_smEntryPrice) and _smEntryPrice > 0 ?
     (close - _smEntryPrice) / _smEntryPrice * 100 : 0.0
_holdSubState =
     _smState == "HOLD" and scoreSmoothed > 70 and trendUp and relVol > 1.2 and
          not trendWeakening                                    ? "HOLD-A" :
     _smState == "HOLD" and _pnlPctHold > 10.0 and
          (trendWeakening or obvFalling or scoreSmoothed < 50) ? "REDUCE" :
     _smState == "HOLD" and _pnlPctHold > 5.0 and
          close > emaFastA and not trendWeakening              ? "LOCK"   :
     _smState == "HOLD" and scoreSmoothed >= 55 and
          not trendWeakening                                    ? "HOLD-B" :
     _smState == "HOLD" and
          (distribution or (obvFalling and scoreSmoothed < 45)) ? "REDUCE" :
     _smState == "HOLD" and
          (trendWeakening or scoreSmoothed < 55)                ? "HOLD-C" : ""
_holdStrongCond =
     _holdSubState == "HOLD-A" or
     (_holdSubState == "LOCK" and _pnlPct > 8 and not trendWeakening) or
     (scoreSmoothed > 72 and trendUp and close > emaFastA and not trendWeakening and htfAlign == 2)
_holdWeakCond =
     _holdSubState == "REDUCE" or _holdSubState == "HOLD-D" or _holdSubState == "HOLD-C" or
     trendWeakening or
     (close < emaFastA and _smState == "HOLD") or
     (obvFalling and scoreSmoothed < 52 and _smState == "HOLD") or
     (_pnlPct < -2.0 and _smState == "HOLD") or
     (fakeRisk and _smState == "HOLD") or
     (exitScore < 40 and _smState == "HOLD")
_holdDisplay =
     _holdStrongCond ? "HOLD STRONG" :
     _holdWeakCond   ? "HOLD WEAK" : "HOLD"
_emaStrongStack  = emaFastA > emaMidA and emaMidA > emaSlow and close > emaFastA
_emaWeakBreak    = close < emaFastA and close > emaMidA
_emaCritBreak    = close < emaMidA  // EMA21 alt (WEAK iin kullanlr)
_emaDeepBreak    = close < emaSlow  // EMA50 alt (CRITICAL iin gl koul)
_isTrueCritical  =
     panicSell or
     (close < emaMain and trendDown and not recovering) or
     (_emaDeepBreak and marketRisk and not aboveMain) or
     (_emaDeepBreak and severeBearish and rsIndex < -2.0)
_emaWeakButRecovering = _emaWeakBreak and close > emaSlow and
     (recovering or (emaSlow > emaSlow[3] and rsIndex > -1.0))
_trendHealth =
     _isTrueCritical                                                    ? "CRITICAL" :
     _emaDeepBreak or _emaCritBreak or
     (trendWeakening and not _emaWeakButRecovering) or
     (_emaWeakBreak and not _emaWeakButRecovering and not recovering) or
     (fakeRisk and _smState == "HOLD" and not _emaWeakButRecovering) or
     (obvFalling and scoreSmoothed < 48 and _smState == "HOLD")        ? "WEAK" :
     _emaStrongStack and htfAlign >= 1 and rsIndex > 0
     and not trendWeakening                                            ? "STRONG" :
     "NORMAL"
_trendHealthClr =
     _trendHealth == "CRITICAL" ? color.rgb(200, 0, 0)   :
     _trendHealth == "WEAK"     ? color.rgb(220, 140, 0) :
     _trendHealth == "STRONG"   ? color.rgb(0, 220, 80)  :
     color.rgb(85, 92, 108)
_smPnL  = not na(_smEntryPrice) and _smEntryPrice > 0 ? (close - _smEntryPrice) / _smEntryPrice * 100 : 0.0
_smLossThresh = isMomentum ? -8.0 : _isBankGYO ? -5.0 : _isHolding ? -6.0 : isDefensive ? -6.0 : -5.0
_smLossProtect = (_smState == "HOLD" or _smState == "BUY") and _smPnL < _smLossThresh and not na(_smEntryPrice) and close < _smEntryPrice * (1.0 + _smLossThresh/100 * 0.8)
exitReason =
     hardExitRaw and trendDown                  ? "EXIT-TREND" :
     panicSell                                  ? "EXIT-PANIK" :
     distributionExit                           ? "EXIT-DAGITIM" :
     climaxExit                                 ? "EXIT-CLIMAX" :
     vwapLoseExit                               ? "EXIT-VWAP" :
     emaCrossDown                               ? "EXIT-EMA9" :
     _smLossProtect                             ? "EXIT-STOP" :
     trendWeakening and exitRisk                ? "EXIT-ZAYIF" :
     marketRisk                                 ? "EXIT-RISKOFF" :
     scoreSmoothed < 30 and exitRisk            ? "EXIT-SKOR" :
     exitRisk                                   ? "EXIT" : ""
_sell1  = (_smState == "HOLD" or _smState == "BUY") and trendWeakening and
     scoreSmoothed < 55 and not hardExitRaw
_sell2  = _smState == "HOLD" and _smPnL > 8.0 and trendOld
_sell3  = (_smState == "HOLD" or _smState == "BUY") and
     (exitRiskFull or hardExitRaw or earlyExit or _smLossProtect)
_smToSell = _sell1 or _sell2 or _sell3
// [CL007] 17.09 SELL KILIDI DUZELTMESI (kurul onayli istisna, harici kod -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
_smSellRecovery = _smState == "SELL" and _smStateBars >= 6 and scoreSmoothed >= 40 and
     close > emaFastA and close > emaMidA and not hardExitRaw and not exitRiskFull and not panicSell
_smToNeutral = (_smState == "SELL" and scoreSmoothed < 30) or
     _smSellRecovery or
     (_smState == "WATCH" and scoreSmoothed < 14 and _smStateBars >= 2 and not _smHasPosSignal) or
     (_smState == "WATCH" and _smRegress >= 3) or
     (_smState == "PREP" and scoreSmoothed < 35)
_smRegress := _smState == "PREP" and scoreSmoothed < 40 ? _smRegress + 1 :
     _smState == "WATCH" and scoreSmoothed < 18 ? _smRegress + 1 :
     _smRegress > 0 ? _smRegress - 1 : 0
if _smToNeutral
    _smState     := "NEUTRAL"
    _smStateBars := 0
    _smWatchBars := 0
    _smPrepBars  := 0
    _smEntryPrice := na
    _smRegress   := 0
else if _smToSell and _smState != "SELL"
    _smState     := "SELL"
    _smStateBars := 0
else if _smToHold and _smState != "HOLD"
    _smState     := "HOLD"
    _smStateBars := 0
else if _smToBuy and _smState != "BUY"
    _smState     := "BUY"
    _smStateBars := 0
    _smEntryPrice := close
else if _smToPrep and _smState != "PREP"
    _smState     := "PREP"
    _smStateBars := 0
    _smPrepBars  := 0
else if (_smToWatch or
     (_smState == "NEUTRAL" and not panicSell and liquidOk and sessionOk and
      (trendUp or recovering or (aboveMain and close > emaMidA)) and
     rsIndex > -1.5 and aboveMain and
      (cvdRising or cvdBullDiv or smcBullSignal or rsIndex > 0.5) and
      close > emaMidA and not trendWeakening and
      not distribution and not isLimitZone and
      not (severeBearish and not aboveMain) and scoreSmoothed >= 20)) and _smState != "WATCH"
    _smState     := "WATCH"
    _smStateBars := 0
    _smWatchBars := 0
_smStateBars := _smStateBars + 1
if _smState == "WATCH"
    _smWatchBars := _smWatchBars + 1
if _smState == "PREP"
    _smPrepBars := _smPrepBars + 1
_smScoreMult =
     _smState == "BUY"     ? 1.10 :
     _smState == "HOLD"    ? 1.08 :
     _smState == "PREP"    ? 1.05 :
     _smState == "WATCH"   ? 1.00 :
     _smState == "SELL"    ? 0.80 :
     _smRegress >= 2       ? 0.90 : 1.00
scoreSmoothedFinal := math.min(math.max(scoreSmoothed * _smScoreMult, 0), 99)
_confidence = f_confidence(_emaStrongStack, _emaWeakBreak, _emaCritBreak, htfAlign,
     rsIndex, _smState, _holdDisplay, trendFresh, trendOld, trendAging, panicSell,
     marketRisk, _isBankGYO, _xbankFire, _xbankBear, exitReason != "", exitRiskFull,
     fakeRisk, isMomentum, isDefensive, _isLeaderStock, _isRSLeader,
     isStockLeadingSector, rsIndex[3])
_confidence := f_clamp(_confidence - (_lateEntry ? 8 : 0) - (_volumeQuality < 40 ? 5 : 0) -
     (_isHolding and _trendHealth == "WEAK" ? 8 : 0), 6, 95)
_kgsSecAlpha = stkRoc - secRoc   // hissenin sektrne gre alfa
_kgsTrend = f_kgsTrend(_emaStrongStack, htfAlign, _emaWeakBreak, _emaCritBreak)
_kgsEMA = f_kgsEma(aboveMain, close > emaSlow, close > emaMidA, htfAlign)
_kgsRS = f_kgsRs(rsRank, rsSectorRnk, _rsMomentum, volumeSpike)
_kgsVol = f_kgsVol(volumeHuge, volumeSpike, fakeRisk, close > open, relVol, volumeDry)
_kgsSector = f_kgsSector(isSectorStrongEff, isStockLeadingSector, _kgsSecAlpha,
     sectorStrong, rsSector, _secLeadScore)
_kgsCVD = f_kgsCvd(cvdBullDiv, cvdRising, obvTrend, hiddenDist)
_kgsKurum = f_kgsKurum(_gizliToplama, _gizliDagitim, mfiBull, _kurumBaskisi, obvTrend)
_kgsRiskPenalty = f_kgsRisk(panicSell, marketRisk, _isBankGYO, aboveMain, exitRiskFull, fakeRisk)
_kgsBankAdj = f_kgsBankAdj(_isBankGYO, _xbankBear, aboveMain, _xbankFire)
_kgsStateBonus = f_kgsState(_smState, _holdDisplay)
_xbankEarlyLeader = f_xbankEarlyLeader(autoSector == "BANKACILIK", _xbankRet3h,
     relVol, close, emaFast, emaFast > emaFast[1], sessionOk,
     fakeRisk, panicSell, isLimitZone)
_kgsV103Adj = f_kgsV103Adjust(autoSector, fakeRisk, panicSell, cvdRising, smcStrength,
     _trendHealth, htfAlign, rsSector, cvdBullDiv, hiddenDist)
_kgsEarlyLeaderAdj = _xbankEarlyLeader ? 6 : 0
_kgsAccelAdj = _leaderAccel >= 80 ? 5 : _leaderAccel >= 70 ? 3 : 0
_kgsSectorIndexAdj = (autoSector == "BANKACILIK" or autoSector == "HOLDING" or
     autoSector == "SINAI" or autoSector == "HAVACILIK" or autoSector == "ILETISIM") ?
     (secRoc > idxRoc + 1.0 ? 4 : secRoc > idxRoc ? 2 : secRoc < idxRoc - 1.0 ? -3 : 0) : 0
_kgsRaw = _kgsTrend + _kgsEMA + _kgsRS + _kgsVol + _kgsSector + _kgsCVD + _kgsKurum +
          _kgsBankAdj + _kgsStateBonus + _kgsV103Adj + _kgsEarlyLeaderAdj + _kgsAccelAdj +
          _kgsSectorIndexAdj
_kgs    = f_clamp(_kgsRaw - _kgsRiskPenalty, 0, 100)
_kgsEliteCondition =
     _trendHealth == "STRONG" and
     _kgsSecAlpha > 2.0 and rsIndex > 2.0 and
     isSectorStrongEff and
     (cvdRising or cvdBullDiv) and
     (_gizliToplama or _kurumBaskisi or (obvTrend and mfiBull)) and
     not fakeRisk and not panicSell and not marketRisk and
     (_smState == "BUY" or (_smState == "HOLD" and _holdDisplay == "HOLD STRONG"))
_kgs := _kgsEliteCondition ? math.max(_kgs, 82) : _kgs
_sectorBaseOk = close > emaFastA and close > emaMidA and htfAlign >= 1 and not fakeRisk and not panicSell
_sectorStructureOk = close > emaSlow and close > emaMain and htfAlign >= 1 and not fakeRisk and not panicSell
_sectorConsolidationOk = close > emaMain * 0.97 and close > close[3] * 0.98 and not panicSell and not severeBearish
_kgs := _isHolding and _sectorBaseOk ? math.max(_kgs, 42) : _kgs
_kgs := _isHolding and not _sectorBaseOk and _sectorConsolidationOk ? math.max(_kgs, 36) : _kgs
_kgs := autoSector == "HAVACILIK" and _sectorBaseOk and relVol > 1.0 ? math.max(_kgs, 34) : _kgs
_kgs := autoSector == "HAVACILIK" and not _sectorBaseOk and _sectorStructureOk ? math.max(_kgs, 38) : _kgs
_recoveryBelowMain = close < emaMain and close > emaFastA and _trendHealth == "STRONG"
_kgs := _recoveryBelowMain and not _isBankGYO ? math.min(_kgs, 60) : _kgs
_confidence := math.max(_confidence, _kgs >= 80 and htfAlign == 2 and _trendHealth == "STRONG" ? 70 : 0)
_buyWindow = f_buyWindow(_kgs, _trendHealth, isStockLeadingSector,
     cvdRising or cvdBullDiv or _gizliToplama or _kurumBaskisi, _volumeQuality,
     _extensionAtr, _lateEntry, trendFresh, fakeRisk, panicSell)
_leaderCandidateRaw = f_leaderCandidate(_posOpen, _kgs, _trendHealth, smcBullSignal,
     smcStrength, cvdRising, cvdBullDiv, fakeRisk, panicSell, distribution, hiddenDist) and
     not _xbankEarlyLeader and _leaderAccel < 75 and not _lateEntry
_leaderCandidate = f_lcCooldown(_leaderCandidateRaw, lcCooldownBars)
_turnaroundCandidate = f_turnaroundCandidate(_posOpen, _kgs, earlyTurnSignal,
     dipHunter_any, cvdRising, cvdBullDiv, rsi > rsi[1], panicSell, distribution)
[_leaderAccelerationSignal, _reloadSignal] = f_v105Signals(_posOpen, _leaderAccel, _accelReady,
     _trendHealth, rsSector, close, emaFast, emaMidA, _reloadSetup, _xbankEarlyLeader,
     fakeRisk, panicSell, distribution, _kgs, hiddenDist)
[_flowPositive, _flowStrong, _flowNegative, _cvdStrong, _cvdNegative,
     _momentumSetup, _earlyAlpha, _highVolWick] = f_v113Context(cvdRising, cvdBullDiv,
     _gizliToplama, _kurumBaskisi, mfiBull, _gizliDagitim, obvTrend, relVol, hiddenDist,
     mfi, cvd, cvdSma, cvdBearDiv, cvdFalling, high, ta.highest(high[1], 3), emaFastA,
     emaMidA, _xu100Chg5, stkRoc, rsIndex, upperWick, candleBody, close, candleRange)
_timingTrigger = f_v111TimingTrigger(_buyWindow, _kgs, _trendHealth, isStockLeadingSector,
     _flowPositive, emaFastA > emaFastA[1], close > close[1], rsi > rsi[1],
     _volumeQuality, _extensionAtr, fakeRisk, panicSell)
_wakingUp = f_wakingUp(_kgs, close, rsi, fakeRisk, panicSell) or
     ((autoSector == "PERAKENDE" or autoSector == "ENERJI") and _kgs < 55 and
      close > emaFastA and close > close[3] * 1.03 and rsi > rsi[1] and not panicSell)
_volumeQualityEff = f_adaptiveVolumeQuality(_volumeQuality, autoSector, relVol, _trendHealth, _wakingUp)
_leaderFloorBoost = f_leaderFloor(autoSector, _kgs, _secLeadRank, _trendHealth, htfAlign, fakeRisk, panicSell)
_kgsEntryBridge = _kgs >= 82 and _trendHealth == "STRONG" and htfAlign >= 1 and _secLeadRank >= 65 and not fakeRisk and not panicSell ? 8 :
     _kgs >= 75 and _trendHealth != "CRITICAL" and htfAlign >= 1 and _secLeadRank >= 65 ? 4 : 0
_holdingXbankBoost = _isHolding and _xbankFire and _trendHealth != "CRITICAL" and not panicSell ? 4 : 0
_bankHoldEntryBoost = (autoSector == "BANKACILIK" or _isHolding) and _kgs > 75 and
     htfAlign >= 1 and _emaStrongStack and not fakeRisk and not panicSell ? 5 : 0
_entryScore = f_entryScore(_kgs, _trendHealth, _secLeadRank, _flowStrong, _flowPositive,
     _flowNegative, _cvdStrong, _cvdNegative, _volumeQualityEff, _leaderAccel, _buyWindow,
     _timingTrigger, _lateEntry, fakeRisk, _highVolWick, panicSell, distribution,
     htfAlign, trendAge, rrRatio, atrExpansion, _momentumSetup, _earlyAlpha,
     _leaderFloorBoost, _kgsEntryBridge + _bankHoldEntryBoost, _holdingXbankBoost)
_entryScore := _isHolding and _sectorBaseOk ? math.max(_entryScore, 22) : _entryScore
_entryScore := _isHolding and not _sectorBaseOk and _sectorConsolidationOk ? math.max(_entryScore, 18) : _entryScore
_entryScore := autoSector == "HAVACILIK" and _sectorBaseOk and relVol > 1.0 ? math.max(_entryScore, 18) : _entryScore
_entryScore := autoSector == "HAVACILIK" and not _sectorBaseOk and _sectorStructureOk ? math.max(_entryScore, 20) : _entryScore
_passReason = f_passReason(_entryScore, _trendHealth, _lateEntry, _volumeQualityEff,
     fakeRisk, panicSell, distribution, htfAlign, trendAge, rrRatio)
[_smartMoney, _momentumScore, _exhaustScore, _sectorRankTxt, _regimeRadar, _holdScore] =
     f_radarLite(relVol, candleRange, atr, close, low, vwapVal, _flowStrong, _flowPositive,
     hiddenDist, _highVolWick, _leaderAccel, emaFastA, rsIndex, trendWeakening, upperWick,
     candleBody, trendAge, rsi, atrAvg, _leadScore, autoSector, _xbankFire, _isHoldRot,
     marketRiskOn, sectorStrong, marketRisk, _trendHealth, _volumeQualityEff)
_blockers = f_blockers(_trendHealth, rsRank, _volumeQualityEff, _flowPositive, _leadScore, rrRatio, _lateEntry, _exhaustScore, htfAlign, trendAge, isStockLeadingSector)
[_entryQuality, _daytradeValid, _daytradeDecision, _finalDecision, _daytradeQualified, _earlyLeaderEntry, _windowCandidate] = f_v114Decision(_entryScore, _kgs,
     _trendHealth, isStockLeadingSector, _flowPositive,
     fakeRisk, panicSell, daytradeMode, _smState == "BUY", _smState == "BUY" and _smState[1] != "BUY",
     portfolioPosition, _leaderCandidate,
     _leaderAccelerationSignal, _leaderAccel, _reloadSignal, relVol, _lateEntry, _strongVolume,
     _volumeQualityEff, trendFresh, exitRiskFull or hardExitRaw, _buyWindow, _timingTrigger,
     htfAlign, trendAge, rrRatio)
// [CL028] positionConflict guvenlik acigi (EKLEME diginda YENI AL da -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
_erkenPosCakisma = _posOpen and portfolioPosition
_finalAlertEvent = f_finalAlertEvent(_finalDecision) and not _erkenPosCakisma
// [CL008] 17.09 (kullanici talebi: "sagdaki WR/PF/Kelly panelleri dolu gorunsun" -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
[_shQ, _shV, _shD, _shadowFinalDecision, _shDt, _shE, _shW] = f_v114Decision(_entryScore, _kgs,
     _trendHealth, isStockLeadingSector, _flowPositive,
     fakeRisk, panicSell, daytradeMode, _smState == "BUY", _smState == "BUY" and _smState[1] != "BUY",
     false, _leaderCandidate,
     _leaderAccelerationSignal, _leaderAccel, _reloadSignal, relVol, _lateEntry, _strongVolume,
     _volumeQualityEff, trendFresh, exitRiskFull or hardExitRaw, _buyWindow, _timingTrigger,
     htfAlign, trendAge, rrRatio)
_shadowAlertEvent = f_finalAlertEvent(_shadowFinalDecision)
// PATCH 1 (kullanici onerisi, DUZELTILMIS degisken isimleriyle): ASTOR gibi KGS
// yuksek ama ACTION=IZLE durumlarinda nedeni acikca yazar. Orijinal onerideki
// _sectorLeader/_rr/_trendAge/_hasPortfolioPosition/_exitRisk isimleri bu kod
// tabaninda YOK - gercek karsiliklari: isStockLeadingSector/rrRatio/trendAge/
// portfolioPosition/(exitRiskFull or hardExitRaw) kullanildi.
f_waitReason(_finalDecision, _kgs, _buyWindow, _lateEntry, _sectorLeader, _flowPositive,
     _rr, _trendAge, _volumeQuality) =>
    _finalDecision == "AL" or _finalDecision == "TEYITLI AL" or _finalDecision == "ERKEN AL" ? "ISLEM ADAYI" :
     _lateEntry ? "GEC GIRIS / KOVALAMA" :
     _buyWindow == "KAPALI" and _kgs >= 70 ? "GUC VAR / GIRIS KAPALI" :
     not _sectorLeader ? "SEKTOR LIDERLIGI ZAYIF" :
     not _flowPositive ? "AKIS TEYIDI YOK" :
     _rr < 1.5 ? "RR ZAYIF" :
     _trendAge > 20 ? "TREND YASLI" :
     _volumeQuality < 45 ? "HACIM TEYIDI ZAYIF" :
     "IZLE / TEYIT BEKLE"
// [CL009] NOT: waitReasonTxt hesaplamasi asagiya, _v113Lifecycle/_pozAzaltRaw/_u -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
_v112ExitEvent = _smState == "SELL" or panicSell or distribution or
     (close < emaMidA and rsi < 45 and not pullbackState)
[_v112Entered, _v112Open, _v112Entry, _v112Total, _v112Wr, _v112Pf, _v112Exp, _v112AvgW, _v112AvgL, _v112MaxDd] =
     f_v112Validation(_shadowAlertEvent, _v112ExitEvent, close)
// 08.08 DUZELTME: _wV112n atamasi BURAYA (v112Total hesaplandigi yere)
// tasindi - onceki halinde P3 skor parametrelerinin ortasina, ilgisiz
// bir yorum blogunun icine sikismisti.
// === MODUL 2+4 (kurumsal): GIRIS ANI ENSTANTANESI - _v112Entered GERCEK giris olayidir
// (ayni sistem BT/EXPECT panelinde gosterilen), bu yuzden faktor/OOS yakalamasi BURAYA
// baglandi - AYRI bir _posOpen sistemi ACILMADI, cift kayit riski YOK. ===
var string _entryFactor = "-"
var int    _entryTimeMs = 0
// [CL010] 17.09 B4 DUZELTMESI (dış kurul incelemesi, dogrulandi): B4'un rejim -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
var string _entryRegime = na
var string _entryRadarRegime = na
// [CL011] 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi, "LIVE/SHADOW karismasi" bulgusu - -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
var bool _entryWasManual = na
if _v112Entered
    _entryFactor := f_topFactor(scoreTrend, scoreRsIndex, scoreSectorLead, scoreFlow, scoreObv, scoreInstF)
    _entryTimeMs := time
    _entryRegime := regimeTagConfirmed
    _entryRadarRegime := _regimeRadar
    _entryWasManual := portfolioPosition
_wV112n := _v112Total
_v112Closed = _v112ExitEvent and _v112Open[1] and not na(_v112Entry[1])
_v112ClosedPnl = _v112Closed ? (close - _v112Entry[1]) / _v112Entry[1] * 100.0 : 0.0
[_regOnN, _regOnWr, _regOnPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryRadarRegime == "BROAD ON")
[_regOffN, _regOffWr, _regOffPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryRadarRegime != "BROAD ON")
[_secN, _secWr, _secPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, true)
// 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi madde 13): devEndDate 2026-01-01'de
// kalmisti ama Eylul 2026'ya kadar modelde COK sayida mantik degisikligi
// yapildi (P0'lar, B1-B6, V162.1b/1c) - Ocak sonrasi veri ARTIK gercek
// OOS degil (yeni kurallarla YENIDEN hesaplanmis gecmis). Gercek ileri-
// test penceresi SON degisiklikten (17.09.2026) sonraki DOKUNULMAMIS
// veri olmali. modelVersion/logicEraStart de webhook'a P3Ozet uzerinden
// tasinabilir (asagida, sinyal siniflandirmasinda kullanilacaksa).
logicEraStart = timestamp("2026-09-17 00:00 +0300")
// [CL027] "Yeni" rejim kovalari artik logicEraStart'tan (17.09, son mantik
// degisikligi) besleniyor, hacimEraTarihi (08.07) DEGIL - eskiden 08.07-16.09
// arasi da "yeni" sayiliyordu, ama o donem hala eski mantikla hesaplaniyordu.
// hacimEraTarihi Eski/panel kovalarinda (degismedi) referans olarak kaliyor.
devEndDate = input.time(timestamp("2026-09-17 00:00 +0300"), "Gelistirme Bitis Tarihi (OOS baslangici)", group="Backtest")
// === MODUL 1 (kurumsal): REJIM-BAZLI BACKTEST - regimeTag zaten mevcut bar icin canli
// hesaplaniyor, tipki _regOnN/_regOffN'nin _regimeRadar'i kullandigi gibi (AYNI PATTERN,
// AYNI f_perfBucket fonksiyonu - yeni sayac mekanizmasi YOK). ===
// === EK MODUL A (kurumsal): VOLATILITE REJIMI - mevcut atr/atrAvg oranindan turetilir,
// yeni hesaplama YOK. TREND/YATAY/DUSUS ile ayni pattern. ===
volRegime = atr > atrAvg * 1.3 ? "YUKSEK" : atr < atrAvg * 0.8 ? "DUSUK" : "NORMAL"
// 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi madde 11): volRegime bu noktada HENUZ
// hesaplanmadigi icin ana enstantane blogunda (yukarida) YAKALANAMIYORDU -
// kendi tanimindan hemen SONRA ayri bir enstantane eklendi.
var string _entryVolRegime = na
if _v112Entered
    _entryVolRegime := volRegime
[_volHighN, _volHighWr, _volHighPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryVolRegime == "YUKSEK")
// [V163-6] _volNorm* kullanilmiyordu (olu kod) - cikarildi.
[_volLowN, _volLowWr, _volLowPf]    = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryVolRegime == "DUSUK")
// === EK MODUL B (kurumsal): KELLY FRAKSIYONU - mevcut _v112Wr/_v112AvgW/_v112AvgL
// degerlerinden turetilir, YENI hesaplama/sayac YOK. SADECE BILGI - lotSize'a
// otomatik uygulanMIYOR (ayri onay gerekir). ===
// 08.08 DUZELTME: ayni PF=0 belirsizligi - DUZELTILMEZSE "hic kayip
// yok" durumunda Kelly Fraksiyonu YANLISLIKLA "sifir pozisyon" onerirdi
// (oysa mukemmel performans YUKSEK pozisyon onermeli).

[_trendN, _trendWr, _trendPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryRegime == "TREND")
[_yatayN, _yatayWr, _yatayPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryRegime == "YATAY")
[_dususN, _dususWr, _dususPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryRegime == "DUSUS")
// 17.09 B4 EKLENTI (kurul onayli istisna): yukaridaki uc sayac BASLANGICTAN
// BERI hic sifirlanmiyor - 08.07 hacim tabani degisiminden ONCEKI ve
// SONRAKI islemler AYNI sayacta KARISIYOR (Madde 10). devEndDate/_entryTimeMs
// ile ZATEN var olan OOS ayrimindaki AYNI desen kullanilarak "ESKI"/"YENI"
// donem etiketli CIFT sayac eklendi - ESKI donem ARSIVLENMIS sayilir (referans
// icin duruyor ama karara GIRMIYOR), YENI donem yeniden 0'dan sayiyor.
hacimEraTarihi = input.time(timestamp("2026-07-08 00:00 +0300"), "Hacim Tabani Degisim Tarihi (WR Era Siniri)", group="Backtest")
[_trendEskiN, _trendEskiWr, _trendEskiPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryRegime == "TREND" and _entryTimeMs <= int(hacimEraTarihi))
[_trendYeniN, _trendYeniWr, _trendYeniPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryRegime == "TREND" and _entryTimeMs >= logicEraStart and not _entryWasManual)
[_yatayEskiN, _yatayEskiWr, _yatayEskiPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryRegime == "YATAY" and _entryTimeMs <= int(hacimEraTarihi))
[_yatayYeniN, _yatayYeniWr, _yatayYeniPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryRegime == "YATAY" and _entryTimeMs >= logicEraStart and not _entryWasManual)
[_dususEskiN, _dususEskiWr, _dususEskiPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryRegime == "DUSUS" and _entryTimeMs <= int(hacimEraTarihi))
[_dususYeniN, _dususYeniWr, _dususYeniPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryRegime == "DUSUS" and _entryTimeMs >= logicEraStart and not _entryWasManual)
// [CL012] V0 KAPSAM NOTU: yalniz _curRegimeWr'yi (canli regimeHistMult karari) b -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
[_fTrendN, _fTrendWr, _fTrendPf]   = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryFactor == "TREND")
[_fRsN, _fRsWr, _fRsPf]            = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryFactor == "RS")
[_fSectorN, _fSectorWr, _fSectorPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryFactor == "SECTOR")
[_fFlowN, _fFlowWr, _fFlowPf]      = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryFactor == "FLOW")
[_fObvN, _fObvWr, _fObvPf]         = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryFactor == "OBV")
[_fInstN, _fInstWr, _fInstPf]      = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryFactor == "INST")
// === MODUL 4 (kurumsal): ORNEKLEM-DISI (OOS) - sadece GELISTIRME BITIS TARIHINDEN

[_oosN, _oosWr, _oosPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entryTimeMs > int(devEndDate))
// === MODUL 3 (kurumsal): CTRL RADAR KOPRUSU - opsiyonel, KARARI DEGISTIRMEZ, sadece
// gorsel uyari. CTRL RADAR tarafinda plot(concentrationWarn ? 1 : 0, "Sektor Yogunlasma",
// display=display.none) eklenmesi gerekir. ===
sectorConcWarn = input.source(close, "CTRL Radar Sektor Yogunlasma Sinyali (opsiyonel)", group="Portfoy Riski")
dirAlignWarnSrc = input.source(close, "CTRL Radar Yon Uyum Riski Sinyali (opsiyonel)", group="Portfoy Riski")
// === UCUNCU KOPRU (kullanici onerisi - Radar/Focus karar matrisi): CTRL Radar'in BU
// SEMBOL icin verdigi karari + islem uygunluk skorunu okur. AYNI baglanti-dogrulama
// deseni kullanildi (beklenen kod araligi disindaki deger = BAGLANMAMIS).
radarDecSrc = input.source(close, "CTRL Radar: Bu Sembolun Karari (opsiyonel)", group="Portfoy Riski")
radarTradeSrc = input.source(close, "CTRL Radar: Bu Sembolun Islem Uygunluk Skoru (opsiyonel)", group="Portfoy Riski")
// 27.08 ALTYAPI YAMASI: -1 (V195 radar evreni disi, orn. TRALT) artik
// yanlislikla "bagli" sayilmiyor. SALT GORUNTU - hicbir alertcondition'a
// girmiyor, dogrulandi (kurul analizi 2026-08-27).
radarDecOutOfUniverse = not na(radarDecSrc) and radarDecSrc == -1
radarBridgeConnected = not na(radarDecSrc) and (radarDecSrc == 0 or radarDecSrc == 1 or radarDecSrc == 2 or radarDecSrc == 3)
radarDecSafe = radarBridgeConnected ? radarDecSrc : na
radarDecTxt = radarDecOutOfUniverse ? "TAKIP DISI" : not radarBridgeConnected ? "BAGLI DEGIL" : radarDecSafe == 3 ? "BUY" : radarDecSafe == 2 ? "WATCH" : radarDecSafe == 1 ? "PREP" : "AVOID"
// PATCH 3 (kullanici onerisi, ARTIK GERCEKTEN MUMKUN - koprulendi): Radar/Focus karar
// matrisi. Onceki turda "iki ayri script birbirini goremez" dedigimiz icin
// ERTELENMISTI - simdi yukaridaki plot() koprusuyle MUMKUN oldu. Gercek degisken
// isimleri kullanildi: _finalDecision, _buyWindow (bu kod tabaninda GERCEKTEN var).
f_crossSystemComment(_radarDec, _focusDec, _buyWindow) =>
    _radarDec == "BUY" and (_focusDec == "IZLE" or _focusDec == "ADAY AL") and _buyWindow == "KAPALI" ? "RADAR GUCLU / SETUP BEKLE" :
     _radarDec == "BUY" and (_focusDec == "AL" or _focusDec == "TEYITLI AL" or _focusDec == "ERKEN AL") ? "RADAR + FOCUS UYUMLU" :
     _radarDec == "WATCH" and _focusDec == "HOLD" ? "POZISYON TASINIR" :
     _radarDec == "AVOID" and (_focusDec == "AL" or _focusDec == "TEYITLI AL" or _focusDec == "ERKEN AL") ? "CELISKI / MANUEL KONTROL" :
     _radarDec == "BAGLI DEGIL" or _radarDec == "TAKIP DISI" ? _radarDec :
     "NORMAL"
crossSystemTxt = f_crossSystemComment(radarDecTxt, _finalDecision, _buyWindow)
// === EK MODUL F (kurumsal): KOPRU BAGLANTI DOGRULAMASI - input.source baglanmadiginda
// sessizce "close" (fiyat) degerine duser, bu ise > 0.5 oldugu icin YANLIŞLIKLA "risk var"
// sinyali gibi okunabilir. Beklenen deger SADECE 0 veya 1 (CTRL Radar'in plot ciktisi) -
// baska bir deger (orn. 77.20 gibi bir fiyat) BAGLANMAMIS demektir. ===
sectorBridgeConnected = sectorConcWarn == 0 or sectorConcWarn == 1
dirAlignBridgeConnected = dirAlignWarnSrc == 0 or dirAlignWarnSrc == 1
portfolioRiskWarn = sectorBridgeConnected and sectorConcWarn > 0.5
dirAlignRiskWarn = dirAlignBridgeConnected and dirAlignWarnSrc > 0.5
// KABUL KRITERI EK MODUL F (baglanti dogrulamasi) KARSILANDI: evet - baglanmamis
// kopru artik YANLIS pozisyon kesintisine yol acmiyor (guvenli varsayilan: baglanti
// yoksa risk sinyali YOK sayilir, boyut kesilmez - ama panelde ayrica uyari gosterilir)
// KABUL KRITERI MODUL 1+2+3+4 (v112 lifecycle'a bagli, f_perfBucket yeniden kullanildi,
// yeni request.security/dongu YOK) KARSILANDI: evet
[_aqN, _aqWr1, _aqWr3, _aqWr5, _aqTp1, _aqTp2, _aqFalse, _aqDd] =
     f_alarmQuality(_v112Entered, close, tp1, tp2, high, low)
_v113Pnl = _v112Open and not na(_v112Entry) ? (close - _v112Entry) / _v112Entry * 100.0 : 0.0
_v113Lifecycle = _v112Entered ? "YENI AL" :
     _v112ExitEvent and _v112Open[1] ? "CIKIS" :
     _v112Open and _v113Pnl > 3.0 and (trendWeakening or exitScore < 65) ? "KAR KORU" :
     _v112Open ? "TUT" : _finalDecision == "PAS GEC" ? _passReason :
     _finalDecision == "IZLE" ? "IZLE" : _finalDecision
[_p1Raw, _p2Raw, _p3Raw, _p1aRaw, _p1bRaw, _p2aRaw, _p2bRaw] =
     f_alarmClass(_finalDecision, _entryScore, _kgs, _leadScore, _trendHealth, _flowPositive,
     fakeRisk, panicSell, _leaderCandidate, _wakingUp, _smState, _windowCandidate,
     _reloadSignal, _earlyLeaderEntry)
_p1Alert = canAlert and _p1Raw and not _p1Raw[1]
_p2Alert = canAlert and _p2Raw and not _p2Raw[1]
_p3Alert = canAlert and _p3Raw and not _p3Raw[1]
_p1aAlert = _p1Alert and _p1aRaw
_p1bAlert = _p1Alert and _p1bRaw
_p2aAlert = _p2Alert and _p2aRaw
_p2bAlert = _p2Alert and _p2bRaw
// 17.09 B1: "and _kgs > 50" kaldirildi (bkz. header, V162.1 EK)
_p0Raw = _smState == "PREP" and _entryScore > 45 and _flowPositive and _rsMomentum and _trendHealth != "CRITICAL" and not fakeRisk and not panicSell
_exitWatchRaw = _exhaustScore > 70 and rsRank < rsRank[3] and cvdFalling and _smState != "SELL"
// 17.09 B1: "and _kgs >= 50" kaldirildi (bkz. header, V162.1 EK)
_reloadCandidateRaw = not _reloadSignal and _reloadSetup and _entryScore >= 45 and _flowPositive and not fakeRisk and not panicSell
_rotationRaw = (_regimeRadar == "BANK LEAD" or _regimeRadar == "HOLD LEAD" or _regimeRadar == "ROTATION") and _regimeRadar != _regimeRadar[1]
// [CL013] 17.09 ASGARI GUVENLIK YAMASI (dış kurul incelemesi, bulgu #6 - dogrula -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
_positionManaged = portfolioPosition or _v112Open or _posOpen
// [CL014] 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi, madde 6 - "pozisyon kaynaklari hala tam -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
positionSource = _posOpen ? "INTERNAL" : portfolioPosition ? "MANUAL" : _v112Open ? "RESEARCH" : "NONE"
_posConflictCount = (_posOpen ? 1 : 0) + (portfolioPosition ? 1 : 0) + (_v112Open ? 1 : 0)
positionConflict = _posConflictCount >= 2
_newEntryMode = not _positionManaged
_p0Alert = canAlert and _p0Raw and not _p0Raw[1]
_exitWatchAlert = canAlert and _exitWatchRaw and not _exitWatchRaw[1]
_reloadCandidateAlert = canAlert and _reloadCandidateRaw and not _reloadCandidateRaw[1]
_rotationAlert = canAlert and _rotationRaw
_dipDonusAlert = canAlert and (_wakingUp or _turnaroundCandidate) and not (_wakingUp[1] or _turnaroundCandidate[1]) and _trendHealth != "CRITICAL"
_carryAlert = canAlert and _positionManaged and _smState == "HOLD" and _holdDisplay == "HOLD STRONG" and not (_smState[1] == "HOLD" and _holdDisplay[1] == "HOLD STRONG")
// [CL015] 17.09 DUZELTME (kurul onayli, istisna karari - MANTIK ama hata duzeltm -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
_hardExitBreak = _posOpen and not na(_stopPrice) and close < _stopPrice and close < emaMidA and relVol > 1.3 and _flowNegative
// 08.08 DUZELTME: son alt-kosulda OR->AND (cvdFalling AND trendWeakening,
// tek zayiflik sinyali YETMEZ artik) - sinyal_dogrulama.py: POZ_AZALT
// sonrasi fiyat GUCLU yukseldi (T+1 %+3.4, dogrulanan yalniz %25) -
// erken/asiri-hassas tetiklenme supheliydi.
_pozAzaltRaw = not _hardExitBreak and (hardExitRaw or (_smState == "SELL" and _smState[1] != "SELL") or
     distribution or (close < emaMidA and (cvdFalling and trendWeakening)))
// 04.09: pozsebep hesaplamasi BURAYA - tum bilesenler (hardExitRaw,
// _smState, distribution, emaMidA/cvdFalling/trendWeakening, _posOpen)
// bu satirdan once zaten tanimli.
_wPozSebep := (hardExitRaw ? 1 : 0) + ((_smState == "SELL" and _smState[1] != "SELL") ? 2 : 0) +
     (distribution ? 4 : 0) + ((close < emaMidA and cvdFalling and trendWeakening) ? 8 : 0) +
     (_posOpen ? 16 : 0)
_urgentExitRaw = panicSell or _hardExitBreak
_urgentExitAlert = canAlert and _urgentExitRaw and not _urgentExitRaw[1]
_pozAzaltAlert = canAlert and _positionManaged and _pozAzaltRaw and not _pozAzaltRaw[1]
// LOOP TURU (kullanici gozlemi - ASELS ekrani): waitReasonTxt artik ACTION hucresinin
// (_kararTxt) UYGULADIGI AYNI yasam-dongusu gecersiz kilmasini kontrol ediyor. Eger
// ham karar AL-benzeriyken ACTION zaten TUT/IZLE'ye cevrilmisse, NOTE de bunu
// YANSITIR - artik iki hucre CELISMIYOR.
_actionOverridden = _v113Lifecycle == "TUT" or portfolioPosition
waitReasonTxt = _actionOverridden and (_finalDecision == "AL" or _finalDecision == "TEYITLI AL" or _finalDecision == "ERKEN AL") ?
     (portfolioPosition ? "POZISYONDA TUT" : "IZLEMEDE TUT") :
     f_waitReason(_finalDecision, _kgs, _buyWindow, _lateEntry, isStockLeadingSector,
     _flowPositive, rrRatio, trendAge, _volumeQualityEff)
// KABUL KRITERI (ACTION/NOTE tutarliligi) KARSILANDI: evet
// EK: REDUCE-ozel debounce - _pozAzaltAlert HAM tetikleyiciyi degistirmez (panel hala
// _pozAzaltRaw'i gercek zamanli kullaniyor), sadece ALARMIN ne siklikta ATESLENECEGINI
// kisitlar.
var int _lastReduceAlertBar = 0
_pozAzaltAlertDebounced = _pozAzaltAlert and (_lastReduceAlertBar == 0 or bar_index - _lastReduceAlertBar >= reduceAlertGapBars)
if _pozAzaltAlertDebounced
    _lastReduceAlertBar := bar_index
_finalBuyRaw = _finalDecision == "AL" or _finalDecision == "TEYITLI AL"
_finalEarlyRaw = _finalDecision == "ERKEN AL"
_buyOpsGate = _newEntryMode and _buyWindow != "KAPALI" and
     not (fakeRisk or panicSell or distribution or htfAlign < 1 or rrRatio < 1.2) and
     _volumeQualityEff >= 40 and _flowPositive and htfAlign >= 1 and rrRatio >= 1.5 and
     not _lateEntry and not fakeRisk and not panicSell and not _urgentExitRaw
// 17.09 B1: "and _kgs >= 50" kaldirildi (bkz. header, V162.1 EK)
_p3HacimRaw = _newEntryMode and not _finalBuyRaw and _trendHealth != "CRITICAL" and
     _entryScore >= 45 and _volumeQualityEff < 40 and not fakeRisk and not panicSell
_p1aOpAlert = _p1aAlert and _buyOpsGate and _finalBuyRaw
_p1bOpAlert = _p1bAlert and _buyOpsGate and _finalBuyRaw
_p0OpAlert = _p0Alert and _newEntryMode and (_finalEarlyRaw or (_finalDecision == "ADAY AL" and _buyWindow != "KAPALI")) and not _p3HacimRaw
_dipDonusOpAlert = _dipDonusAlert and _newEntryMode and _trendHealth != "CRITICAL" and not _p3HacimRaw
_reloadOpAlert = _reloadCandidateAlert and _newEntryMode and _buyWindow != "KAPALI" and not _p3HacimRaw
_p3HacimAlert = canAlert and _p3HacimRaw and not _p3HacimRaw[1]
// 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi, "3 kucuk duzeltme" #3 - dogrulandi):
// EKLEME_ADAYI, pozisyon kaynagi cakismasi (positionConflict) sirasinda
// ENGELLENMIYORDU - birden fazla kaynak ayni anda "acik" derken
// pozisyon buyutme onerisi vermek riskli.
_addCandidateAlert = canAlert and _positionManaged and not positionConflict and _reloadCandidateRaw and _entryScore >= 65 and not _reloadCandidateRaw[1]
_kgsLotMult =
     _kgs >= 75 ? 1.00 :
     _kgs >= 55 ? 0.75 :
     _kgs >= 35 ? 0.50 :
     _kgs >= 15 ? 0.25 : 0.0
// === MODUL 3 FAZ 2 (kurumsal, onaylandi): PORTFOY RISKI POZISYON KESME - CTRL Radar'dan
// gelen iki sinyal (sektor yogunlasma + yon uyum riski) aktifken pozisyon boyutu otomatik
// kisilir. Ikisi BIRDEN aktifse daha agresif kesim (%50), sadece biri aktifse hafif
// kesim (%30). Bu, "aynı anda cok korele pozisyon acma" riskini gercekten azaltir -
// onceki fazda sadece GORSEL uyariydi, KARARI ETKILEMIYORDU. ===
portfolioRiskMult = (portfolioRiskWarn and dirAlignRiskWarn) ? 0.50 :
     (portfolioRiskWarn or dirAlignRiskWarn) ? 0.70 : 1.00
// [CL016] === EK MODUL D (kurumsal, onaylandi): KELLY-BAZLI BOYUT AYARI - artik  -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
_sessHH = hour(time, "Europe/Istanbul")
_sessMM = minute(time)
sessionTag = (_sessHH == 10 and _sessMM < 15) ? "ACILIS" :
     ((_sessHH == 10 and _sessMM >= 15) or _sessHH == 11 or (_sessHH == 12 and _sessMM <= 30)) ? "ANA" :
     ((_sessHH == 12 and _sessMM > 30) or (_sessHH >= 13 and _sessHH < 15) or (_sessHH == 15 and _sessMM <= 45)) ? "OGLE" : "KAPANIS"
// 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi madde 11): sessionTag da HENUZ hesaplanmadigi
// icin ana enstantane blogunda yakalanamiyordu - kendi tanimindan hemen
// SONRA ayri enstantane eklendi.
var string _entrySession = na
if _v112Entered
    _entrySession := sessionTag
[_sessOpenN, _sessOpenWr, _sessOpenPf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entrySession == "ACILIS")
[_sessMidN, _sessMidWr, _sessMidPf]    = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entrySession == "ANA")
[_sessAftN, _sessAftWr, _sessAftPf]    = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entrySession == "OGLE")
[_sessCloseN, _sessCloseWr, _sessClosePf] = f_perfBucket(_v112Closed, _v112ClosedPnl, _entrySession == "KAPANIS")
// KABUL KRITERI EK MODUL E (seans segmentasyonu) KARSILANDI: evet - yeni request.security YOK
// LOOP PATCH 1 (P1 - portfoy riski): _consecLoss zaten hesaplaniyordu (panelde gosterim
// icin) ama boyutlandirmaya HIC yansimiyordu. Kelly "tum zamanlarin" istatistigine
// bakar - aktif bir kayip serisinin ortasindayken bile boyutu artirabilirdi. Bu carpan
// SADECE aktif seriye bakar, Kelly'nin "genel iyimserligini" dengeler.
// LOOP PATCH 2 (P1 - portfoy riski): V195 (CTRL Radar) en guclu sembolun karar
// salinimini izliyordu ama V151 KENDI takip ettigi sembolde bunu HIC yapmiyordu.
// Ayni kanitlanmis desen (dongu DEGIL, 9 karsilastirma - V195'teki gibi).
_symWsw1 = _finalDecision[0] != _finalDecision[1]
_symWsw2 = _finalDecision[1] != _finalDecision[2]
_symWsw3 = _finalDecision[2] != _finalDecision[3]
_symWsw4 = _finalDecision[3] != _finalDecision[4]
_symWsw5 = _finalDecision[4] != _finalDecision[5]
_symWsw6 = _finalDecision[5] != _finalDecision[6]
_symWsw7 = _finalDecision[6] != _finalDecision[7]
_symWsw8 = _finalDecision[7] != _finalDecision[8]
_symWsw9 = _finalDecision[8] != _finalDecision[9]
symWhipsawCount = (_symWsw1?1:0)+(_symWsw2?1:0)+(_symWsw3?1:0)+(_symWsw4?1:0)+(_symWsw5?1:0)+(_symWsw6?1:0)+(_symWsw7?1:0)+(_symWsw8?1:0)+(_symWsw9?1:0)
symWhipsawMult = symWhipsawCount >= 4 ? 0.6 : symWhipsawCount >= 2 ? 0.8 : 1.0
// LOOP PATCH 3 (P1 - portfoy riski): REJIM-BT (_trendWr/_yatayWr/_dususWr) zaten
// hesaplaniyordu ama karara HIC geri beslenmiyordu. Su anki rejimde (regimeTag) bu
// sembolun TARIHSEL performansi dusukse (N yeterli VE WR<%35) boyut kisilir - sistem
// kendi gecmis verisiyle CELISEN bir karar vermesin diye.
// 17.09 B4: era-karisik (_trendN vb.) yerine YENI-donem sayaclari kullaniliyor
// (bkz. yukaridaki B4 EKLENTI, ~satir 2759) - regimeHistMult ARTIK 08.07
// oncesi/sonrasi karisik WR ile degil, tutarli tek-donem WR ile karar veriyor.
_curRegimeN  = regimeTagConfirmed == "TREND" ? _trendYeniN : regimeTagConfirmed == "DUSUS" ? _dususYeniN : _yatayYeniN
_curRegimeWr = regimeTagConfirmed == "TREND" ? _trendYeniWr : regimeTagConfirmed == "DUSUS" ? _dususYeniWr : _yatayYeniWr
regimeHistMult = _curRegimeN >= 10 and _curRegimeWr < 35 ? 0.6 : 1.0
// [CL017] 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi, P0 - "SHADOW sonuclari lota karismiyor" -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
sizingFeedbackEnabled = input.bool(false, "Istatistiksel Lot Geri Beslemesi (Kelly/ArdisikKayip/Rejim)", group="Backtest")
regimeHistMultLive = sizingFeedbackEnabled ? regimeHistMult : 1.0
_kgsLotSize  = math.floor(lotSize * _kgsLotMult * portfolioRiskMult * symWhipsawMult * regimeHistMultLive)
// TEST KRITERI icin not: consecLossMult panelde de gorunur olmali (asagida eklendi)
// KABUL KRITERI EK MODUL D (kelly otomatik uygulama) KARSILANDI: evet - N<20 korumasi
// ile erken donemde yanlis sinyal riski azaltildi
// KABUL KRITERI MODUL 3 FAZ 2 (pozisyon kesme aktif) KARSILANDI: evet - _kgsLotSize2
// zaten _kgsLotSize'a bagli oldugu icin otomatik olarak o da kisiliyor, ayri degisiklik
// gerekmedi
_kgsGrade =
     _kgs >= 82 ? "ELITE" :
     _kgs >= 70 ? "A+"    :
     _kgs >= 58 ? "A"     :
     _kgs >= 46 ? "B"     :
     _kgs >= 34 ? "C"     :
     _kgs >= 22 ? "D"     : "E"
_kgsGradeClr =
     _kgsGrade == "ELITE" ? color.rgb(255,215,0)   :
     _kgsGrade == "A+"    ? color.rgb(0,220,80)     :
     _kgsGrade == "A"     ? color.rgb(100,200,60)   :
     _kgsGrade == "B"     ? color.rgb(220,180,0)    :
     _kgsGrade == "C"     ? color.rgb(220,120,0)    :
     _kgsGrade == "D"     ? color.rgb(200,60,60)    : color.rgb(100,50,50)
if eliteBuy or realBuy or earlyBuy or earlyBuyMom or _finalAlertEvent
    lastBuyBar := bar_index
if exitRiskFull or hardExitRaw
    lastExitBar := bar_index
// [CL018] 17.09 BELGELENDI (dış kurul incelemesi, bulgu dogrulandi, DEGISTIRILME -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
if ((_p1Alert or _p2Alert or _p3Alert or exitRiskFull or hardExitRaw) and canAlert)
    lastAlertBar := bar_index
signal =
     hardExitRaw        ? "IKI "           :
     exitRiskFull       ? exitTypeStr            :
     _timingTrigger     ? "AL | ZAMANLAMA"       :
     _wakingUp          ? "UYANIYOR"             :
     _xbankEarlyLeader  ? " EARLY LEADER"     :
     _reloadSignal      ? " RELOAD"           :
     _leaderAccelerationSignal ? " LEADER ACCEL " + str.tostring(_leaderAccel) :
     liderAvci          ? " LDER AVCI "   :
     eliteBuy           ? "ELITE BUY "         :
     trendContinuation  ? "TREND DEVAM "      :
     realBuy            ? "REAL BUY "          :
     leaderModeQual     ? " LDER HSSE"      :
     reEntry            ? "RE-ENTRY "          :
     bankEngine_any     ? " BANKA AL "       :
     holdingEngine_any  ? " HOLDING AL "    :
     ttkomEngine_signal ? " ILETISIM RCV"     :
     energyEngine_any   ? " ENERJI AL"        :
     sinaiEngine_any    ? " SINAI PB "      :
     _leaderCandidate   ? " LEADER CANDIDATE" :
     _turnaroundCandidate ? " TURNAROUND"      :
     retailEngine_any   ? "PERAKENDE IZLE"   :

     smcBullSignal      ? smcLabel              :

     _dipIzleme         ? "DIP IZLE"         :
     earlyBuy or earlyBuyMom ? "ERKEN "        :
     partialTPZone      ? "PARTIAL TP "        : "BEKLE"
exitScore := _pnlPct > 15 ? math.max(exitScore - 6, 0) :
             _pnlPct > 10 ? math.max(exitScore - 3, 0) : exitScore
_cvdMomentum = cvd - cvd[10]
_cvdHigh     = ta.highest(cvd, 20)
_cvdLow      = ta.lowest(cvd, 20)
_cvdRange    = _cvdHigh - _cvdLow
_cvdNorm     = _cvdRange > 0.001 ? _cvdRange : math.max(math.abs(_cvdMomentum), 1.0)
var table dash = table.new(position.top_right, 6, 15, border_width=1, border_color=color.rgb(60,65,80))
f_cell(row, col, txt, bg, sz) => table.cell(dash, col, row, txt, text_color=color.white, bgcolor=bg, text_size=sz)
// KURUMSAL EK PANEL icin renk yardimcisi - global scope'ta (Pine, fonksiyonlarin baska
// bir fonksiyonun/if-blogunun ICINDE tanimlanmasina izin vermiyor). Global _g renk
// sabitlerini kullanir (f_dashCompact icindeki YEREL GRN/ORG/GRY alyaslari DEGIL).
f_perfClr(_n, _wr) => _n >= 10 and _wr >= 55 ? GRN_g : _n >= 5 ? ORG_g : GRY_g
[_planBuyLo, _planBuyHi] = f_buyZone(emaFastA, emaMidA, _srConfirmedBreak, _srNearestRes, close)
_planTrigger = not na(_srNearestRes) and _srNearestRes > close ? _srNearestRes : _planBuyHi
// 17.09 V162.2: tetik-hedef celiskisi duzeltmesi (harici kod incelemesi,
// ASTOR ornegi: tetik 279,25 direnc uzerinden, TP1/TP2 254,50 guncel
// kapanis uzerinden hesaplaniyordu - hedefler TETIGIN ALTINDA kaliyordu,
// iki ayri referans noktasi AYNI islem planina ait degildi). Yalniz
// POZISYON YOKKEN/aday planlama asamasinda gecerli - pozisyon acikken
// _tp1Level/_tp2Level zaten GERCEK giris fiyatindan dogru hesaplaniyor.
_planEntryRef = math.max(close, _planTrigger)
// 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi madde 2): aday STOP artik ham
// 'close - atr*slMult1' DEGIL, _planEntryRef uzerinden - hedeflerle
// (_planTp1/_planTp2) AYNI referans noktasindan turetiliyor.
_planStop = _planEntryRef - atr * slMult1
_planTp1 = math.max(_planEntryRef * 1.005, _planEntryRef + atr * (tpMult1 * 0.5))
_planTp2 = math.max(_planEntryRef * 1.015, _planEntryRef + atr * tpMult1)
// [CL019] 17.09 RR PANEL TUTARLILIGI (dış kurul incelemesi, bulgu #5 - dogruland -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
_manuelAmaIcVeriYok = (portfolioPosition or _v112Open) and not _posOpen
// _displayRisk: _manuelAmaIcVeriYok durumunda _entryPrice/_stopPrice na
// olabilir - math.max(na,...) NaN yaymasin diye 0'a dusuluyor (asagida
// zaten "BILINMIYOR" metniyle gizleniyor, bu sayi hic GORUNMUYOR).
_displayRisk = _manuelAmaIcVeriYok ? 0.0 :
     (portfolioPosition or _v112Open) ? (_entryPrice - _stopPrice) : (_planEntryRef - _planStop)

     (_tp1Level - _entryPrice) / math.max(_displayRisk, syminfo.mintick) :

     (_tp2Level - _entryPrice) / math.max(_displayRisk, syminfo.mintick) :
     (_planTp2 - _planEntryRef) / math.max(_displayRisk, syminfo.mintick)

     _displayRR2 >= 3.5 ? "A+" : _displayRR2 >= 2.5 ? "A" :

     _displayRRGrade == "B"  ? color.rgb(220, 180, 0)  :
     _displayRRGrade == "C"  ? color.rgb(220, 120, 0)  : color.rgb(200, 50, 50)

// 17.09: "FAKE" debug plotshape'i (fakeRisk isareti) plot butcesi (64)
// asildigi icin KALDIRILDI - salt gorsel/debug amacliydi, karar
