// 17.09: PB debug plotshape'i (ve onu besleyen lastPBBar/pbCooldownOk)
// token payi icin kaldirildi - salt gorsel, pullbackState degiskeni
// hala normal kullaniliyor.
f_dashCompact() =>
    if showTable
        table.set_position(dash, _tablePosEff)
        HC = HC_g
        GRN = GRN_g
        RED = RED_g
        ORG = ORG_g
        GRY = GRY_g
        YLW = YLW_g
        CYN = CYN_g
        T = size.tiny
        S = size.small
        [_reloadQualityTxt, _reloadQualityClr, _ema9DistPct, _fomoTxt, _fomoClr, _alarmPriorityTxt, _alarmPriorityClr] =
             f_v118Meta(_reloadSignal, _kgs, close, emaMidA, relVol, _flowStrong, _flowPositive,
             _trendHealth, emaFastA, _lateEntry, _finalDecision, _entryScore, _leadScore, fakeRisk,
             panicSell, _windowCandidate, _leaderCandidate, _wakingUp, _smState,
             ta.valuewhen(_xbankEarlyLeader or _leaderCandidate or _reloadSignal, close, 0))
        _realPortfolio = portfolioPosition
        _kararTxt = _urgentExitRaw or _v113Lifecycle == "CIKIS" or _finalDecision == "CIK" ? "CIK" :
             _pozAzaltRaw and _realPortfolio ? "AZALT" :
             _realPortfolio ? "TUT" :
             _v113Lifecycle == "TUT" ? "IZLE" :
             _finalDecision == "AL" or _finalDecision == "TEYITLI AL" or _finalDecision == "ERKEN AL" ? "AL" :
             _finalDecision == "IZLE" or _finalDecision == "ADAY AL" ? "IZLE" : "PAS"
        _finalClr = _kararTxt == "AL" ? GRN : _kararTxt == "TUT" ? CYN :
             _kararTxt == "IZLE" ? ORG : _kararTxt == "CIK" ? RED : RED
        _entryClr = _entryScore >= 80 ? GRN : _entryScore >= 65 ? ORG : _entryScore >= 55 ? YLW : RED
        _dipRaw = _wakingUp or _turnaroundCandidate
        _trendPanelTxt = _recoveryBelowMain ? "RECOVERY" :
             autoSector == "HAVACILIK" and _trendHealth == "WEAK" and _sectorStructureOk ? "RECOVERY" :
             _trendHealth == "STRONG" and _leadScore >= 75 and _kgs >= 75 ? "ULTRA" : _trendHealth
        _trendPanelClr = _trendPanelTxt == "ULTRA" ? color.rgb(0,255,120) :
             _trendPanelTxt == "RECOVERY" ? CYN : _trendHealthClr
        _trendAgeTxt = trendAge <= 8 ? "TA ERKEN " + str.tostring(trendAge) :
             trendAge <= 20 ? "TA ORTA " + str.tostring(trendAge) :
             trendAge <= 35 ? "TA YASLI " + str.tostring(trendAge) : "TA COK YASLI " + str.tostring(trendAge)
        // 17.09 (ekranda yanılgı riski - dış kurul incelemesi madde 13'ün
        // "LEGACY_HTF etiketlensin" onerisiyle AYNI ilke): MTF skoru
        // htfAlign'a bagli, htfAlign HALA gerçek HTF EMA DEGIL (golge
        // olcum suruyor, n=200'de kanit yok) - "L" harfi bu belirsizligi
        // KISACA hatirlatiyor (tablo hucresi kucuk, uzun metin sigmiyor).
        _mtfTxt = "MTF" + str.tostring(mtfScore) + "L"
        _distGrade = distScore >= 70 ? "DANGER" : distScore >= 45 ? "UYARI" : "TEMIZ"
        _distPanelTxt = "DIST " + str.tostring(distScore) + " " + _distGrade
        _rsAccelTxt = "RSA" + (rsAccel >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(rsAccel)
        _modeBaseTxt = _positionManaged ? (_urgentExitRaw ? "CIKIS" :
             _pozAzaltRaw ? "POZ AZALT" :
             _exitWatchRaw ? "KAR KORU" :
             _reloadCandidateRaw and _entryScore >= 65 ? "EKLEME BEKLE" : "TASIMA") :
             _buyOpsGate and (_finalBuyRaw or _finalEarlyRaw) ? "YENI POZ" :
             _p3HacimRaw ? "HACIM BEKLE" :
             _finalDecision == "IZLE" or _finalDecision == "ADAY AL" ? "RADAR" : "ISLEM YOK"
        _alarmTxt = _positionManaged ? (_urgentExitRaw ? "ACIL CIK" :
             _pozAzaltRaw ? "POZ AZALT" :
             _exitWatchRaw ? "KAR KORU" :
             _reloadCandidateRaw and _entryScore >= 65 ? "EKLEME" : "TASIMA") :
             _urgentExitRaw ? "ACIL CIK" :
             _p1aOpAlert ? "P1 KALITE" :
             _p1bOpAlert ? "P1 AL" :
             _p0OpAlert ? "P2 ERKEN" :
             _dipDonusOpAlert ? "P2 DIP" :
             _reloadOpAlert ? "P2 DESTEK" :
             _p2aRaw and _finalDecision != "PAS GEC" ? "P2 ADAY" :
             _p3HacimRaw ? "P3 HACIM" :
             _p2bRaw or _p3Raw ? "P3 RADAR" : "PAS"
        _alarmClr = _alarmTxt == "P1 KALITE" or _alarmTxt == "P1 AL" or _alarmTxt == "P2 ERKEN" ? GRN :
             _alarmTxt == "P2 DIP" or _alarmTxt == "P2 DESTEK" or _alarmTxt == "P2 ADAY" or _alarmTxt == "EKLEME" ? ORG :
             _alarmTxt == "P3 RADAR" or _alarmTxt == "TASIMA" ? CYN :
             _alarmTxt == "KAR KORU" or _alarmTxt == "POZ AZALT" ? YLW : _alarmTxt == "ACIL CIK" ? RED : GRY
        _riskTxt = _urgentExitRaw or fakeRisk or panicSell or htfAlign < 1 or rrRatio < 1.2 ? "HIGH" :
             _lateEntry or trendAge > 20 or _volumeQualityEff < 40 or rrRatio < 1.5 or _exhaustScore >= 70 ? "MID" : "LOW"
        _riskClr = _riskTxt == "LOW" ? GRN : _riskTxt == "MID" ? ORG : RED
        _engelTxt = fakeRisk or panicSell or distribution ? "RISK" :
             _trendHealth == "WEAK" or _trendHealth == "CRITICAL" ? "TREND" :
             rsRank < 35 ? "RS" :
             htfAlign < 1 ? "HTF" :
             rrRatio < 1.5 ? "RR" :
             _volumeQualityEff < 40 ? "HACIM" :
             not _flowPositive ? "FLOW" :
             _lateEntry or trendAge > 20 ? "GEC" :
             _exhaustScore >= 70 ? "FATIGUE" : "YOK"
        _kgsShort = _kgs >= 70 ? "A+" : _kgs >= 58 ? "A" : _kgs >= 46 ? "B" : _kgs >= 34 ? "C" : _kgs >= 22 ? "D" : "E"
        _kgsMeaning = _kgs >= 70 ? "LIDER" : _kgs >= 58 ? "GUCLU" : _kgs >= 46 ? "ORTA" : _kgs >= 34 ? "RADAR" : _kgs >= 22 ? "ZAYIF" : "KACIN"
        _emaZoneLo = math.min(emaFastA, emaMidA)
        _emaZoneHi = math.max(emaFastA, emaMidA)
        _retestLo = _srConfirmedBreak and not na(_srNearestRes) ? _srNearestRes * 0.997 : na
        _retestHi = _srConfirmedBreak and not na(_srNearestRes) ? _srNearestRes * 1.006 : na
        _buyLoRaw = not na(_retestLo) ? math.max(_retestLo, _emaZoneLo) : _emaZoneLo
        _buyHiRaw = not na(_retestHi) ? math.min(math.max(_retestHi, _buyLoRaw), close) : _emaZoneHi
        _buyLo = math.min(_buyLoRaw, _buyHiRaw)
        _buyHi = math.max(_buyLoRaw, _buyHiRaw)
        _buyZoneTxt = str.tostring(_buyLo,"#.##") + "-" + str.tostring(_buyHi,"#.##")
        _triggerLevel = not na(_srNearestRes) and _srNearestRes > close ? _srNearestRes : _buyHi
        _triggerTxt = close >= _triggerLevel ? "GECTI " + str.tostring(_triggerLevel,"#.##") : str.tostring(_triggerLevel,"#.##") + " USTU"
        _invalidTxt = str.tostring(stop,"#.##") + " ALTI"
        _zoneStatus = close > _buyHi ? "KACIRDI" : close < _buyLo ? "BEKLE" : "ICINDE"
        _zoneClr = _zoneStatus == "ICINDE" ? GRN : _zoneStatus == "BEKLE" ? ORG : RED
        _eklemeTxt = _urgentExitRaw ? "YOK" :
             _pozAzaltRaw ? "AZALT" :
             _exitWatchRaw ? "KORU" :
             _zoneStatus == "KACIRDI" ? "GERI CEKIL" :
             _zoneStatus == "ICINDE" and _entryScore >= 65 and not _p3HacimRaw ? "UYGUN" : "BEKLE"
        _eklemeClr = _eklemeTxt == "UYGUN" ? GRN : _eklemeTxt == "KORU" or _eklemeTxt == "BEKLE" or _eklemeTxt == "GERI CEKIL" ? ORG : RED
        _entryRowName = _positionManaged ? "EKLEME" : "ENTRY"
        // 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi madde 17): entryScore 0 iken cikti
        // "ENTRY 0" gibi bir emir/deger varmis izlenimi veriyordu -
        // artik "YOK" gosteriliyor.
        _entryRowVal = _positionManaged ? _eklemeTxt : (_entryScore <= 0 ? "YOK" : str.tostring(_entryScore))
        _entryExplain = _kgs >= 70 and _kararTxt != "AL" and not _positionManaged ?
             "KGS OK AL YOK:" + _engelTxt : "L" + str.tostring(_leadScore) + " RS" + str.tostring(rsRank)
        _entryRowDetail = _positionManaged ? ("KALITE " + str.tostring(_entryScore)) : _entryExplain
        _entryRowClr = _positionManaged ? _eklemeClr : _entryClr
        _volNeed = 1.3
        _volTxt = "VOL " + str.tostring(relVol,"#.#") + "x / " + str.tostring(_volNeed,"#.#") + "x"
        _pozPct = _urgentExitRaw or _kararTxt == "PAS" ? 0 :
             _realPortfolio ? (_pozAzaltRaw ? 50 : _exitWatchRaw ? 75 : 100) :
             _buyOpsGate and _finalBuyRaw ? (_entryScore >= 80 and _riskTxt == "LOW" ? 75 : 50) :
             (_p0OpAlert or _dipDonusOpAlert or _reloadOpAlert) ? 25 : 0
        // 17.09: tablo tamamen dolu (15 satir), yeni satir acmadan mevcut
        // POZ hucresine positionSource/conflict bilgisi eklendi.
        _pozTxt = (str.tostring(_pozPct) + "%") +
             (positionConflict ? " !CONFLICT" : positionSource != "NONE" ? " " + positionSource : "")
        _pozClr = positionConflict ? RED : _pozPct >= 75 ? GRN : _pozPct >= 50 ? ORG : _pozPct > 0 ? YLW : GRY
        _pozDetail = _realPortfolio ? (_pozAzaltRaw ? "AZALT" : _exitWatchRaw ? "KORU" : _zoneStatus == "KACIRDI" ? "EKLEME YOK" : "TASIMA") :
             _pozPct > 0 ? "YENI POZ" : _engelTxt == "YOK" ? "BEKLE" : "PAS-" + _engelTxt
        _rankScore = f_clamp(math.round(_kgs * 0.28 + _entryScore * 0.28 +
             _leadScore * 0.22 + _volumeQualityEff * 0.10 + _v112Exp * 4.0 -
             (_riskTxt == "HIGH" ? 12 : _riskTxt == "MID" ? 5 : 0) -
             (_zoneStatus == "KACIRDI" and not _positionManaged ? 8 : 0)), 0, 100)
        _rankTxt = _rankScore >= 85 ? "TOP10" : _rankScore >= 75 ? "TOP20" :
             _rankScore >= 65 ? "TOP30" : _rankScore >= 50 ? "TOP50" : "WATCH"
        _rankClr = _rankScore >= 75 ? GRN : _rankScore >= 65 ? CYN : _rankScore >= 50 ? YLW : GRY
        _watchGrade = _rankScore >= 65 and _leadScore >= 60 ? "WATCH A" :
             _rankScore >= 50 ? "WATCH B" : "WATCH C"
        _modeTxt = _modeBaseTxt == "RADAR" ? _watchGrade : _modeBaseTxt
        _portScore = f_clamp(math.round(_rankScore + (_realPortfolio ? 8 : 0) +
             (_kararTxt == "TUT" ? 6 : _kararTxt == "AL" ? 8 : _kararTxt == "IZLE" ? 0 : -10) -
             (_zoneStatus == "KACIRDI" and not _positionManaged ? 7 : 0) -
             (_engelTxt == "RISK" or _engelTxt == "HTF" ? 10 : _engelTxt == "HACIM" ? 4 : 0)), 0, 100)
        _portTxt = "PORT " + str.tostring(_portScore) + " " +
             (_portScore >= 85 ? "K1" : _portScore >= 75 ? "K2" :
             _portScore >= 65 ? "K3" : _portScore >= 55 ? "K4" : "BEKLE")
        _portClr = _portScore >= 75 ? GRN : _portScore >= 65 ? CYN : _portScore >= 55 ? ORG : GRY
        _reloadScoreLite = f_clamp((_reloadSignal ? 25 : _reloadCandidateRaw ? 15 : 0) +
             (_kgs >= 82 ? 22 : _kgs >= 70 ? 17 : _kgs >= 60 ? 10 : 0) +
             (close > emaMidA ? 15 : 0) + (relVol > 1.5 ? 18 : relVol > 1.2 ? 10 : 0) +
             (_flowStrong ? 15 : _flowPositive ? 8 : 0) +
             (_trendPanelTxt == "ULTRA" ? 18 : _trendPanelTxt == "STRONG" ? 15 :
             _trendPanelTxt == "RECOVERY" ? 10 : _trendHealth == "NORMAL" ? 8 : 0), 0, 100)
        _reloadTxt = (_reloadScoreLite >= 80 ? "LC A " : _reloadScoreLite >= 65 ? "LC B " :
             _reloadScoreLite >= 50 ? "LC C " : "LC D ") + str.tostring(_reloadScoreLite)
        _reloadClr = _reloadScoreLite >= 80 ? GRN : _reloadScoreLite >= 65 ? ORG : _reloadScoreLite >= 50 ? YLW : GRY
        _trailScore = f_clamp(math.round(_holdScore * 0.40 + (100 - _exhaustScore) * 0.25 +
             _smartMoney * 0.20 + (not trendWeakening ? 15 : 0) -
             (distribution ? 20 : 0) - distScore * 0.15), 0, 100)
        _trailTxt = _trailScore >= 80 ? "HOLD STRONG" : _trailScore >= 60 ? "HOLD" :
             _trailScore >= 40 ? "HOLD WEAK" : "EXIT SOON"
        _trailClr = _trailScore >= 80 ? GRN : _trailScore >= 60 ? CYN : _trailScore >= 40 ? ORG : RED
        _exitScore = f_clamp(math.round(_exhaustScore * 0.35 + distScore * 0.30 +
             (trendWeakening ? 15 : 0) + (cvdFalling ? 10 : 0) +
             (_pozAzaltRaw ? 15 : 0) + (_urgentExitRaw ? 25 : 0)), 0, 100)
        // 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi madde 17): dusuk exitScore'da metin
        // "EXIT " ile basliyordu ama renk YESIL'di - "EXIT" kelimesi
        // alarm cagrisi yaparken yesil "sorun yok" mesaji veriyordu,
        // metin/renk celisiyordu. Dusuk skorda artik "SAGLAM" (EXIT
        // kelimesi olmadan) gosteriliyor.
        _exitTxt = _exitScore >= 70 ? "EXIT " + str.tostring(_exitScore) :
             _exitScore >= 45 ? "AZALT " + str.tostring(_exitScore) :
             "SAGLAM " + str.tostring(_exitScore)
        _exitClr = _exitScore >= 70 ? RED : _exitScore >= 45 ? YLW : GRN
        _kgsMapTxt = "MOM" + str.tostring(_kgsTrend) + " RS" + str.tostring(_kgsRS) + " VOL" + str.tostring(_kgsVol)
        _kgsMapTxt2 = "SEC" + str.tostring(_kgsSector) + " CVD" + str.tostring(_kgsCVD) + " FLOW" + str.tostring(_kgsKurum)
        _expectTxt = "EXP5 " + str.tostring(_v112Exp,"#.#") + "%"
        _expectTl = capital * _v112Exp / 100.0
        _expectTlTxt = (_expectTl >= 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(_expectTl,"#") + "TL"
        _alarmExpectTxt = _aqN >= 5 ? "A" + str.tostring(_aqN) + " W5 " + str.tostring(math.round(_aqWr5)) + "%" : "A DATA"
        _rankDetailTxt = "SEC" + str.tostring(_secLeadRank) + " L" + str.tostring(_leadScore) + " " + _rsAccelTxt
        f_cell(0, 0, "DSS", HC, S)
        _gunTxt = (_gunlukChg >= 0 ? " +" : " ") + str.tostring(_gunlukChg, "#.##") + "%"
        _dnaTxt = " [" + (stableDNA == "MOMENTUM" ? "MOM" : stableDNA == "KURUMSAL" ? "KRM" : stableDNA == "DEFENSIVE" ? "DEF" : "HVY") + "]"
        _gunClr = _gunlukChg >= 0.05 ? GRN_g : _gunlukChg <= -0.05 ? RED_g : BLU_g
        f_cell(0, 1, syminfo.ticker + "/" + f_secShort(autoSector) + _gunTxt + _dnaTxt, _gunClr, S)
        f_cell(0, 2, "DSS " + str.tostring(_entryScore) + " " + (_entryScore >= 70 ? "HI" : _entryScore >= 50 ? "MID" : "LOW"), _entryClr, T)
        f_cell(1, 0, "ACTION", HC, S)
        f_cell(1, 1, _kararTxt, _finalClr, S)
        f_cell(1, 2, _alarmTxt + (_lateEntry ? " (KOVALAMA)" : ""), _lateEntry ? RED : _alarmClr, T)
        f_cell(2, 0, "LIFE", HC, T)
        f_cell(2, 1, _v113Lifecycle, _alarmClr, S)
        f_cell(2, 2, _pozDetail, _pozClr, T)
        f_cell(3, 0, "TREND", HC, T)
        f_cell(3, 1, _trendPanelTxt, _trendPanelClr, T)
        f_cell(3, 2, _trendAgeTxt + " " + _mtfTxt, mtfScore >= 75 ? GRN : mtfScore >= 50 ? ORG : RED, T)
        f_cell(4, 0, "KGS", HC, T)
        f_cell(4, 1, _kgsShort + str.tostring(_kgs), _kgsGradeClr, S)
        f_cell(4, 2, _kgsMeaning + " " + _buyWindow, _buyWindow == "ACIK" ? GRN : _buyWindow == "DARALIYOR" ? ORG : RED, T)
        f_cell(5, 0, _entryRowName, HC, T)
        f_cell(5, 1, _entryRowVal, _entryRowClr, S)
        f_cell(5, 2, (_reloadSignal or _reloadCandidateRaw or _positionManaged) ? _reloadTxt : _entryRowDetail, (_reloadSignal or _reloadCandidateRaw or _positionManaged) ? _reloadClr : _entryRowClr, T)
        f_cell(6, 0, "RISK", HC, T)
        f_cell(6, 1, _riskTxt, _riskClr, S)
        f_cell(6, 2, _p3HacimRaw ? _volTxt : "RR " + str.tostring(rrRatio,"#.#") + " EXH" + str.tostring(_exhaustScore) + " D" + str.tostring(distScore), _p3HacimRaw ? YLW : distScore >= 70 ? RED : distScore >= 45 ? ORG : _riskClr, T)
        f_cell(7, 0, "AL BOLGE", HC, T)
        f_cell(7, 1, _buyZoneTxt, ORG, T)
        f_cell(7, 2, _zoneStatus, _zoneClr, T)
        f_cell(8, 0, "STOP", HC, T)
        f_cell(8, 1, _planPasif ? "AKTIF DEGIL" : _manuelAmaIcVeriYok ? "MODEL:" + str.tostring(f_bistRoundStop(_planStop),"#.##") : str.tostring(f_bistRoundStop((portfolioPosition or _v112Open) ? _stopPrice : _planStop),"#.##"), _planPasif ? GRI_g : _manuelAmaIcVeriYok ? ORG : RED, S)
        f_cell(8, 2, _planPasif ? "PLAN PASIF" : _manuelAmaIcVeriYok ? "MALIYET YOK" : "R1 " + str.tostring(_displayRR1,"#.#") + " R2 " + str.tostring(_displayRR2,"#.#"), _planPasif ? GRI_g : _manuelAmaIcVeriYok ? GRI_g : _displayRRGradeClr, T)
        f_cell(9, 0, "TP1/TP2", HC, T)
        f_cell(9, 1, _planPasif ? "AKTIF DEGIL" : (str.tostring(f_bistRound(_manuelAmaIcVeriYok ? _planTp1 : (portfolioPosition or _v112Open) ? _tp1Level : _planTp1),"#.##") + " / " + str.tostring(f_bistRound(_manuelAmaIcVeriYok ? _planTp2 : (portfolioPosition or _v112Open) ? _tp2Level : _planTp2),"#.##") + (_manuelAmaIcVeriYok ? " (MODEL)" : "")), _planPasif ? GRI_g : _manuelAmaIcVeriYok ? YLW : GRN, T)
        f_cell(9, 2, _exitTxt + " " + _trailTxt, _exitClr, T)
        f_cell(10, 0, "BLOCK", HC, T)
        f_cell(10, 1, _engelTxt == "YOK" ? "NONE" : _engelTxt, _engelTxt == "YOK" ? GRN : _engelTxt == "HACIM" or _engelTxt == "GEC" or _engelTxt == "FATIGUE" ? ORG : RED, S)
        f_cell(10, 2, _blockers == "" ? (_kararTxt == "PAS" ? _pozDetail : "OK") : _blockers, _kararTxt == "PAS" ? RED : _entryClr, T)
        f_cell(11, 0, _planPasif ? "ADAY TETIK" : "TRIG", HC, T)
        f_cell(11, 1, _triggerTxt, _planPasif ? GRI_g : ORG, T)
        f_cell(11, 2, _p3HacimRaw ? "HACIM BEKLE" : "KAPANIS", _p3HacimRaw ? YLW : GRY, T)
        f_cell(12, 0, _planPasif ? "ADAY GECERSIZLIK" : "INVALID", HC, T)
        f_cell(12, 1, _invalidTxt, _planPasif ? GRI_g : RED, T)

        _btClr = _v112Total >= 30 and _v112Pf >= 1.5 and _v112Exp > 0 ? GRN :
             _v112Total >= 10 and _v112Exp >= 0 ? ORG : GRY
        f_cell(13, 0, "POZ", HC, T)
        f_cell(13, 1, _pozTxt, _pozClr, S)
        f_cell(13, 2, _portTxt, _portClr, T)
        f_cell(0, 3, "SH-BT", HC, T)
        // 17.09 (son inceleme, P1 - "WR/PF yaziyor ama gercek islem degil,
        // golge/karsi-olgusal sonuc - yaniltici" bulgusu): etiketler SHWR/SHPF
        // oldu, "SH" (shadow) gercek emir/portfoy performansi ile
        // KARISTIRILMAMASI icin.
        f_cell(0, 4, "N" + str.tostring(_v112Total) + " SHWR" + str.tostring(math.round(_v112Wr)) + "%", _btClr, T)

        // durumunda sifira bolmeyi onlemek icin kullanilan ic sentinel
        // deger) DOGRUDAN metin olarak gosterilince kucuk orneklemde
        // asiri guclu bir izlenim yaratiyordu - artik N/A gosteriliyor.
        f_cell(0, 5, "SHPF" + (_v112Pf >= 999 ? " N/A" : str.tostring(_v112Pf,"#.#")) + " DD" + str.tostring(_v112MaxDd,"#.#"), _btClr, T)
        _testClr = _aqN >= 10 and _aqWr5 >= 55 and _aqFalse <= 25 ? GRN : _aqN >= 5 ? ORG : GRY
        f_cell(1, 3, "EXPECT", HC, T)
        f_cell(1, 4, _expectTxt, _btClr, T)
        f_cell(1, 5, _expectTlTxt + " " + _alarmExpectTxt, _testClr, T)
        f_cell(2, 3, "NOTE", HC, T)
        f_cell(2, 4, _engelTxt == "YOK" ? "OK" : "WAIT " + _engelTxt, _engelTxt == "YOK" ? GRN : ORG, T)
        f_cell(2, 5, waitReasonTxt, waitReasonTxt == "ISLEM ADAYI" ? GRN : ORG, T)
        _regN = _regimeRadar == "BROAD ON" ? _regOnN : _regOffN
        _regWr = _regimeRadar == "BROAD ON" ? _regOnWr : _regOffWr
        _regPf = _regimeRadar == "BROAD ON" ? _regOnPf : _regOffPf
        _regClr = _regN >= 10 and _regPf >= 1.3 ? GRN : _regN >= 5 ? ORG : GRY
        _secClr = _secN >= 10 and _secPf >= 1.3 ? GRN : _secN >= 5 ? ORG : GRY
        f_cell(3, 3, "RANK", HC, T)
        f_cell(3, 4, _rankTxt, _rankClr, S)
        f_cell(3, 5, _rankDetailTxt, _secClr, T)
        // === KURUMSAL EK PANEL (satir 18-24, daha once BOS - yeni satir acmadan
        // mevcut tablo boyutu 28'e sigiyor) ===
        f_cell(4, 3, "REJIM-BT", HC, T)

        f_cell(4, 5, "YT N" + str.tostring(_yatayN) + " " + str.tostring(math.round(_yatayWr)) + "%", f_perfClr(_yatayN, _yatayWr), T)
        f_cell(5, 3, "", HC, T)
        f_cell(5, 4, "DS N" + str.tostring(_dususN) + " " + str.tostring(math.round(_dususWr)) + "%", f_perfClr(_dususN, _dususWr), T)
        f_cell(5, 5, regimeHistMult < 1.0 ? "REJIM ZAYIF x" + str.tostring(regimeHistMult, "#.#") : "", regimeHistMult < 1.0 ? RED : GRY, T)
        f_cell(6, 3, "FAKTOR-BT", HC, T)
        f_cell(6, 4, "TREND N" + str.tostring(_fTrendN) + " " + str.tostring(math.round(_fTrendWr)) + "%", f_perfClr(_fTrendN, _fTrendWr), T)
        f_cell(6, 5, "RS N" + str.tostring(_fRsN) + " " + str.tostring(math.round(_fRsWr)) + "%", f_perfClr(_fRsN, _fRsWr), T)
        f_cell(7, 3, "", HC, T)
        f_cell(7, 4, "SEC N" + str.tostring(_fSectorN) + " " + str.tostring(math.round(_fSectorWr)) + "%", f_perfClr(_fSectorN, _fSectorWr), T)
        f_cell(7, 5, "FLOW N" + str.tostring(_fFlowN) + " " + str.tostring(math.round(_fFlowWr)) + "%", f_perfClr(_fFlowN, _fFlowWr), T)
        f_cell(8, 3, "", HC, T)
        f_cell(8, 4, "OBV N" + str.tostring(_fObvN) + " " + str.tostring(math.round(_fObvWr)) + "%", f_perfClr(_fObvN, _fObvWr), T)
        f_cell(8, 5, "INST N" + str.tostring(_fInstN) + " " + str.tostring(math.round(_fInstWr)) + "%", f_perfClr(_fInstN, _fInstWr), T)
        f_cell(9, 3, "OOS", HC, T)
        f_cell(9, 4, "N" + str.tostring(_oosN) + " WR" + str.tostring(math.round(_oosWr)) + "%", f_perfClr(_oosN, _oosWr), T)
        f_cell(9, 5, "PF" + (_oosPf >= 999 ? " N/A" : str.tostring(_oosPf, "#.#")), f_perfClr(_oosN, _oosWr), T)
        f_cell(10, 3, "PORTFOY", HC, T)
        f_cell(10, 4, not sectorBridgeConnected ? "BAGLI DEGIL" : portfolioRiskWarn ? "SEKTOR YOGUN" : "OK", not sectorBridgeConnected ? GRY : portfolioRiskWarn ? ORG : GRN, T)
        f_cell(10, 5, not dirAlignBridgeConnected ? "BAGLI DEGIL" : dirAlignRiskWarn ? "YON TEK" : "OK", not dirAlignBridgeConnected ? GRY : dirAlignRiskWarn ? ORG : GRN, T)
        f_cell(11, 3, "BOYUT KESIM", HC, T)

        // cunku onceden 28 satirin sadece 18'i kullaniliyordu) KARSILANDI: evet
f_dash1() => 0
f_dash2b() => 0
if barstate.islast
    if _compactPanel
        f_dashCompact()
    else
        f_dash1()
        f_dash2b()
// PATCH 4 (kullanici onerisi, DUZELTILMIS): CORE modu, mevcut 14 alarmi SILMEZ (FULL
// kullanicilar icin hepsi duruyor), sadece 3 sade alarm EKLER. Orijinal onerideki
// _hasPortfolioPosition/_exitRisk bu kod tabaninda yok - gercek karsiliklari
// portfolioPosition / (exitRiskFull or hardExitRaw) kullanildi.
grpAlertMode = "Alarm Sadelestirme"
alertMode = input.string("FULL", "Alarm Modu (CORE: +3 sade alarm ekler; eski 14 alarm HER modda tanimli/aktif kalir)", options=["CORE", "FULL"], group=grpAlertMode)
// 17.09 ALARM KAPANIS TEYIDI: CORE alarmlari canAlert'e BAGLI DEGIL (ayri
// mekanizma), o yuzden yukaridaki canAlert duzeltmesi bunlari kapsamiyor -
// barstate.isconfirmed AYRICA eklendi.
coreBuyAlert = alertMode == "CORE" and (_finalDecision == "AL" or _finalDecision == "TEYITLI AL") and _finalDecision[1] != _finalDecision and barstate.isconfirmed
coreWatchAlert = alertMode == "CORE" and _finalDecision == "IZLE" and _entryScore >= 68 and _finalDecision[1] != "IZLE" and barstate.isconfirmed
_exitSignalNow = exitRiskFull or hardExitRaw
coreExitAlert = alertMode == "CORE" and portfolioPosition and _exitSignalNow and not _exitSignalNow[1] and barstate.isconfirmed
// === G1 ALARM ZENGINLESTIRME (10.07.2026) ==================================
// Sinyal ani metriklerini webhook'a tasiyan gizli plotlar. SINYAL MANTIGI
// DEGISMEZ - yalniz mevcut global degerler OKUNUR. Rejim kodlamasi:
// 2 = ON ailesi (RISK-ON / ROT*), 1 = notr/karisik, 0 = RISK-OFF
_g1RejimKod = str.contains(marketRegimeTxt, "RISK-OFF") ? 0 : (str.contains(marketRegimeTxt, "ROT") or str.contains(marketRegimeTxt, "RISK-ON")) ? 2 : 1
// 03.09 DUZELTME: 2 ondalik basamaga yuvarlama - SADECE webhook/goruntu
// icin (bu _w* degiskenleri baska hicbir yerde OKUNMUYOR, dogrulandi -
// karar mantigina etkisi YOK, salt bicimlendirme).
_wSkor   := math.round(scoreSmoothedFinal * 100) / 100
_wKgs    := math.round(_kgs * 100) / 100
// [CL021] 17.09 V162.2: math.round(*100)/100 -> f_bistRound. Eskiden 254,37 gibi -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
_wStop   := _stopHitEvent ? f_bistRoundStop(_lastStopFill) : f_bistRoundStop(_posOpen ? _stopPrice : _planStop)
_wRejim  := _g1RejimKod
_wRelVol := math.round(relVol * 100) / 100
// [CL022] 03.09: zaman damgasi ({{time}}) ve 2 ondalik yuvarlama kaldi. -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
grp_p3 = "P3_SKOR (skor-tabanli giris - IP-4 EK-1)"
p3Enabled      = input.bool(true, "P3_SKOR Aktif (ana salter)", group=grp_p3)
pSkorTaban     = 32   // [V163-4] sabit (eskiden input - grafik basina farkli olabiliyordu)
// 03.09: _wP3Ozet ataması BURAYA - pSkorTaban tanımından ÖNCE kullanılınca
// "Undeclared identifier" hatası verir (daha önce _wSkorTb'de yaşandı).
_wP3Ozet       := htfAlign * 1000 + (stableDNA == "DEFENSIVE" ? 100 : 0) + pSkorTaban
// [CL023] 03.09 (2. tur) GECICI DENEME PLOTU: bar-gecikme sorununun kaynagi olan -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
// [CL030] SkorGecTest kaldirildi (amacina ulasti, 03.09da KESINLESTI) -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
pSkorMomBar    = input.int(5, "Skor Yukselis Penceresi (bar)", minval=3, maxval=10, group=grp_p3)
pTepePencere   = input.int(10, "Tepe Asim Penceresi (bar)", minval=5, maxval=20, group=grp_p3)
pTetikHacim    = input.float(1.2, "Tetik RelVol Esigi", minval=1.1, maxval=1.5, step=0.05, group=grp_p3)
pKovalamaPct   = input.float(1.5, "Kovalama Bandi (%)", minval=1.0, maxval=2.0, step=0.1, group=grp_p3)
pSogumaBar     = input.int(20, "ACIL_CIK Sonrasi Soguma (bar)", minval=10, maxval=40, group=grp_p3)
pAzamiStopPct  = input.float(4.0, "Azami Stop Mesafesi (%)", minval=2.0, maxval=5.0, step=0.5, group=grp_p3)
pAzamiHtf      = input.int(1, "Asgari HTF Hizasi (0/1/2)", minval=0, maxval=2, group=grp_p3)

// ---- BAGLAM: trend canli mi, skor tabani + yukselis + rejim + HTF hizasi uygun mu
// v3 (23.07) DUZELTME: GUNLUK_OZET'in tasidigi gercek degiskenler bulundu
// (_wSkor := scoreSmoothedFinal, _wRejim := _g1RejimKod - G1 ALARM
// ZENGINLESTIRME blogu, ~satir 3294). Katman A kalibrasyonu bu ikisini
// olcuyordu; onceki v1/v2 yanlislikla scoreSmoothed/regimeTag kullaniyordu
// (KCHOL/TUPRS D2' testinde tarih takasi buradan cikti).
// [08.08: _wV112n atamasi buradan _v112Total hesaplandigi yere tasindi]
_p3SkorTaban    = scoreSmoothedFinal >= pSkorTaban
_p3SkorYukselis = scoreSmoothedFinal > scoreSmoothedFinal[pSkorMomBar]
_p3RejimUygun   = _g1RejimKod >= 1       // GUNLUK_OZET/Katman A ile AYNI rejim kodu (0/1/2)
_p3HtfUygun     = htfAlign >= pAzamiHtf  // 23.07 EREGL vakasi: taramada guclu (V195) ama
                                          // htfAlign<1 oldugu icin V150'de PAS/HTF blogu -
                                          // P3_SKOR ayni tuzaga dusmesin diye eklendi
_p3DnaUygun     = stableDNA != "DEFENSIVE"  // A1 karari (23.07): DEFENSIVE (banka/savunma)
                                             // ortalamaya-donus karakterli, momentum-kirilim
                                             // degil - v1'de haric tutuluyor (PPK gunu +
                                             // canli AKBNK/YKBNK pozisyonlari riski)
_p3Baglam       = _p3SkorTaban and _p3SkorYukselis and _p3RejimUygun and _p3HtfUygun and _p3DnaUygun

// ---- TETIK: tepe asim kapanisi + hacim genislemesi + bar onaylandi
_p3TetikSeviye = ta.highest(high[1], pTepePencere)
_p3TepeAsim    = close > _p3TetikSeviye
_p3HacimVar    = relVol >= pTetikHacim
_p3Tetik       = _p3Baglam and _p3TepeAsim and _p3HacimVar and barstate.isconfirmed

// [CL024] ---- BEKCILER: kovalama / ACIL_CIK sonrasi soguma / asiri stop mesafes -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
_p3KovalamaUzakKat    = 4.0  // Faz V0 kaniti: 4x bandin (%6.0) otesi en zayif kademe
_p3KovalamaTamVeto    = close > _p3TetikSeviye * (1 + (pKovalamaPct * _p3KovalamaUzakKat) / 100)
_p3KovalamaYariBoyut  = close > _p3TetikSeviye * (1 + pKovalamaPct / 100) and not _p3KovalamaTamVeto
// 17.09 B3: P3Ozet'e 10000'lik basamakta yariBoyut biti EKLENDI (yeni plot
// DEGIL - "ilk 20 plot" kisiti zaten webhook'a tasinamayacagini kesinlestirmisti,
// bkz. SkorGecTest 03.09 bulgusu). BILEREK ayni degiskene IKINCI atama - P3Ozet
// zaten bir-bar-gecikmeli calisiyor (bilinen, kuluckada cozulmeyen kisit), yeni
// alan da AYNI gecikmeyi tasir - tutarli, YENI bir tutarsizlik EKLEMEZ.
// Decode: yariBoyut = deger // 10000 (0/1), htf = (deger % 10000) // 1000, ...
// [CL029] entryScore P3Ozet'e eklendi (100000'lik basamak) -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
_wP3Ozet := _wP3Ozet + (_p3KovalamaYariBoyut ? 10000 : 0) + math.round(_entryScore) * 100000
// 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi, dogrulandi): _urgentExitAlert canAlert
// cooldown'una tabi - cooldown suresince gercek bir acil cikis olsa bile
// alert BASTIRILABILIR, bu durumda soguma HIC BASLAMAZDI. _urgentExitRaw
// (cooldown'suz ham olay) kullanildi.
_p3BarsSinceUrgent = ta.barssince(_urgentExitRaw)
_p3Soguma         = not na(_p3BarsSinceUrgent) and _p3BarsSinceUrgent < pSogumaBar
_p3StopAday       = ta.lowest(low, pTepePencere)
_p3StopMesafePct  = (close - _p3StopAday) / close * 100
_p3StopAsiri      = _p3StopMesafePct > pAzamiStopPct

// 17.09 DUZELTME (kurul onayli, istisna karari): P3_SKOR_AL elde pozisyon
// varken de yeniden AL sinyali uretebiliyordu - _newEntryMode kontrolu yoktu.
// _newEntryMode (satir 2788: not _positionManaged) eklendi. Not: ekleme/
// piramitleme ayri, kasitli bir karar olmali - bu alarm artik sadece YENI
// giris icin. Harici kod incelemesiyle bulundu.
// 17.09 B3: "not _p3Kovalama" -> "not _p3KovalamaTamVeto" (bkz. yukarida)
_p3AlRaw = _newEntryMode and _p3Tetik and not _p3KovalamaTamVeto and not _p3Soguma and not _p3StopAsiri

// ---- GUNLUK TAVAN: ayni gun icinde tek sinyal (15dk grafikte gun ici tekillik)
var int _p3SonTetikGun = na
// 17.09 DUZELTME (kurul onayli, istisna karari): sadece dayofmonth
// saklaniyordu - bir onceki ayin ayni gun numarasinda (orn. 16 Agustos'tan
// sonra 16 Eylul) yanlislikla "bugun zaten tetiklendi" sanip P3 sinyalini
// engelleyebilirdi. year*10000+month*100+day tekil gun kimligine gecildi.
// Harici kod incelemesiyle bulundu.
_p3GunKimlik = year * 10000 + month * 100 + dayofmonth
_p3GunlukIlk = na(_p3SonTetikGun) or _p3SonTetikGun != _p3GunKimlik
// 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi, dogrulandi): p3Enabled=false iken bile
// _p3GunlukIlk (gunluk tekillik) TUKETILIYORDU - modul kapaliyken bile
// "bugun sinyal uretildi" sayiliyor, modul tekrar acildiginda o gun
// tekrar tetiklenemiyordu. p3Enabled artik ana kosula dahil.
_p3Al = p3Enabled and _p3AlRaw and _p3GunlukIlk
// 17.09 NOT: "YARIM BOYUT" gorsel etiketi (plotshape) DENENDI ama script
// 64 plot sinirini asti (RE10140, "creates too many plots (65)") -
// GERI ALINDI. B3 yarim-boyut bilgisi hala P3Ozet biti olarak webhook'a
// gidiyor (karar mantigina etkisi yok), sadece grafikte GORSEL isaret
// YOK. Panelde/tabloda goruntulemek (plot bütçesi harcamayan bir yol)
// ayri bir gorev olarak ele alinabilir.
if _p3Al
    _p3SonTetikGun := _p3GunKimlik

// [CL025] ---- GORSEL (opsiyonel - grafikte P3 girisini isaretler) -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
if p3Enabled and _p3Al
    label.new(bar_index, low, "P3 " + str.tostring(math.round(scoreSmoothedFinal)) +
         " " + str.tostring(year) + "-" + str.tostring(month) + "-" + str.tostring(dayofmonth) +
         " " + str.tostring(hour) + ":" + str.tostring(minute),
         style=label.style_label_up, color=color.rgb(0, 180, 255),
         textcolor=color.white, size=size.tiny)

// [CL026] ============================================================ -> bkz. docs/CHANGELOG_V162.md
grp_gstop = "GERCEK STOP ALARMI (portfoy.json ile senkron)"
gercekStopEnabled = input.bool(false, "Gercek Stop Alarmi Aktif (pozisyon varsa ac)", group=grp_gstop)
gercekStopSeviye  = input.float(0.0, "Gercek Stop Seviyesi (portfoy.json stop_seviye)", group=grp_gstop, step=0.01)

// Kenar-tetik: yalniz KIRILMA ANINDA bir kez ateşler, her barda tekrarlamaz
_gStopKirildi = gercekStopEnabled and gercekStopSeviye > 0 and
     close < gercekStopSeviye and close[1] >= gercekStopSeviye

// 17.09 NOT: eventId/schemaVersion alanlari artik TAMAMEN LITERAL
// {{...}} placeholder metni ile insa ediliyor (asagidaki her
// alertcondition() kendi satirinda) - fonksiyona SARILMADI, cunku
// alertcondition()'in message parametresi 'const string' istiyor,
// bir kullanici fonksiyonunun donus degeri 'series string' sayiliyor
// ve REDDEDILIYOR (once denendi, derleme hatasi verdi, geri alindi).
alertcondition(_gStopKirildi, title="GERCEK STOP KIRILDI",
     message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|STOP_KIRILDI|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "STOP_KIRILDI", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "v112n": "{{plot("V112N")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}"}')

// 17.09 (dış kurul incelemesi madde 5): IC pozisyon motorunun kendi stop
// olayi - GERCEK STOP KIRILDI'dan (manuel gercekStopSeviye girdisi) ve
// CORE_CIKIS'ten (exitRiskFull/hardExitRaw) BAGIMSIZ, ayri bir gorunurluk.
alertcondition(_stopHitEvent, title="IC STOP TETIKLENDI",
     message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|IC_STOP|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "IC_STOP", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "v112n": "{{plot("V112N")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}"}')

// ---- ALARM (P1/P2/ACIL_CIK ile AYNI basit sablon - sembol/sinyal/interval/fiyat)
alertcondition(p3Enabled and _p3Al, title="P3 SKOR AL",
     message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|P3_SKOR_AL|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "P3_SKOR_AL", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "v112n": "{{plot("V112N")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}"}')

// GUNLUK_OZET (13.07.2026): seans kapanis fotografi - M4 (rejim) ve gunluk
// skor/KGS kaydi icin. Kosul yalnizca takvimseldir, sinyal mantigi DEGISMEZ.
// Engel/aksiyon alanlari B7 (birikim) - tanimlari render blogunda oldugundan
// kulucka icinde tasinmadi.
alertcondition(session.islastbar_regular and barstate.isconfirmed, title="GUNLUK OZET", message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|GUNLUK_OZET|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "GUNLUK_OZET", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "v112n": "{{plot("V112N")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}"}')
alertcondition(coreBuyAlert, title="CORE: AL ADAYI", message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|CORE_AL|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "CORE_AL", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}"}')

alertcondition(_p2aAlert and _newEntryMode and _finalDecision != "PAS GEC", title="P2 ADAY", message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|P2_ADAY|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "P2_ADAY", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}"}')
alertcondition(_p3HacimAlert, title="P3 HACIM BEKLE", message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|P3_HACIM_BEKLE|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "P3_HACIM_BEKLE", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}"}')
alertcondition(_p2bAlert and _newEntryMode, title="P3 RADAR", message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|P3_RADAR|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "P3_RADAR", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}"}')
alertcondition(_p3Alert and _newEntryMode, title="P4 HAZIRLIK", message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|P4_HAZIRLIK|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "P4_HAZIRLIK", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}"}')
alertcondition(_carryAlert, title="TASIMA", message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|TASIMA|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "TASIMA", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}"}')

alertcondition(_pozAzaltAlertDebounced, title="POZ AZALT", message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|POZ_AZALT|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "POZ_AZALT", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}", "pozsebep": "{{plot("PozSebep")}}"}')
alertcondition(_urgentExitAlert, title="ACIL CIK", message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|ACIL_CIK|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "ACIL_CIK", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}"}')
alertcondition(_rotationAlert, title="SEKTOR ROTASYONU", message='{"schemaVersion": "v163.0", "carrierLagBars": 1, "eventId": "{{ticker}}|{{interval}}|SEKTOR_ROTASYONU|{{time}}", "symbol": "{{ticker}}", "signal": "SEKTOR_ROTASYONU", "interval": "{{interval}}", "price": "{{close}}", "skor": "{{plot("Skor")}}", "kgs": "{{plot("KGS")}}", "stop": "{{plot("Stop")}}", "rejim": "{{plot("Rejim")}}", "relvol": "{{plot("RelVol")}}", "zaman": "{{time}}", "p3ozet": "{{plot("P3Ozet")}}"}')
